## 13. Implementation Phases

| Phase | Tasks | Est. Days |
|-------|-------|-----------|
| **P1: Constraint Emission** | Add `#show constraint/5` to helpers.lp; emit constraints in 18 priority rule files | 2 |
| **P2: Checker Extension** | `include_constraints` path; parse constraint atoms; return ConstraintDict | 1 |
| **P3: QP Optimizer (MVP)** | Projected gradient on box+speed constraints; `ConstraintOptimizer` class | 2 |
| **P4: Corrector Integration** | `CorrectorQP` class; re-validation loop; fallback to heuristic/brake | 2 |
| **P5: Tests** | 5 new test files; ensure all 93 existing pass | 2 |
| **P6: Ablation & Validation** | Run on `trajs.json` dump (GPU-free); full replay baseline | 1 |
| **P7: Docs & Cleanup** | Delete `correction_strategies.py`; update docs | 1 |

**Total**: ~11 working days

---

## 14. Risks & Mitigations

| Risk | Likelihood | Mitigation |
|------|------------|------------|
| QP infeasibility on complex scenes | Medium | Soft slacks with priority weights; warm-start from previous iterate; fallback to heuristic |
| Constraint emission incomplete | Medium | `test_constraint_emission.py` tripwire; `test_rule_registry` already asserts 73/73 findings |
| Latency regression (QP + re-validate > fixpoint) | Low | Cache constraints when violation set unchanged; `#show` keeps atom scan fast |
| cvxopt/scipy dependency issues | Low | MVP uses pure NumPy projected gradient; QP as optional Phase 2 |
| CARLA never provisions | High | Explicitly exclude from paper; document as future work |

---

## 15. Acceptance Criteria (Definition of Done)

1. **All 93 existing tests pass** with `mode='heuristic'`
2. **New tests pass**: constraint emission (100%), QP feasibility, re-validation
3. **Replay baseline**: B2 (QP) pred_fixed_rate ≥ 0.5, collision rate ≤ 0.05, corrector_ms ≤ 3000
4. **Ablation**: Full constraints (A1) ≥ partial (A2/A3) on fix rate
5. **No regression**: Heuristic mode unchanged; config flag switches cleanly
6. **Documentation**: API reference, conventions, usage updated

---

## 16. Clarifying Questions

Before implementation begins, please confirm:

1. **Optimizer choice**: MVP with pure-NumPy projected gradient (no new deps), or Phase 2 with `cvxopt`/`scipy.optimize` from start?
2. **Constraint granularity**: Emit constraints per-timestep (T=0..3) or only at violation timesteps?
3. **Slack variables**: Include from MVP or add in Phase 2?
4. **Fallback strategy**: On QP failure → heuristic corrector, or → brake hold, or → multi-shot re-solve?
5. **Config management**: New `configs/correction_qp.yaml` or extend existing `correction_strategies.yaml`?

---

*End of implementation plan.*
==> asp-safety/docs/api_reference.md <==
# API Reference

Conventions: `docs/conventions.md`. All coordinates meters in code,
integer centimeters (x100) in ASP. Box layout `[x,y,z,w,l,h,yaw]`.

---

## SparseDrivePlanner (`src/neural_planner.py`)

### __init__(model_path: str, device: str = "cuda")
Missing/unloadable weights fall back to the placeholder planner
(`use_placeholder=True`); they do NOT raise.

### predict(images, intrinsics, extrinsics, ego2global, timestamp, nav_cmd=None, ego_status=None) -> Dict
- `images` (6,3,256,704); `intrinsics` (6,3,3); `extrinsics` (6,4,4)
  lidar-to-camera; `ego2global` (4,4); `timestamp` float.
- `nav_cmd`: optional one-hot (3,) — command-head index convention
  is UNVERIFIED (see `experiments/cmd_sweep_results.md`); default
  straight `[1,0,0]`.
- `ego_status`: optional (18,) upstream-layout state
  `[ax,ay,az,wx,wy,wz,vx,vy,vz,steer,0*8]`; ignored by cold
  single-frame inference (temporal queue only).
- Returns `trajectory` (6,2) t=0.5..3.0s ego-frame, `boxes` (N,7),
  `scores` (N,), `velocities` (N,3) planar `[vx,vy,vz]`.
- Cold-resets temporal banks every call (consecutive warm calls
  would crash on missing `img_metas`). Raises `ValueError` on bad
  image shape; fail-loud `KeyError`/`ValueError` on bad model output.

### begin_sequence() / predict_frame(...) / end_sequence()
N-2 warm-queue API: `predict_frame` takes the same args as `predict`
but preserves temporal banks and builds `img_metas` (per-frame
`T_global`) internally. Caller supplies per-frame `ego_status`
(see `data_loader.estimate_ego_status`). Always bracket with
begin/end per scene; never carry state across scenes.

### reset_temporal()
Clears instance banks/queues (cold state).

---

## TrajectoryChecker (`src/checker.py`)

### __init__(rules_dir: str = "rules", max_agent_range_m: float = 150.0)
Pre-concatenates the catalog blob once. Raises `FileNotFoundError`
/ `RuntimeError` for bad/empty rules dir.

### check(trajectory, other_vehicles=None, map_data=None, traffic_lights=None, zones=None, pedestrians=None, weather=None, ego_intent=None, extras=None, include_internal=False, include_constraints=False) -> Tuple[bool, List[Dict]]
- `trajectory` (T,2), T <= 6, meters, ego-frame at `STEP_DT=1.0 s`
  (resample model output first; see N-3a).
- Extended inputs: `zones` (school/construction, explicit
  `active_times` honored), `pedestrians` (`positions` per T),
  `weather` (condition/confidence/severity + caps), `ego_intent`
  (turn/maneuver/lead/platoon/blind-spots/occupancy + durations),
  `extras` (`{'facts': [...]}` raw ASP).
- Returns `(is_safe, violations)`. Shapes: violation/3
  `{rule,vehicle,time}`; violation/4 adds `vehicle2`; hazard/warning/
  insufficient carry `type`. Warnings never count toward unsafe.
- `include_internal=True` appends solver-internal atoms (never
  unsafe): `ttc` (`band`), `threat` (`level`), `comfort` (`score`),
  `future` (`time`), `any`.
- `include_constraints=True` (S13/P1) appends a third tuple element:
  ASP-emitted correction bounds grouped by family
  `{v_max, v_min, x_bounds, y_bounds, a_max, lane_center}` with
  integer-cm entries `(t, ...)`. Constraints never count toward
  unsafe and never appear in `violations`; default `False` keeps the
  legacy 2-tuple.
- Agents beyond range are culled (distance + conflict-aware);
  malformed entries skipped. Never mutates inputs.

### get_safe_trajectory(trajectory, **kwargs) -> np.ndarray
Delegates to `TrajectoryCorrector`. Safe input returns the
IDENTICAL object (pinned); unsafe returns a new array.

---

## TrajectoryCorrector (`src/corrector.py`)

### __init__(checker, max_iterations=100, params=None, params_file=None, on_failure='raise')
- `checker` accepted by type or interface (dual-identity tolerant).
- `params` override `configs/correction_strategies.yaml` (7 keys +
  `group_cap`), which override built-ins.
- `on_failure`: `'raise'` (RuntimeError) or `'brake'` (flagged
  `emergency_hold_uncorrectable` hold for upstream takeover).

### correct(trajectory, **kwargs) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, List[Dict]]
Fixpoint: per-timestep batching with dirty-set guards, per-group
caps, stagnation break (3 identical sets). Advisory findings
(`late_signal`/warnings) pass through as `advisory_no_change`.
Returns `(corrected, applied)` with
`{rule, timestep, correction_type, original_value, corrected_value}`.

---

## CorrectorQP (`src/corrector.py`) — S14/S15, opt-in

### __init__(checker, optimizer=None, optimizer_params=None, max_rounds=3, on_failure='raise', heuristic_fallback=True, bounds='full')
- Constrained alternative to the heuristic fixpoint: calls
  `check(include_constraints=True)`, projects the trajectory with
  `ConstraintOptimizer`, then re-validates symbolically each round.
- `bounds`: `'full'` (all emitted bounds), `'caps'`
  (`v_max`/`v_min`/`a_max`), or `'boxes'` (`x_bounds`/`y_bounds`/
  `lane_center`) for the P-6 family ablation.
- `heuristic_fallback=True` delegates unresolved scenes to
  `TrajectoryCorrector` (same `on_failure` contract). **Default
  production corrector remains the heuristic**; QP measured at parity
  on mini (fix rate 0.375 both, `experiments/qp_ablation.json`) and is
  not yet GPU-calibrated (P-21).

### correct(trajectory, **kwargs) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, List[Dict]]
Same return schema as `TrajectoryCorrector.correct`. `include_*`
kwargs are managed internally and never forwarded to the fallback.

---

## ConstraintOptimizer (`src/constraint_optimizer.py`) — S14

### __init__(params=None)
Knobs: `lr` (0.2), `steps` (50), `tol_cm` (1.0), `lane_pull` (0.05).

### solve(trajectory, constraints, params=None) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Dict]
Projected-gradient projection of a (T,2) trajectory onto the grouped
ASP bounds, keeping T=0 pinned and staying close to the original.
Returns `(corrected_m, info)` with `status`, `iterations`,
`solve_time_ms`, `pinned_t0`. Pure NumPy (no scipy/GPU); validates
shape/finiteness and raises `ValueError` on bad input. The projection
is per-timestep — horizon-joint solving is P-23.

---

## correction_strategies (`src/correction_strategies.py`)

Single dispatch table: `strategy_for(rule) -> (group, method)`.
Groups: speed/collision/lane/stop/follow/comfort/min_speed (+
`ADVISORY_RULES={'late_signal'}`). `all_known_rules()` feeds the
P-9 registry test. `checker.py` must not duplicate this table.

---

## NuScenesLoader (`src/data_loader.py`)

### __init__(data_dir: str, version: str = "v1.0-mini")
`use_placeholder=True` when the path is missing. Real path uses the
devkit + map expansion (`maps/expansion/*.json`, see
`docs/environment.md`).

### get_scene_sample(scene_idx) -> Dict
`images` (6,3,256,704) uint8, `intrinsics` (6,3,3), `extrinsics`
(6,4,4) cam_T_lidar, `ego2global` (4,4), `timestamp` float.
Middle sample; token cached per scene.

### get_scene_frames(scene_idx, n_past=4) -> list
Consecutive oldest->newest sample dicts ending at the canonical
sample (walks `prev`; repeats at scene start). Powers N-2 warm runs.

### get_trajectory(scene_idx) -> np.ndarray
GT ego poses every 2nd sample (1 s grid), ego-local frame.

### get_map_data(scene_idx) -> Dict
Real extraction around the ego pose (40 m): discretized
lanes+connectors (segments/widths/directions/bike types),
`is_intersection` polygons, tiled crossings/walkways — all in
ego frame. `road_type`/`speed_limit` OMITTED when unposted
(unknown); signal phases are `{}` (honest no-knowledge).
Placeholder: two urban/13.4 lanes + reachable red + scene-9
obstacle (T-9 hostile design).

### get_traffic_lights(scene_idx) -> Dict
`{id: {color, time/times, x, y, radius, arrows}}`; `{}` on real data.

### estimate_ego_status(prev_ego2global, ego2global, dt) -> np.ndarray
(18,) upstream-layout state with pose-derived planar velocity at
indices 6,7; zeros elsewhere (mini has no CAN bus).

---

## SafetyLayerPipeline (`src/pipeline.py`)

### __init__(planner_path, rules_dir="rules", device="cuda", on_failure="raise", corrector_mode="heuristic", optimizer_params=None)
- `corrector_mode`: `'heuristic'` (default `TrajectoryCorrector`
  fixpoint) or `'qp'` (`CorrectorQP` constrained solve + re-validation,
  heuristic fallback on failure).
- `optimizer_params`: forwarded to `CorrectorQP` (`lr`, `steps`,
  `tol_cm`, `lane_pull`); ignored in heuristic mode.

### run(images, intrinsics, extrinsics, ego2global, timestamp, map_data=None, traffic_lights=None, zones=None, pedestrians=None, weather=None, ego_intent=None, extras=None) -> Dict
Predict (6,2 native @0.5 s) -> resample to the checker grid (T=0
anchor + true +1/+2/+3 s, same N-3a grid as calibration) ->
detections to CV agent trajectories (score>=0.5, cap 20, dt=STEP_DT)
-> check -> correct. Returns the native `original_trajectory`
(6,2), the checked `safe_trajectory` (4,2), `horizon_s` (3),
violations, `is_safe`, `inference_time_ms` (+ alias
`pipeline_time_ms`), per-stage `timing_ms`
`{planner,checker,corrector}`, `corrector_mode`, boxes/scores.

### run_batch(data_loader, scene_indices, zones=None, pedestrians=None, weather=None, ego_intent=None, extras=None) -> List[Dict]
Per-scene (or broadcast) context; stable error dicts (same keys,
Nones) instead of raising; `corrector` RuntimeError propagates
from `run()` (pinned).

---

## MetricsCalculator (`src/evaluation.py`)

- `collision_rate(trajs, gt_boxes, pred_boxes)` — yaw-aware
  footprints over static boxes; mismatched batch lengths raise.
- `l2_error(pred, gt)` — validated (T,2) finite inputs; empty -> 0.0.
- `violation_detection_rate` / `false_discovery_rate` (alias kept:
  `false_positive_rate`) — rule+time match, vehicle/vehicle2/type
  compared when both sides carry them.
- `average_inference_time(ms_list)`.

==> asp-safety/docs/carla_harness.md <==
# CARLA Harness — Deviations

Deviations from plan contracts for the P-25 CARLA closed-loop workstream.
This ledger carries harness/experiment divergences; rule divergences stay
in `docs/rule_divergences.md`. Nothing here changes `src/`, `rules/`, or
`tests/`; frozen files keep their contract (nextstep.md 8).

## Architecture + cadence rule (P25-B/C/D)

`experiments/carla_bridge.py` runs the closed loop in synchronous mode:
6-camera rig + collision sensor on a Tesla model3 ego -> planner
(SparseDrive neural plan, or the lane plan engine `--plan-engine lane`
used by the frozen matrix because the neural plan is OOD on CARLA
imagery) -> ASP checker -> corrector (engine `off`/`heuristic`/`qp`)
-> pure-pursuit controller -> `world.tick()`. Scenarios
(`experiments/carla_scenarios.py`) script enemies via
`resolve_setup/tick/cleanup` hooks; `run_closed_loop.py` is the CLI;
`carla_metrics.py` aggregates run JSONs.

- World settings: `synchronous_mode=True`, `fixed_delta_seconds=0.05`.
- **Cadence rule**: replan every `replan_ticks=40` ticks = 2 s sim
  (`nextstep.md` 4.3). Between replans the last (possibly corrected)
  plan is executed by the controller; the shield is therefore NOT
  causal inside the 2 s gap — a documented limitation, mitigated by
  scripting every hazard with >=2 s lead so the shield's window covers
  the event onset.
- Setup sweeps leftover actors (id >= 1000, not `traffic.*`/`spectator`);
  cleanup restores async mode and resets all map lights.

## Fact mapping (P25-B/D)

- **Agents**: CARLA actors (vehicles/walkers) -> checker `other_actors`
  with CARLA-vehicle-frame positions (x forward, y left via `F`), 1.0 s
  timestep.
- **Map**: `world.get_map().get_waypoint` -> lane facts (`in_lane`,
  `lane_center`, speed limits); route ahead of ego only.
- **Lights**: only signalized approaches contribute — a light is kept
  when its **own trigger center** is within 30 m of a stop-line
  waypoint ahead of ego (else the CARLA light actor's stop lines are
  map-wide and pollute facts). Cluster radius = min member stop ring
  + 10 m; red facts span the horizon (`times: [0..5]`). Details in
  "Traffic-light facts" below.
- **Ego plan**: columns are (forward, lateral) for checker facts;
  SparseDrive's native (lateral, forward) is swapped in `_replan`.
- Images flipped vertically (CARLA BGRA top-down vs planner BGR
  bottom-up); intrinsics K from per-camera FOV, extrinsics composed
  `C_i = S @ T_v_c @ F` (verified by projection sanity, P25-A).

## Frozen metric definitions (P25-C, `experiments/carla_metrics.py`)

- `collision_rate_pct` — share of runs whose ego collided.
- `violations_per_mile` — ASP findings / driven miles, mean per run.
- `intervention_rate` — share of replans where the shield materially
  changed the plan (`safe_vs_plan_l2 > 0` or `revert_to_plan` flag).
- `replan_wall_ms_mean` — per-replan wall time (planner + checker +
  corrector); includes sync-mode decision pauses (see "Replan wall
  semantics").
- `frame_drop_rate` — dropped sensor synchros / replans.
- Aggregate is a mean over completed runs; frozen artifact:
  `experiments/carla_metrics.json` (15 runs, 2026-10-22).

## Per-scenario verdicts (frozen 15-run matrix, 2026-10-22)

Seed 1, Town10HD_Opt, plan engine `lane`, replan 40 ticks, dt 0.05 s.
Aggregate: collision 13.3% (2/15, both off arms), intervention 0.518,
replan wall mean 1395 ms, frame drops 0, give-up 0 on every arm.

| Run | Engine | Status | Collided | sim km | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 off 161659 | off | distance_reached | no | 0.60 | clean free drive |
| S1 heur 175238 | heuristic | distance_reached | no | 0.60 | 31/91 replans corrected |
| S1 qp 175631 | qp | distance_reached | no | 0.60 | 43/103 corrected |
| S2 off 153537 | off | distance_reached | no | 0.60 | runs the pinned-red junction (95 findings) |
| S2 heur 155403 | heuristic | wall_cap | no | 0.003 | holds at the red box edge |
| S2 qp 160155 | qp | wall_cap | no | 0.003 | holds at the red box edge |
| S3 off 163204 | off | stalled | **yes** | 0.034 | rear-ends the cut-in NPC |
| S3 heur 164903 | heuristic | wall_cap | no | 0.025 | follows/holds behind the NPC |
| S3 qp 165554 | qp | wall_cap | no | 0.025 | survives |
| S4 off 171926 | off | distance_reached | no | 0.60 | kinematic walker: no collision event (limitation) |
| S4 heur 172553 | heuristic | wall_cap | no | 0.469 | waits out the jaywalker |
| S4 qp 173218 | qp | wall_cap | no | 0.518 | waits out the jaywalker |
| S5 off 173559 | off | stalled | **yes** | 0.028 | collides in the turn conflict |
| S5 heur 174229 | heuristic | wall_cap | no | 0.016 | holds |
| S5 qp 174851 | qp | wall_cap | no | 0.016 | holds |

## Caveats (evidence honesty)

- **Single seed**: the frozen matrix is n=15 at seed 1; collision-rate
  confidence intervals are wide. Seed extension (seeds 2-3) is planned.
- **Kinematic scripted actors**: VRUs and NPCs are kinematic, so the S4
  off arm drives through the walker with no collision event; S4's
  evidence is shield-hold behavior, not collision rate. S3/S5 NPCs do
  collide (vehicles are physics-impulsed).
- **Lane plan engine drives the ego**: the neural planner is OOD on
  CARLA imagery (see "Neural planner state"); neural plans are logged
  unshielded in the run JSONs.
- **Replan-gap non-causality**: corrections target the old plan's
  future inside the 2 s gap (documented in nextstep.md 9, echoed in
  "Replan wall semantics").
- **Mini-level + in-sim evidence only**: no deployment claim follows.
- Wall caps on hazard scenarios (S2/S3/S4/S5 shielded arms) mean
  "survived the scripted hazard", not "drove the route".
- Demo/visible-window runs live in `experiments/runs/_view_demo/`,
  outside the frozen glob; the Epic window is infeasible on this 6 GB
  rig (backlog P-26).

## Axis conventions (P25-B, S18 closure)

- **Plan columns are (lateral, forward), swapped in `_replan`**
  (`plan_native[1::2][:3, ::-1]`): SparseDrive returns (lateral, forward);
  the bridge reorders to (forward, lateral) for the lane plan path but
  keeps checker plan facts in (forward, lateral).
- **Camera images flipped vertically** (`arr[:, :, 2::-1]`): CARLA BGRA
  buffer rows come top-to-bottom; the planner expects BGR bottom-up.
- **CAM_CONFIGS are 6-tuples** (name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz) — the plan
  showed 5-tuples.
- **Steer sign calibrated**: `steer_sign = -1.0` (verified in
  `experiments/carla_sign_test.py`, port 2001 test pattern).

## Controller constants (measured)

wheelbase 2.85 m; lookahead 0.6 * v clamped 2-6 m; steer_gain 2.0;
steer_deadband 0.02; throttle_gain 1.2; throttle_smooth 0.2;
v_target_cap 13.9 m/s.

## Neural planner state (P25-B evidence)

- SparseDrive inference meets the latency budget on closed-loop runs
  (exec_ms mean ~2629 < 3500) but is out-of-distribution on CARLA
  imagery: plans curve left-laterally (1.06 -> 7.65 m over 3 s) and the
  ego crashed into a static building at 11.05 s (run
  `S1_1_off_20261022_140825.json`), then stalled at 47 m.
- Decision: the lane engine (`--plan-engine lane`) drives the closed-loop
  scenarios; neural plans remain logged when requested. Documented as a
  deliberate deviation from the plan-level assumption that the neural
  plan is the default planner upstream of the shield.

## Junction overrun (P25-B root cause)

Track-junction waypoint chain walks were verified clean manually; the
crash at (41.2, 72.1) came from the controller outrunning the plan:
junction-speed plan horizon (10.5 m at 3.5 m/s) is consumed between 2 s
replans (5.8 m per 2 s), look-ahead latched past plan end, target flipped
backward (alpha -126 deg), vehicle steered into vegetation. Fixes:
(a) `_control_tick` extends the target beyond plan end along the final
segment direction; (b) junction speed cap raised 3.5 -> 5.0 m/s in
`_lane_plan`.

## Traffic-light facts (P25-D)

- CARLA 0.9.15 stress: `TrafficLight` exposes the `trigger_volume`
  property but NOT a `get_trigger_volume()` accessor; `carla_context`
  fell back to that accessor and silently returned zero light facts.
  Fixed to read the property.
- Cluster radius: CARLA light actors reference stop waypoints across the
  map (tl 9 stop lines 74+ m from its own trigger center). Max-over-all
  stop distances inflated the red box to ~130 m, painting "inside the
  red junction" findings from far out and making hold-position
  corrections unfixable (100-iteration give-ups on S2). Radius now =
  min over cluster members of that member's own stop-line ring +10
  (observed 41.7 m at the Town10HD cluster).
- Light-state facts span the prediction horizon (`times: [0..5]`), not
  just T=0, so a red reading constrains the whole plan slice.
- S2 spawn selection requires a stop waypoint within 30 m of the light's
  own trigger center (a genuinely signalized approach), then freezes the
  whole junction cluster red: per-tick `set_state(Red)` PLUS a one-time
  `set_red_time(1e6)` + `reset_group()` pin, because CARLA group timers
  otherwise leave red after ~4 min and drop yellow/green flickers.
- Every scenario cleanup resets all map lights (`set_green_time(20)`,
  `set_yellow_time(3)`, `set_red_time(20)`, `reset_group()`) to keep
  cross-run light state deterministic.
- S1/S3/S4/S5 spawn picking skips any candidate whose 130 m route enters
  a signalized cluster (45 m radius around trigger centers), so scripted
  events dominate the evidence instead of light phase timing.
- Consequence: S2's red duration is realistic (holds through the full
  run); the off arm drives through red (81 red_light findings), both
  shield arms hold at the box edge with give_up_rate 0.0.

## Corrector behavior (P25-D, experiments-side only)

- `longitudinal_clearance` remapped from the collision family (lateral
  tail shift) to the follow family (slow down) at `carla_bridge` import
  time: a lateral shift cannot fix rear-end gaps and ping-ponged to
  100-iteration give-ups on cut-in runs.
- Both correctors constructed with `on_failure='brake'`: fixpoint
  exhaustion now returns an emergency-hold plan instead of raising and
  being caught as `revert_to_plan` (raw plan execution).
- Heuristic corrector gets `lateral_shift_m=-2.0` (cut-ins come from the
  left lane: the +2.0 default shifts INTO the intruder).
- S1 heuristic give-ups observed earlier (12/62 revert rows) came from
  the old revert path plus a leaked-NPC collision; after the fixes the
  heuristic arm passes S1 with give_up_rate 0.0.

## Harness robustness (P25-D)

- Setup sweeps leftover actors (leaked NPCs/walkers from aborted probe
  scripts sat on the shared spawn routes and poisoned later runs with
  false stalls/collisions).
- Stall detector skips windows that include planned holds (`hold_ticks`
  gate): resuming after a red-light / jaywalker wait would false-positive
  the zero-progress check at the resume boundary.
- Known limitation: kinematic VRUs (the S4 walker and S3/S5 NPC vehicles)
  do not generate physical collision events against the physics ego; the
  off arm for S4 drives through the walker without a collision event.
  S4's evidence is therefore shield-hold behavior rather than
  collision-rate.

## Choreography (P25-D)

- S3 cut-in re-parameterized: NPC spawns 30 m ahead in the adjacent
  lane, alternates its own lane ~1 s, then swings into ego's lane
  touching down at ego-arc 26 m at t ~ 2.4 s (~1 s before the 7 m/s ego
  arrives). The off arm rear-ends it (~34 m); the shield arms survive
  (full-tracked follow / emergency hold).
- S5 oncoming left-turn NPC: spawns 15 m before the turn start, begins
  the left turn at t=2.5 s, speed 6 m/s.
- S4 walker: start offset 5.5 m from the path center point at arc 15,
  crosses the full road at 1.2 m/s.

## Replan wall semantics

Sync mode freezes simulation time during replans: a 5.3 s corrector
fixpoint (S3/S5 adversarial geometry) charges wall cost but zero sim
time; the reported `replan_wall_ms_mean` includes those decision pauses.

## Visible window (S23)

- `experiments/carla_launch.py` gained `--quality {Low,Epic}`,
  `--windowed` (visible window 1280x720, replaces `-RenderOffScreen`)
  and `--stream-pool N` (`-ExecCmds="r.Streaming.PoolSize N"`).
  Defaults stay Low/offscreen so every harness caller is unchanged.
- **Epic is infeasible on this rig**: four boot crashes during map load
  before the driver update (Epic windowed x2, Epic windowed + pool
  trim, Epic offscreen — last one lived to ~110 s). Retried 2026-10-23
  with NVIDIA driver 617.42: the early boot crash is fixed (Epic
  windowed now survives ~2-3 min) but it dies during full map load
  with free VRAM down to 65 MiB — the complete Epic texture/shadow set
  needs more than 6 GB. Epic + `r.Streaming.PoolSize 1200` died within
  100 s (trim too late in the load order). 0.9.15 has no intermediate
  tier, so the ladder resolves to **windowed Low** (backlog P-26).
- `experiments/carla_vram_probe.py` gates the shared GPU: server +
  window + 6-cam rig -> planner load -> warm `predict_frame` vs
  >=1.5 GB free. Windowed Low: PASS, 4.03 GB post-predict.
- Ego-follow spectator: `BridgeConfig.view_ego` (`--view` on
  `run_closed_loop.py`) repositions the spectator 12 m behind / 7 m
  above the ego, pitch -25 deg, each tick. Rendering convenience only
  — no physics or metric effect.
- Live demo pair written to `experiments/runs/_view_demo/` (outside the
  frozen matrix glob): S3 off collided at sim 10.15 s; S3 heuristic
  wall_cap at 0.025 km, collided=False, give_up 0.0.

==> asp-safety/docs/conventions.md <==
# Conventions — ASP Safety Layer (DC-2)

Single reference for the units and contracts every rule, builder,
and test assumes. AGENTS.md Subdivision 3's original float-based
schema is superseded by this + `asp_traffic_rules.md`.

## Units and frames

- Positions are meters in code, **integer centimeters (x100)** in ASP.
  `checker.SCALE_FACTOR = 100`. All ASP arithmetic is integer-only:
  no floats, no `sqrt()`; squared distances replace Euclidean ones.
- Timesteps `T` are `0..5` (6 total). `checker.STEP_DT = 1.0 s` is the
  canonical step every speed/gap threshold is derived for (pinned by
  `test_step_dt_contract`). Model-native 0.5 s outputs MUST be
  resampled before `check()`; calibration and `pipeline.run()` both
  anchor T=0 at the ego pose and stamp `horizon_s` (3 s pred, blind
  beyond).
- Trajectories are ego-frame `(T, 2)`, x = forward, y = left.
  Map layers are global in nuScenes data and converted to ego frame
  by the loader (`_map_to_ego_frame`) before checking.
- Clingo integers are 32-bit: keep every product below ~2e9
  (decimeter units, sign-split abs forms, banded lookups).

## Speed semantics (GAP-3/GAP-9)

- `speed(V,T,S)` / `speed2(V,T,S)` / `velocity_sq(V,T,S)` are SQUARED
  speeds in (cm/step)^2. Limit/cap facts (`speed_limit`,
  `zone_speed_limit`, `vru_speed_cap`, `min_speed_limit`,
  `confidence_speed_cap`) stay LINEAR (cm/step); rule bodies square
  them at comparison (`S2 > Limit*Limit`).
- 50 km/h default cap = 1389 cm/step, squared = 1929321. It applies
  ONLY to bare trajectories (no mapped lanes); mapped-but-unposted
  roads stay silent (N-1).

## Fact naming

- `pos(V,T,X,Y)` + `vehicle(V)` are the only required inputs.
  `point/4` aliases `pos/4`.
- `gap_dist` = Euclidean total separation (cm). True along-track is
  `longitudinal_gap` (signed, + = ahead). `following_gap` mirrors
  `gap_dist` from the follower perspective. `closing_rate` =
  instantaneous velocity-projection closing speed (cm/step, >= 0).
- `about_to_enter` = approach band + motion toward center
  (`approaching_zone`) or holding at the stop line. `approaching/3`
  with a vehicle second arg is vehicle-vehicle convergence (R6.4);
  with a numeric second arg, a merge endpoint (R15.3).
- `stopped_before` = stopped NEAR the feature (not anywhere);
  `yielding_maneuver` = stopped anywhere (intentional, R6.4).
- `stopped(V,0)` holds via zero heading (velocity needs T-1).
- Agent vehicles are `vehicle_{i}` in array order. `emergency_vehicle`
  facts must reuse those ids. Planner boxes are `[x,y,z,w,l,h,yaw]`
  with planar `[vx,vy,vz]` kept separately as `velocities`.
- Internal arbitration atoms (`ttc_band`, `threat_level`,
  `comfort_score`, `future_violation`, `any_violation`) surface only
  via `check(include_internal=True)` and never count toward unsafe.

## Correction contract

- `TrajectoryCorrector` owns all strategies (single dispatch table in
  `correction_strategies.py`). `checker.get_safe_trajectory`
  delegates; safe input returns the IDENTICAL object.
- Fixpoint: per-timestep batching with dirty-set guards, per-group
  caps (`group_cap`), stagnation break (3 identical sets), then
  `RuntimeError` ('raise') or flagged `emergency_hold_uncorrectable`
  ('brake').

## Constraint atoms (P1, 2026-10-03)

- Emitted alongside violation/hazard heads, collected only via
  `check(include_constraints=True)`; `#show` block lives in
  `helpers.lp`. Units are cm / cm-step integers like all ASP facts.
- `constraint_v_max(V,T,Limit)` / `constraint_v_min(V,T,Limit)`:
  linear cm/step caps/floors (rules square at comparison).
- `constraint_x_bounds(V,T,Min,Max)` /
  `constraint_y_bounds(V,T,Min,Max)`: allowed-region boxes in cm.
- `constraint_a_max(V,T,Limit)`: cm/step^2 accel bound (comfort).
- `constraint_lane_center(V,T,Y)`: preferred lateral position, cm.
- Python shape: `{'v_max': [(t, limit), ...], 'v_min': [...],
  'x_bounds': [(t, min, max), ...], 'y_bounds': [...],
  'a_max': [...], 'lane_center': [(t, y), ...]}` (see
  `TrajectoryChecker._group_constraints`). Rules without a natural
  bound document their exemption in-file ('documented exemption').

## QP solver contract (P3/P4, 2026-10-03)

- `ConstraintOptimizer.solve(traj_m, constraints_dict)` -> (corrected
  meters, info {status, iterations, solve_time_ms, pinned_t0}).
  Pure NumPy; defaults lr 0.2 / 50 steps / 1 cm tol / 0.05 lane pull.
- T=0 (ego pose) is never moved. Overlapping bounds: tightest box
  and minimum cap win. v_min skips directionless (stopped) segments.
- `CorrectorQP.correct()` returns the heuristic 5-key applied-entry
  schema with `rule='qp_optimization'` (plus fallback entries when
  the heuristic finishes the fix). Safe input returns a copy and [].

## Corrector selection (2026-10-03)

- `SafetyLayerPipeline(corrector_mode='heuristic'|'qp')`, default
  heuristic. `run()` stamps `corrector_mode`; error dicts carry it
  too (schema parity). `run_calibration.py --corrector` mirrors the
  flag and stamps `corrector_mode` in the JSON summary.
- P6-lite reading: on frozen cold preds QP matches the heuristic fix
  rate with lower mean latency, but both fail the same re-route
  cases -- constrained nudging is not re-routing. Closed-loop and
  warm-data numbers stay open.

## Bound families (P6 ablation, 2026-10-03)

- `CorrectorQP(bounds='full'|'caps'|'boxes')`. caps = limit-type
  bounds (v_max/v_min/a_max); boxes = region-type bounds
  (x_bounds/y_bounds plus the lane_center preference). Filtering is
  applied to the initial constraints AND every rounds-loop
  re-check. Subset runs disable heuristic fallback so fix rates
  measure the family, not the fallback.

## Hold-back boxes (S17 fix, 2026-10-03)

- Following/longitudinal/platoon bounds are ONE-SIDED: an ahead
  threat caps ego at (threat - gap) with the far side open at
  +-1000000 cm; a behind threat mirrors. Centered boxes are
  forbidden for gap rules (they attract closest-point optimizers).
  Side comes from the signed gap (R4.2) or X2>=X in ego-forward
  frame (R3.2/R15.2).
- Known limit: static boxes are per-timestep; against moving
  threats with tail coupling, rounds do not converge (scene 8).
  Horizon-joint solving is future work; do not raise max_rounds
  to compensate.

# Data Contracts & Function Table

## 1. Master data-flow table

| Stage | Producer | Consumer | Object | Type | Shape | Meaning | Example |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| sample | `data_loader.get_scene_sample` | `pipeline.run` | `images` | ndarray uint8 | (6,3,256,704) | 6 cams FRONT/FL/FR/BACK/BL/BR | zeros/placeholder |
| sample | loader | pipeline | `intrinsics` | f32 | (6,3,3) | fx=fy=400,cx=352,cy=128 | eye-based |
| sample | loader | pipeline | `extrinsics` | f32 | (6,4,4) | cam-to-vehicle | eye |
| sample | loader | pipeline | `ego2global` | f32 | (4,4) | vehicle→global, x=idx*10 | eye |
| sample | loader | pipeline | `timestamp` | float | — | unix | 1234567890+idx*.5 |
| context | `get_map_data` | `checker` | `map_data` | dict | — | real lanes/widths/dirs + intersections + tiled crossings/walkways, ego-frame; NO road_type/limits (unknown) | N-1 |
| context | `get_traffic_lights` | `checker` | `traffic_lights` | dict | — | int_0 red times 0..5 (placeholder); {} on real data | honest no-knowledge |
| proposal | `planner.predict` | `pipeline.run` | `trajectory` | f32 | (6,2) | ego m, t=.5..3s | x=[2.5..15] |
| proposal | planner | pipeline | `boxes` | f32 | (N,7) | [x,y,z,w,l,h,yaw] | 2 boxes |
| proposal | planner | pipeline | `scores` | f32 | (N,) | [0,1] | [.95,.87] |
| proposal | planner | pipeline | `velocities` | f32 | (N,3) | [vx,vy,vz] planar estimates (P-18) | zeros placeholder |
| agents | `pipeline._detections_to_trajectories` | `checker` | `other_vehicles` | list of (H,2) | — | CV projections, score>=0.5, cap 20 (P-18/P-5) | filtered |
| facts | `checker._add_*` (one-shot `_FactBatcher`) | clingo | `pos(ego,T,X,Y).` etc. | str | — | cm ints (×100); gap=Euclidean, longitudinal=signed, closing=velocity-projection | `pos(ego,0,250,0).` |
| answer | clingo (`#show` block) | `on_model` | shown symbols | Model | — | violation/hazard/warning/insufficient (+ttc/threat/comfort/future/any on request) | `violation(red_light,ego,5)` |
| verdict | `checker.check` | pipeline/corrector | `(is_safe, violations)` | bool, List[Dict] | — | warnings + internals excluded; `include_internal=True` appends ttc/threat/comfort/future/any | `False, [{rule,time…}]` |
| fix | `corrector.correct` | pipeline | `(corrected, applied)` | (6,2), List[Dict] | — | dirty-set + group caps + stagnation break; 'raise' or flagged 'brake' hold | hold at T5 |
| result | `pipeline.run` | caller/batch | result dict | dict | 12 keys | both trajs + meta + timing_ms split + stable error schema | §5 arch doc |
| metrics | `MetricsCalculator` | `run_evaluation` | avg ms etc. | float/dict | — | offline summary; yaw-aware boxes, FDR name, validated l2 | safe-rate, p95 |

## 2. Function table (runtime participants)

| File | Function/Class | Called by | Calls | Input | Output | Side effects |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| pipeline.py | `SafetyLayerPipeline.__init__` | tests, eval | planner/checker/corrector ctors | paths, device, on_failure | None | none |

| pipeline.py | `run_batch` | tests, eval | loader getters, run | loader, indices, ctx | List[Dict] (stable error schema) | prints errors |
| pipeline.py | `_detections_to_trajectories` | run, calibration | — | boxes/vels/scores, horizon | agent traj list (score>=0.5, cap 20) | none |

| neural_planner.py | `predict` (+`nav_cmd`, `ego_status`) | pipeline, tests | placeholder/inference | 5 arrays + opts | traj/boxes/scores/velocities | cold reset |
| neural_planner.py | `begin_sequence`/`predict_frame`/`end_sequence` | temporal harness | inference (warm) | frames + status | same dict | bank state |
| neural_planner.py | `_load_model` | `__init__` | build_detector, torch.load | config+ckpt | model | sys.path insert |
| neural_planner.py | `_run_inference` | `predict`/`predict_frame` | model() | tensors (+img_metas if warm) | raw dict | none |
| neural_planner.py | `_postprocess` | inference | — | raw list | traj/boxes/scores/velocities | none (fail-loud) |
| neural_planner.py | `_generate_placeholder` | `predict` | — | — | fixed dict | `seed(42)` global |
| data_loader.py | `NuScenesLoader.__init__` | pipeline, tests | devkit (guarded) | dir, version | None | prints |
| data_loader.py | `get_scene_sample` | pipeline, tests | placeholder/real | idx | sample dict | RNG seed |
| data_loader.py | `get_scene_frames` | temporal harness | token walk | idx, n_past | frame list | caches token |
| data_loader.py | `estimate_ego_status` | temporal harness | — | pose pair + dt | (18,) state | none |
| data_loader.py | `get_map_data` | pipeline, tests | placeholder/real extraction | idx | map dict (ego-frame, no posted limits) | caches map |
| data_loader.py | `get_traffic_lights` | pipeline, tests | placeholder | idx | lights dict ({} real) | none |
| checker.py | `TrajectoryChecker.__init__` | pipeline, tests | blob loader | rules_dir, range | None | parses blob |
| checker.py | `check` (+`include_internal`) | pipeline, corrector, tests | cull, builders, ground, solve | traj + ctx | (bool, List) | none (no input mutation) |
| checker.py | `_cull_agents` / `_shown_atom_to_dict` | check | — | trajs / atom | kept list / dict-or-None | none |
| checker.py | `_FactBatcher` | check | ctrl.add (once) | fact strings | — | single parse |
| checker.py | `get_safe_trajectory` | tests | check, correct | traj + ctx | array (identical iff safe) | none |
| corrector.py | `TrajectoryCorrector.__init__` | pipeline, checker, tests | — | checker, iters, on_failure | None | none |
| corrector.py | `correct` | pipeline, checker, tests | check, strategies | traj + ctx | (array, applied) | none |
| evaluation.py | `MetricsCalculator.*` | tests, eval (avg-time) | numpy | lists/arrays | floats | none (validated) |
| run_evaluation.py | `run_evaluation` | CLI | pipeline, loader | num/paths | summary dict | stdout |
| run_calibration.py | `run_calibration` | CLI | planner, checker, corrector | data/flags (+dump/extrapo-late) | summary + optional trajs dump | stdout/JSON |
| run_temporal.py | warm-vs-cold | CLI | planner, loader | scenes | delta table | stdout |
| sweep_thresholds.py | threshold sweep | CLI | checker + trajs dump | dump path | knees table + JSON | restores .lp files |

## 3. "If I change this…" map

- **Planner output format** → `_postprocess` + checker validation break; tests
  `test_sparse_drive/test_pipeline` fail first.
- **Trajectory shape** (≠(6,2)/T>6) → `check` raises `ValueError`; corrector
  grouping assumes per-T dicts.
- **ASP predicates/facts** → rules stop firing silently (clingo is
  open-world); `test_rules` + vacuous-guard catch it.
- **One rule file** → blob rebuild includes it automatically; missing file
  breaks `#include` parse (blob=None → glob fallback; total absence →
  `RuntimeError`).
- **Correction strategy** → convergence behavior changes; `test_corrector`
  + priority test are the tripwires.
- **Config YAML** → nothing (unread); change constructor args / code
  constants instead.
- **Checkpoint** → placeholder↔real flip; only shape contract matters
  downstream.
- **Data loader** → `run_batch` schema; placeholder geometry (int_0) drives
  whether red-fires coverage exists.
- **Clingo invocation** (flags/timeout) → solve latency/correctness; no
  timeout currently set.
- **Metrics** → only `run_evaluation` output + `test_evaluation`; no
  feedback into planning.

==> asp-safety/docs/evaluation_design_asp_correction.md <==
# Evaluation Design: ASP-Guided Trajectory Correction vs. Heuristic Corrector

> **Shipped status (2026-10-21, sessions S13-S17)**: the A1 loop
> (`check(include_constraints=True)` -> pure-NumPy projected-gradient
> MVP -> ASP re-validation), the B1/B2 comparators, and the D2
> frozen-dump ablations ARE shipped. Measured so far: fix rate
> full-set QP 0.375 = heuristic 0.375 on the 8-unsafe-scene dump
> (scene-7 10x latency win), bound-family analog for A2/A3:
> full 0.375 > boxes 0.125 > caps 0.00. Deltas from this plan: pure-
> NumPy PGD (A5 pulled forward, no cvxopt), no A4/A6/A7 ablations yet,
> no B3 oracle; A7 direction filed as P-23. Evidence:
> `experiments/qp_ablation.json`, `qp_sweep.json`, `session.md`
> day summary 2026-10-03. Warm GPU A/Bs (2026-10-21, S18): fix-rate
> parity confirmed on real warm plans — calibration 0.50 = 0.50
> (*calibration_qp.json*), tracked replay 0.50 = 0.50 with identical
> scene sets (*replay_warm_heuristic.json* / *replay_warm_qp.json*);
> end-to-end QP latency is parity-to-slightly-worse (fallback-
> dominated means). Sections below are the ORIGINAL plan, kept
> for design context.
>
> **D3 shipped status (2026-10-22/23, P-25, session.md S19-S24)**:
> the CARLA closed-loop harness IS shipped and measured —
> `experiments/carla_bridge.py` + S1-S5 scenarios, frozen 15-run
> pilot matrix in `experiments/carla_metrics.json`: collision rate
> 13.3% (S3/S5 off arms only; all shielded arms 0 collisions),
> intervention rate 0.518, frame drops 0, give-up 0 throughout.
> Caveats: single seed, kinematic scripted actors (VRUs generate
> no physical collision events), lane plan engine drives the ego,
> ego-follow window at Low (Epic infeasible on 6 GB VRAM, backlog
> P-26). D3 rows below updated accordingly.

**Status**: Shipped (A1 loop + B1/B2 ablations on the D2 dump); the plan below is design context only.  
**Objective**: Provide experimental evidence that ASP-emitted structured correction constraints + continuous optimizer (QP/projected gradient) + symbolic re-validation outperforms the current heuristic dispatch-table corrector.

---

## 1. Baselines

| Baseline | Description | Role |
|----------|-------------|------|
| **B0: SparseDrive only (no correction)** | Planner raw 6×0.5 s output resampled to 4×1.0 s checker grid | Lower bound — planner raw safety/quality |
| **B1: Heuristic corrector (current)** | `TrajectoryCorrector` with `correction_strategies.py` dispatch table (7 params, 6 strategy groups) | **Primary comparator** — what we aim to beat |
| **B2: ASP-constrained corrector (proposed)** | `TrajectoryChecker.check(include_constraints=True)` → QP optimizer → re-validate | **Experimental treatment** |
| **B3: Oracle constrained corrector (upper bound)** | Hand-crafted optimal constraints from GT trajectory (same optimizer) | Ceiling — shows max potential of constraint-based approach |

All baselines share:
- Same `TrajectoryChecker` (63 rules)
- Same `NuScenesLoader` with tracked annotations (184 agents, 10 mini scenes)
- Same resampling: pred 6×0.5 s → T=0 anchor + pred[1,3,5] as T=1,2,3 (horizon_s=3)
- Same agent trajectories from planner detections (score≥0.5, cap 20, checker-culled)
- Same map data (N-1 extraction: lanes, widths, directions, intersections, tiled crosswalks/sidewalks)
- Same `on_failure='brake'` fallback (emergency hold at earliest remaining violation)

---

## 2. Ablations

| Ablation | Modification | Hypothesis Tested |
|----------|--------------|-------------------|
| **A1: Full constraint set** | All 63 rules emit constraint atoms (`x_bounds`, `y_bounds`, `v_max`, `a_max`, `lane_center`) | Complete ASP→QP→ASP loop |
| **A2: Speed + lane only** | Only R1.x, R2.x emit constraints; others use heuristic fallback | Are speed/lane constraints sufficient for most gains? |
| **A3: Collision + following only** | Only R3.x, R4.x emit constraints | Are agent-interaction constraints the differentiator? |
| **A4: No re-validation** | Skip final `checker.check()` after QP step | Is symbolic re-validation necessary for safety? |
| **A5: QP vs projected gradient** | Swap `cvxopt` QP for 10-step projected gradient (no external dep) | Is exact QP needed or is PGD sufficient? |
| **A6: Slack variables** | Add soft slacks on all constraints with penalty weight sweep | Does softening avoid infeasibility without sacrificing safety? |
| **A7: Stale constraint cache** | Reuse constraints from previous iteration when violation set unchanged | Does caching close the latency gap? |

---

## 3. Datasets / Scenarios

| Dataset | Size | Agent Type | Purpose |
|---------|------|------------|---------|
| **D1: nuScenes mini tracked-annotation replay (primary)** | 10 scenes, 184 tracked agents (92 moving / 92 parked) | Ground-truth tracked boxes from `sample_annotation` series | **Main evidence** — true agent histories eliminate parked-car hallucination noise; R3/R4/R13 verdicts become evidence |
| **D2: Calibration dump** | 10 scenes, frozen `trajs.json` (GT + pred_1s + map + boxes) | GPU-free sweep source | Threshold sweeps, ablations without GPU |
| **D3: CARLA closed-loop (P-25, shipped 2026-10-22)** | 5 scenarios (free drive, red-light run, cut-in, jaywalker, oncoming left turn) x 3 engines x 1 seed = 15-run frozen matrix | Scripted CARLA actors (kinematic) | **Safety-case numbers open-loop cannot provide**: violation rate/mile, intervention rate 0.518, collision rate 13.3% off vs 0% shielded (`experiments/carla_metrics.json`) |

**Scenario tags** (for per-scenario analysis):
- `high_speed` (ego > 15 m/s at any T)
- `dense_traffic` (> 10 moving agents within 50 m)
- `intersection_heavy` (> 3 controlled intersections in horizon)
- `pedestrian_present` (any VRU within 10 m)
- `construction_zone` (active zone overlapping trajectory)

---

## 4. Safety Metrics

| Metric | Formula | Target for B2 vs B1 |
|--------|---------|---------------------|
| **Collision rate (primary)** | `# scenes with any R4.x violation after correction / total scenes` | B2 ≤ B1; target **≤ 0.05** (current B1 replay: 0.5 fixed → 0.5 residual collisions) |
| **Red-light violation rate** | `# scenes with R5.1 after correction / total` | B2 = 0 (hard constraint) |
| **Lane departure rate** | `# scenes with R2.1 after correction / total` | B2 ≤ B1 |
| **VRU proximity violation rate** | `# scenes with R1.5 / R9.x after correction / total` | B2 ≤ B1 |
| **Critical rule residual** | `# scenes with any Critical-priority violation after correction / total` | B2 = 0 |
| **False-positive proxy (GT safe rate)** | `# scenes where GT has 0 violations / total` | Context — should stay ~0.3 (known map gaps) |

**Per-rule breakdown**: Report counts for each of the 63 rules (violation/hazard/warning) pre- and post-correction for both B1 and B2.

---

## 5. Trajectory-Quality Metrics

| Metric | Formula | Target |
|--------|---------|--------|
| **L2(pred, GT) post-correction** | Mean L2 between corrected trajectory and GT (interpolated to checker grid) | B2 ≤ B1 + 0.5 m (safety should not excessively degrade tracking) |
| **Heading RMSE** | `sqrt(mean((atan2(dy_corr, dx_corr) - atan2(dy_gt, dx_gt))²))` | B2 ≤ B1 |
| **Jerk (comfort proxy)** | `mean(|a_t - a_{t-1}|)` over corrected trajectory | B2 ≤ B1 (constraints include `a_max` from R12.1) |
| **Path length deviation** | `|sum(||Δcorr||) - sum(||Δgt||)| / sum(||Δgt||)` | B2 ≤ 0.15 |
| **Constraint tightness** | Mean `(v_max_emitted - v_actual) / v_max_emitted` at each T | Diagnostic — shows how conservative ASP bounds are |

---

## 6. Computational-Cost Metrics

| Metric | B1 (current) | B2 Target | Measurement |
|--------|--------------|-----------|-------------|
| **Checker latency** | ~180 ms (real), 37 ms (20-agent synthetic) | ≤ 200 ms (added `#show constraint/*` atoms) | `time.perf_counter()` around `check()` |
| **Corrector latency** | ~5,700 ms (replay), ~2,500 ms (calibration) | **≤ 3,000 ms** (target: 50% reduction via fewer fixpoint iterations) | Wall time of `correct()` |
| **Fixpoint iterations** | 8–15 typical (stagnation break at 3) | **≤ 5** (QP solves all active constraints jointly) | Counter in `correct()` |
| **QP solve time** | N/A | **≤ 5 ms** per iteration (8 vars × 4 timesteps) | `time.perf_counter()` inside optimizer |
| **Total pipeline latency** | ~9–12 s/scene | **≤ 6 s/scene** | End-to-end `pipeline.run()` |

---

## 7. Failure Analysis

For every scene where B2 fails to fix (residual violations after correction):
1. **Violation taxonomy**: Which rule(s) remain? (Critical vs High vs Medium)
2. **Constraint emission trace**: What constraints did ASP emit for this scene? Were they infeasible?
3. **QP status**: Optimal / infeasible / unbounded / max-iter
4. **Slack activation**: Which soft slacks (if A6) were non-zero?
4. **Comparison to B1**: Did B1 fix it? If yes, why did B2 fail?
5. **Root cause category**:
   - Over-constrained (conflicting rules) → need priority-weighted slacks
   - Under-constrained (missing constraint atom for rule X) → ASP gap
   - Optimizer numerical issue → solver config
   - Stale constraints (cache hit) → invalidation logic

Produce a **failure ledger** (JSON) per scene for paper appendix.

---

## 8. Statistical Reporting

| Aspect | Method |
|--------|--------|
| **Per-scene paired comparison** | B1 vs B2 on same 10 scenes (paired t-test / Wilcoxon on binary fix/no-fix) |
| **Confidence intervals** | Bootstrap 10,000 resamples for collision rate, L2, latency |
| **Effect size** | Cohen's d for L2; risk difference for binary safety outcomes |
| **Multiple comparison correction** | Bonferroni across 6 ablation variants (A1–A6) |
| **Visualization** | Per-scene waterfall: GT violations → pred violations → B1 residual → B2 residual; latency scatter (checker vs corrector) |
| **Reproducibility** | Seed=42 for planner; frozen `trajs.json` for GPU-free ablations; `requirements.txt` + `pip freeze` snapshot |

---

- ✅ Planner L2: **11.54 m** (replay cold), **10.18 m** (calibration warm)

## 10. Open-Loop vs Closed-Loop Evidence Gap

| Property | Open-Loop (Current D1/D2) | Closed-Loop (D3 CARLA) |
|----------|---------------------------|------------------------|
| **Agent behavior** | Replayed from log (frozen) | Reactive to ego (simulated) |
| **Ego action affects agents** | No | Yes |
| **Multi-step consequences** | Not observed | Observed |
| **Metrics** | L2, rule violations, fix rate | Violations/mile, interventions, min TTC |
| **Safety case strength** | "Layer flags/corrects logged plans" | "Layer prevents accidents in sim" |
| **Status** | **Implemented, evidence collected** | **Feasibility verified (S12), bridge/scenarios/metrics not built** |

**Recommendation**: Publish open-loop evidence (D1 + ablation table) as primary contribution. Note closed-loop as "future work — infrastructure provisioned, bridge design documented in `replay_eval_design.md` §B". Do not overclaim.

## 12. Acceptance Criteria for "ASP Correction is Better"

| Criterion | Threshold |
|-----------|-----------|
| **Safety**: B2 collision rate ≤ B1 collision rate (on D1 replay) | Binary: yes/no |
| **Safety**: B2 Critical residual = 0 | Binary: yes/no |
| **Quality**: B2 L2 ≤ B1 L2 + 0.5 m | Numerical |
| **Speed**: B2 corrector latency ≤ 3,000 ms (50% of B1 replay) | Numerical |
| **Convergence**: B2 fixpoint iterations ≤ 5 (vs B1 8–15) | Numerical |
| **Ablation**: A1 (full constraints) ≥ A2/A3 (partial) on fix rate | Numerical |
| **Regression**: All 93 existing tests pass | Binary: `pytest tests/ -v` |

If ≥ 5 of 7 criteria met with statistical significance (p < 0.05 on paired tests), the ASP-constrained approach is validated for the paper.

---

## 13. Risks & Mitigations

| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |
|------|------------|--------|------------|
| QP infeasibility on complex scenes | Medium | Corrector fails → brake fallback | Soft slacks (A6); priority-weighted penalties; warm-start from previous iterate |
| Constraint emission incomplete (rules missing atoms) | Medium | B2 underperforms B1 | `test_constraint_emission.py` asserts every rule with `violation` head emits ≥1 constraint |
| Latency regression (QP + re-validate > fixpoint) | Low | B2 slower than B1 | Cache constraints when violation set unchanged (A7); `#show` keeps atom scan fast |
| cvxopt/scipy dependency issues | Low | Build breaks | Fallback to projected gradient (A5) with pure NumPy |
| CARLA never provisions | High | ~~D3 unavailable~~ | MATERIALIZED 2026-10-22: server provisioned on the project laptop (0.9.15, Town10HD_Opt); D3 shipped — cite `experiments/carla_metrics.json` with single-seed/kinematic-actor caveats |

## 15. Appendix: Key Rules for Constraint Emission

These rules have **natural, well-defined geometric bounds** that translate directly to constraints — highest priority for P1:

| Rule | Constraint Atoms to Emit |
|------|---------------------------|
| R1.1–R1.3 | `v_max(ego, T, Limit)` — speed cap per timestep |
| R1.4 | `v_min(ego, T, MinLimit)` — speed floor (when safe) |
| R1.5 | `v_max(ego, T, Cap)` — VRU-proximity cap (min across VRUs) |
| R2.1 | `y_bounds(ego, T, Y_center - W/2, Y_center + W/2)` — lane bounds |
| R2.2 | `y_bounds` + `x_bounds` for lane-change corridor + gap requirement |
| R3.1–R3.2 | `x_bounds(ego, T, lead_x - min_gap, lead_x)` — following corridor |
| R4.1 | `y_bounds(ego, T, V_y ± min_lateral)` — lateral clearance corridor |
| R4.2 | `x_bounds(ego, T, V_x - min_long, V_x)` — longitudinal floor |
| R5.1 | `x_bounds(ego, T, -∞, stopline_x)` — stop line hard bound |
| R5.2 | `v_max(ego, T, sqrt(2*a_max*D))` — dilemma-zone stop feasibility |
| R6.1–R6.5 | `x_bounds` at stop line / yield line / crosswalk |
| R7.2 | `x_bounds` + `y_bounds` for turn path corridor + oncoming TTC |
| R9.1–R9.5 | `x_bounds`/`y_bounds` around crosswalk / bike lane / sidewalk |
| R11.1 | `v_max(ego, T, sqrt(2*max_decel*D_hazard))` — emergency braking feasibility |
| R12.1 | `a_max(ego, T, max_comfort_decel)`, `a_max(ego, T, max_comfort_accel)` |
| R13.2 | `v_max(ego, T, closing_rate * TTC_threshold)` — TTC-derived cap |

These 18 rules cover >80% of observed violations in replay/calibration data (`collision_avoidance`, `lane_keeping`, `uncontrolled_priority`, `longitudinal_clearance`, `lateral_clearance`, `cut_in`, `unsafe_lane_change_gap`, `urban_speed`, `vru_proximity_speed`, `red_light`, `crosswalk_yield`, `emergency_yield`, `excess_accel`, `excess_decel`).

*End of evaluation design.*
==> asp-safety/docs/implementation_plan_p23.md <==
# Implementation Plan: P-23 — Horizon-Joint / Stale-Constraint Solving

> Status: INCREMENT 1 DONE (2026-10-23, S25). Optimizer-side joint
> projection shipped + measured (rate-neutral, diagnosis refined).
> Increment 2 (emission-side, coupled constraints) is the next design
> step. Ledger item: `futuretodoist.md` §5.1 P-23.

## Problem (frozen evidence)

`experiments/qp_ablation.json` (8 unsafe frozen scenes):

- heuristic fixed 0.375 = QP fixed 0.375; caps 0.00, boxes 0.125.
- Scene 8 fails at 3, 5 AND 10 solve rounds: static per-timestep boxes
  whack-a-mole a moving (CV-projected) threat while tail coupling
  cascades new violations at neighboring timesteps.

Root-cause read of the current code:

- `src/constraint_optimizer.py` `solve()` does ONE sequential projection
  pass per iteration: `work += lr*(orig-work)` -> clip boxes ->
  project speeds -> `_clamp_accel` -> pin T=0. The accel clamp can push
  points back OUT of their boxes within the same iteration (the three
  constraint sets are not jointly enforced), so the iteration can cycle
  instead of converge when boxes are tight at a moving threat's
  timesteps.
- `CorrectorQP.correct()` re-derives bounds per outer round
  (`c2` from re-check) — that part already exists; adding rounds does
  not help because the inner solve itself does not reach joint
  feasibility (evidence: fails at 10 rounds).
- A7 (stale-constraint cache) is a latency lever, not a fix-rate lever;
  revisit only after the solve converges.

## Options considered

| Option | Mechanism | Evidence verdict |
|---|---|---|
| A. More rounds | raise `max_rounds` | RULED OUT — scene 8 fails at 3/5/10 |
| B. Joint projection loop inside `solve()` | iterate projections to JOINT feasibility (box ∩ speed ∩ accel), e.g. POCS-style alternating projections with a convergence check, bounded iterations | Primary candidate: attacks the measured root cause directly, pure NumPy, no new deps |
| C. Agent-motion rollouts in bound derivation | re-derive per-timestep boxes from the same CV rollout the rules use, jointly with the solve | Partially exists (per-round `c2` re-derivation); keep as the constraint-side companion to B |
| D. Receding-horizon per-timestep replanning | decompose solve per timestep with lookahead | Out of scope (larger contract change); escalate only if B fails |

## Design (B + C)

1. `ConstraintOptimizer.solve()`: after the sequential projection pass,
   add an inner alternation loop (bounded, e.g. `joint_iters`, default
   3-5): repeat `clip_boxes -> project_speeds -> clamp_accel` until the
   point satisfies ALL constraint families simultaneously (max
   violation < `feas_tol_cm`) or the inner cap is hit; report
   `status='optimal'` vs `'projection_cycle'` (honest: surface
   non-convergence instead of silently returning infeasible).
2. Keep `CorrectorQP`'s per-round re-derivation (C) unchanged; the
   re-check now sees a jointly feasible candidate, so rounds converge
   instead of chasing cascades.
3. Params via `DEFAULT_OPT_PARAMS` additions (`joint_iters`,
   `feas_tol_cm`) — no CLI/contract changes; `bounds` family filter
   untouched.
4. `on_failure='brake'` semantics and the heuristic-fallback contract
   stay exactly as-is; heuristic path is NOT modified.

## Acceptance criteria

- Scene 8 (and the moving-threat class generally) fixed at
  `max_rounds=3`; dump-level QP fixed rate > 0.375 with NO regression
  on the other 7 unsafe scenes (ablate_corrector.py re-run).
- Pure-QP latency stays bounded (POCS inner cap); mean solve ms within
  the existing QP envelope (<= ~2700 ms mean incl. rounds).
- Warm A/B protocols re-run and re-ledgered: `run_calibration.py
  --corrector qp` (0.50 baseline) and `replay_eval.py --corrector qp`
  (0.50 baseline, same scene sets).
- New regression test for the cycling class (tight moving box + accel
  coupling that cycles single-pass projection) in
  `tests/test_qp_feasibility.py`; suite green 120 -> 121+; no test
  weakened.
- If B does not move scene 8: honest negative result ledgered, C/D
  re-scoped (repo culture: measured-or-negative, never hidden).

## Files

- `src/constraint_optimizer.py` (primary), `src/corrector.py`
  (`CorrectorQP` — only if status plumbing needs it), tests:
  `tests/test_qp_feasibility.py`, `tests/test_constraint_emission.py`
  (only if emission touches), A/B: `experiments/ablate_corrector.py`,
  `experiments/qp_ablation.json` (regenerated, old archived).

## Risks

- POCS may converge slowly on adversarial boxes -> bounded inner cap
  + honest `projection_cycle` status; fallback path unchanged.
- Fix-rate gain may be partial (0.375 -> 0.5) — acceptable if
  measured; the claim in the paper moves only as far as the evidence.
- Latency: joint feasibility checks add per-iteration cost; measure
  against the existing envelope before accepting.

## Increment 1 — SHIPPED + measured (2026-10-23, S25)

Implemented in `src/constraint_optimizer.py`:

- Inner alternation loop (`joint_iters`, default 5) after the gradient
  step; breaks on joint feasibility (`_jointly_feasible`, `feas_tol_cm`).
- Honest status: `'optimal'` when jointly feasible, else
  `'projection_cycle'` (no silent infeasible return); `joint_iters`
  added to the info dict.
- Removed the pre-existing unused `List` import (file now flake8-clean
  at `--max-line-length=90`).
- New regression tests (2): the cycle case found by search (old
  single-pass left 9.6 cm violation; new solves to 0.0000 m) and the
  honest-status test. Suite: 120 -> 122 green.

Measured outcome (honest, `experiments/qp_ablation_p23.json`):

- Aggregate rates UNCHANGED: heuristic 0.375 = QP 0.375, caps 0.00,
  boxes 0.125.
- Pure-QP (no fallback) on the 8 unsafe dump scenes: 1/8 before =
  1/8 after (scene 7 only; scenes 0,1,2,3,5,6,8 still raise after 3
  rounds).
- Instrumented scene 8: all 3 rounds end `status=optimal` — the solve
  IS jointly feasible against the emitted bounds, yet the checker
  re-validation still fails. **Diagnosis: the ceiling is
  constraint-emission coverage, not projection convergence.** The
  per-timestep boxes cannot express the coupled corridor a moving
  threat needs; fixing one timestep's bound exposes a new rule
  violation at a neighbor (tail coupling), and 10 rounds did not help
  either (S15 evidence).
- Latency: per-scene solve ms unchanged (old 338-528 vs new 343-540,
  same conditions); aggregate ablation ms is machine-noise-dominated
  (heuristic path untouched by this change).

## Increment 2 — moving box projection (SHIPPED 2026-10-23, S26)

- Implemented in `src/constraint_optimizer.py`:
  - `_index_box` detects consecutive y_bounds with linearly moving
    centers (constant velocity) as "moving boxes".
  - `_project_moving_boxes` projects the entire segment as a rigid
    body + lateral offset to stay within the moving box corridor.
    This couples the independent per-timestep projections and
    prevents the accel-clamp vs box whack-a-mole on moving threats.
  - Suite: 120 -> 122 green; flake8-clean.
- Measured outcome (honest, `experiments/qp_ablation_p23_inc2.json`):
  - Aggregate rates UNCHANGED: heuristic 0.375 = QP 0.375, caps 0.00,
    boxes 0.00 (boxes family lost some fixes due to projection change).
  - Pure-QP (no fallback) on 8 unsafe dump scenes: 1/8 at 3 rounds,
    **2/8 at 10 rounds** (scenes 0 and 7 fixed) — improvement at
    higher round counts.
  - **Diagnosis refined**: the moving box projection helps at higher
    round counts by allowing the solver to track the moving threat's
    corridor. However, the fundamental ceiling remains the emission
    side: accumulated bounds become jointly infeasible (scene 8
    hits `projection_cycle` at round 2+ with accumulation). The
    fundamental ceiling is still constraint-emission coverage.
- Next increment: coupled range emission (`constraint_y_bounds(t0,t1,lo,hi)`)
  in `rules/*.lp` + `src/checker.py` `_group_constraints` to emit
  range/corridor constraints that the optimizer can project as a
  single coupled unit.

## Increment 3 — range constraint emission (SHIPPED 2026-10-23, S27)

- Implemented in `rules/collision_avoidance.lp`:
  - Added `constraint_y_bounds_range/5` emission for lateral corridors
    spanning consecutive overlap timesteps (using `#min`/`#max` aggregates).
  - Added `#show constraint_y_bounds_range/5` to `helpers.lp`.
- Implemented in `src/checker.py`:
  - `_group_constraints` now parses `constraint_y_bounds_range/5` into
    `y_bounds_range` entries `(t0, t1, min, max)`.
- Implemented in `src/constraint_optimizer.py`:
  - Added `_index_range` to index range constraints per timestep.
  - Added `_project_ranges` to project trajectory onto coupled range
    corridors (single lateral/longitudinal offset across the window).
  - Updated `_jointly_feasible` and joint projection loop to include ranges.
  - Suite: 122 green; flake8-clean.
- Measured outcome (honest, `experiments/qp_ablation_p23_final.json`):
  - Aggregate rates UNCHANGED: heuristic 0.375 = QP 0.375, caps 0.00,
    boxes 0.00.
  - Pure-QP (no fallback) on 8 unsafe scenes: **0/8 at 3 rounds, 2/8 at 10 rounds**
    (scenes 0 and 7 fixed at 10r) — **regression at 3 rounds** (scene 7
    was previously fixed at 3r, now requires 10r with range projection).
  - **Diagnosis**: range constraints help at higher rounds by tracking
    the moving corridor, but they interfere with the 3-round fix for
    scene 7. The fundamental ceiling remains **constraint-emission
    coverage**: the per-timestep + range bounds are still jointly
    infeasible for the moving-threat class (accumulated bounds hit
    `projection_cycle`). The fundamental fix requires emission-side
    coupled constraints that express the corridor as a single entity.
- Next increment: coupled range emission directly in rules (already
  shipped); next design step is to refine the range projection or
  emit corridor constraints from the rule side instead of post-hoc.

==> asp-safety/docs/replay_eval_design.md <==
# Replay Evaluation Design (P-11)

Open-loop flagging is not safety evidence. This doc designs the two
harnesses that get us there. **Section A (annotation replay) is
implemented** in `experiments/replay_eval.py` with
`experiments/replay_baseline.json`; **Section B (CARLA closed-loop) is
implemented** (P-25, 2026-10-22 bridge + scenarios + frozen 15-run
pilot matrix; deviations ledger `docs/carla_harness.md`).

## A. nuScenes annotation replay (primary, ~1 week)

Replace single-frame detections with TRACKED agents from
`sample_annotation` records, and score the layer over full scenes:

1. Per scene, walk samples via `sample['next']` from the first token.
2. At each sample, read `sample['anns']` -> annotation records
   (`translation`, `rotation`, `size`, `instance_token`,
   `category_name`). Keep `vehicle.*` + `human.*` categories.
3. Group by `instance_token` across samples -> per-agent position
   series in global frame; transform to the eval sample's ego frame
   (same `_pose_to_mat` math as the loader).
4. Eval frame = middle sample (matches calibration): ego plan =
   planner prediction (cold or N-2 warm), agents = tracked series
   resampled to STEP_DT, map = N-1 extraction, GT = log ego.
5. Metrics per scene: findings on pred vs findings on GT (FP proxy),
   fix rate, timing. Aggregate exactly like `run_calibration.py`
   (reuse its summary schema + `horizon_s` stamps).

Why this beats the current setup: CV-projected single-frame
detections hallucinate motion (parked cars drift, FPs persist one
frame); tracked annotations give true agent histories, so R3/R4/R13
verdicts become evidence instead of noise. Parked-vs-live falls out
of the series variance (no box classes needed).

Cost estimate: annotation walk is devkit-only (no GPU); per-scene
overhead is one extra sample sweep (~10 samples x 6 cameras is NOT
needed -- annotations come from records, no images).

## B. CARLA closed-loop (implemented 2026-10-22, P-25)

Feasibility verdict (probed 2026-09-26, zero changes made): runnable.
No admin rights (prebuilt package needs none), RTX 4050 Laptop 6 GB
(exactly the stated minimum; 8 GB recommended), Ryzen 5 6600H,
16 GB RAM, 76 GB free (packaged server ~= 20 GB; source build at
165 GB+ ruled out), VCRedist x64 present, ports 2000/2001 free,
conda Python 3.10 (cp310 wheels exist).

Installed state: 0.9.15 stable at `C:\CARLA` (outside repo),
client in env, default map Town10HD_Opt smoke-verified
(`SMOKE OK`, planner-resolution frame). Expected rates: server
render ~= 10-20 FPS (6-camera rig); closed-loop wall rate
~= 0.3-0.4 steps/s (planner-bound), so the bridge replans
every 40th tick under synchronous fixed-timestep (deterministic
sim-time; replan wall is sim-time-frozen).

Implemented harness:
1. `experiments/carla_bridge.py`: 6-camera rig -> planner inputs
   (or the lane-plan analyzer behind `--plan-engine lane`), ASP
   checker verdict -> heuristic/qp/shield-off dispatch, plan
   execution by a pure-pursuit speed controller, run JSON per run.
2. `experiments/carla_scenarios.py`: S1 free drive, S2 red-light
   run (cluster pinned red), S3 cut-in, S4 jaywalker, S5 oncoming
   left turn; deterministic spawn, cleanup resets all map lights.
3. `experiments/carla_metrics.py`: frozen aggregate
   (`experiments/carla_metrics.json`, 15 runs).

Measured pilot numbers (seed 1, Town10HD_Opt, plan-engine lane):

| Run | km | status | collisions | shield verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 off / heuristic / qp | 0.60 x3 | distance_reached | 0 | no hazard on clean track; forcings all pass-through |
| S2 off | 0.60 | distance_reached | 0 | runs the red junction (81 red_light findings) |
| S2 heuristic / qp | ~0 | wall_cap (holds) | 0 | holds at the red box edge, give-up 0.0 |
| S3 off | 0.03 | stalled+collided (rear-ends cut-in) | 1 | NA |
| S3 heuristic / qp | 0.03 | wall_cap (follows at 4 m/s) | 0 | survives; emergency-hold brake fallback |
| S4 off | 0.60 | distance_reached | 0* | kinematic walker produces no collision event |
| S4 heuristic / qp | 0.47/0.52 | wall_cap | 0 | waits out the jaywalker |
| S5 off | 0.03 | stalled+collided (turn conflict) | 1 | NA |
| S5 heuristic / qp | ~0.02 | wall_cap | 0 | holds; emergency-hold fallback |

Aggregate (frozen `experiments/carla_metrics.json`): collision rate
13.3% (2/15, both off arms), frame drops 0, give-up 0 across all
frozen arms, intervention rate ~0.52 (dominated by hazard replans on
the hazard-heavy closed ends). Caveats: VRUs/NPCs are kinematic (no
physical impulses); evidence limited to seed 1 for the pilot; Lane
plan engine (not the OOD neural plan) drives EGO relative to the
CARLA imagery — neural plans remain logged in the run JSONs.

## C. Measured numbers for the report (warm official baseline, 10 mini scenes)

Source of truth: `experiments/calibration_baseline.json` (warm,
`horizon_s=3`, tracked replay variants in `replay_baseline.json`).

| Metric | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Planner L2 (cold / warm) | 11.54 m / 10.18 m | replay / calibration |
| GT safe rate | 0.30 (residuals ledgered) | calibration_baseline.json |
| Pred safe rate | 0.20 | same |
| Pred fixed rate | 0.50 (agent-free 0.90 on the earlier cold run) | same |
| Planner / checker / corrector ms | ~13026 / ~60 / ~2562 | same (loaded laptop) |
| 20-agent check (synthetic) | 37 ms min | test_performance |
| Catalog coverage | 73/73 findings fire | test_rule_registry |
| Nav-command conditioning | weak (no mappable semantics) | cmd_sweep_results.md |
| Horizon | 3 s verified shield, blind beyond | N-3(a); extrapolation dropped |
| Corrector A/B (mini, unsafe=8) | heuristic 0.375 = QP 0.375 | qp_ablation.json |
| Bound-family ablation | full 0.375 > boxes 0.125 > caps 0.0 | qp_ablation.json |
| Warm GPU A/B (calibration) | fixed 0.50 = 0.50 | calibration_qp.json |
| Warm tracked-replay A/B | fixed 0.50 = 0.50, same scene sets (5 fixed / 5 give-ups) | replay_warm_heuristic.json / replay_warm_qp.json |

`paper/report.tex` now reflects the 63-rule catalog and these measured
numbers; the numbers are mini-split evidence, not a safety claim.

==> asp-safety/docs/repository_deep_dive.md <==
# EDI / ASP Safety Layer — Comprehensive Repository Analysis & Documentation Deep-Dive

## Executive Summary

The **ASP Safety Layer for Autonomous Vehicles** is a neuro-symbolic safety shield designed to supervise and correct end-to-end neural trajectory planners (specifically **SparseDrive**, ICRA 2025 / ECCV 2026). The system takes real-world multimodal sensor observations, predicts trajectories via a neural network, translates candidate trajectories and environmental contexts into declarative Answer Set Programming (ASP) facts, evaluates them against a formal **63-rule traffic catalog** in **Clingo**, and applies either a heuristic fixpoint or continuous Quadratic Program (QP) / projected-gradient correction to eliminate rule violations before trajectory execution.

---

## 1. Complete Repository Architecture & File Taxonomy

├── opencode.json                               # Tool permissions & agent configurations

## 2. In-Depth Analysis of Key Markdown Documents

### A. Core Blueprints & Rules
- [AGENTS.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/AGENTS.md):
  The definitive project roadmap outlining 11 subdivisions from environment setup to paper submission. Note: Subdivision 3 (initial float-based prototype) was explicitly superseded by Subdivision 11, `asp_traffic_rules.md`, and `docs/conventions.md`.
- [asp_traffic_rules.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp_traffic_rules.md):
  A 1,307-line formal catalog detailing 63 traffic rules across 15 distinct legal/safety categories: Speed (R1), Lanes (R2), Following (R3), Collisions (R4), Signals (R5), Priority (R6), Turning (R7), Overtaking (R8), VRUs (R9), Intersections (R10), Emergencies (R11), Comfort (R12), Prediction (R13), Environment (R14), and Complex Multi-Agent interactions (R15).

### B. Engineering Backlog & Session Logs
- [whatodo.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/whatodo.md):
  The live engineering workboard tracking day-to-day delivery. Documents the completion of Month 1 (Weeks 1 to 4) and the October 2026 ASP constraint emission and continuous QP optimization phases.
- [session.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/session.md):
  A granular 768-line chronological log documenting sessions S0 through S17. Detailed milestones include pure-PyTorch fallbacks for non-MSVC Windows environments, CUDA 2.4s inference on RTX 4050, nuScenes map extraction, warm temporal queue caching, CARLA 0.9.15 installation, and QP bound-family ablations.
- [futuretodoist.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/futuretodoist.md):
  The unified historical backlog cross-referencing task IDs (T-2 to T-13, P-1 to P-20, N-1 to N-4, PR-1 to PR-3) with their completion status and technical artifacts.

### C. Technical Contracts & Reference Documents (`docs/`)
- [conventions.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/conventions.md):
  Enforces coordinate system rules: ego-frame, positions in meters in Python, **integer centimeters (x100)** in ASP. Strict integer arithmetic (no floats, no `sqrt`); speeds in ASP are **squared speeds** in $(\text{cm}/\text{step})^2$.
- [data_contracts.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/data_contracts.md):
  Master producer-consumer contract table detailing tensors, shapes, types, and schemas at every stage of the pipeline (from camera images `(6, 3, 256, 704)` to verified trajectory points `(4, 2)`).
- [sparsedrive_integration.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/sparsedrive_integration.md):
  Details the exact boundary between the neural model and ASP shield: projection matrices ($K \cdot [R|t]$), tensor normalization, 10-dimensional bounding box slicing, synthetic k-means anchors, and temporal state isolation.
- [rule_divergences.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/rule_divergences.md):
  Itemizes 52 specific points of divergence between theoretical law specifications and real-world ASP implementations (e.g., int32 overflow protection in R3.4/R15.4, one-sided hold-back boxes for following vehicles, sign-split absolute values).
- [evaluation_design_asp_correction.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/evaluation_design_asp_correction.md):
  The experimental evaluation plan comparing SparseDrive (B0), the heuristic dispatch corrector (B1), and the ASP-constrained QP corrector (B2) across ablations A1–A7 on nuScenes tracked annotations and CARLA.
- [replay_eval_design.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/replay_eval_design.md):
  Architectural design for annotation replay over 184 tracked agents, isolating parked vs live vehicles through trajectory variance without requiring perception semantic classification.
- [test_coverage.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/docs/test_coverage.md):
  Verifies that all 73 distinct violation, hazard, and warning heads across the 63-rule catalog fire on demand in unit tests.

### D. Literature & Research Benchmarking
- [literature.md](file:///c:/Users/chand/OneDrive/Desktop/EDI/asp-safety/paper/literature.md):
  Exhaustive review of 12 core academic foundations: SparseDriveV2 (ECCV 2026), MomAD (CVPR 2025), NAVSIM (NeurIPS 2024), Bench2Drive (NeurIPS 2024), nuScenes (CVPR 2020), Althoff et al. (Proc. IEEE 2025), SMT trajectory repair (Lin et al. 2025), neuro-symbolic LLM+ASP planning (Wei & AbdAlmageed 2026), conformal perception bounds (NeuS 2025), Road2Code (NeuS 2025), and Clingo multi-shot semantics (Gebser et al., TPLP 2019).

## 3. Measured Experimental Baselines & Key Findings

| Experiment Track | Key Finding / Metric | Empirical Evidence Source |
|---|---|---|
| **Temporal Inference (N-2)** | Warm multi-frame queues beat cold inference in 10/10 scenes: L2 displacement drops from **11.54 m to 10.18 m** | `experiments/temporal_results.md` |
| **Trajectory Extrapolation (N-3b)** | Extrapolating 3s plans to 6s with constant velocity adds +20 false violations and increases corrector latency 6x; **DROPPED** in favor of honest 3s horizon | `experiments/extrapolation_verdict.md` |
| **Detection Score Sweep (P-5)** | Detection confidence knee established at **0.5**; halves false collisions without losing true safety signals | `experiments/sweep_verdict.md` |
| **Nav-Command Conditioning (P-13)** | Neural planner exhibits weak steer-command conditioning under single-frame evaluation | `experiments/cmd_sweep_results.md` |
| **Parked vs Live Vehicles (S10)** | Tracking agent series over time explains **~60% of collision violations** as parked vehicles; solved without neural box classes | `experiments/replay_baseline.json` |
| **ASP-Constrained QP Optimizer (S14-S17)** | Pure QP solves lane violations **10x faster** (228ms vs 2872ms); joint bound solving (`full` 0.38) outperforms isolated bound subsets (`caps` 0.00, `boxes` 0.12) | `experiments/qp_ablation.json` |
| **Official Benchmark Baseline** | Ground-Truth Safe: **0.30** / Raw Pred Safe: **0.20** / Post-Shield Fixed: **0.50** / L2 Error: **10.18 m** | `experiments/calibration_baseline.json` |

## Policy for new rules

Every new `violation/`/`hazard/` head needs a firing test or
`test_rule_registry` fails the suite.
