|---|---|---|
| 37 | Centered threat boxes attract closest-point optimizers; one-sided hold-back gated on signed side (R4.2 gap sign, R3.2/R15.2 X2>=X) | WIRE (fix), tested (exact tuples + absence) |
| 38 | Static per-timestep boxes cannot coordinate a tail against a moving threat (scene-8 whack-a-mole at 3/5/10 rounds) -- needs horizon-joint solving, not more rounds | KEEP (limitation; max_rounds stays 3) |
| 39 | Optimizer knobs flat across 12 combos on frozen data; defaults kept per sweep precedent | KEEP (evidence `experiments/qp_sweep.json`) |

==> asp-safety/docs/sparsedrive_integration.md <==
# SparseDrive Integration — Boundary Only

> Deep internals (detector/map/motion decoders, CUDA kernels) out of scope.
> This doc covers exactly what `asp-safety` asks for and what it consumes.

## 1. How the wrapper reaches SparseDrive

`SparseDrivePlanner._load_model` (`src/neural_planner.py`): inserts
`<repo>/SparseDrive` on `sys.path` → `build_detector` from the mmdet
plugin registry (mmdet, NOT mmdet3d — mmcv version conflict) → config
`projects/configs/sparsedrive_small_stage2.py` via importlib →
`build_detector(cfg.model)` → `torch.load(path, weights_only=True)`
with `strict=False` (`{state_dict}`-or-raw) → `.to(device).eval()`
with CPU fp16 wrappers disabled. Config: `model.type="SparseDrive"`,
head `SparseDriveHead`, `task_config={det,map,motion_plan}`,
`ego_fut_ts=6/mode=6`, `fut_ts=12`, `queue_length=4`,
`input_shape=(704,256)`.
Missing file or load exception → placeholder (`use_placeholder=True`);
`ImportError` → `RuntimeError` with setup hints. Synthetic
checkpoint-exact anchors under `SparseDrive/data/kmeans/*.py`
(overwritten by `load_state_dict`); no-compile fallbacks for the
CUDA-only `deformable_aggregation` op (pure-torch, verified 8.3e-07)
and `flash_attn` (torch SDPA path).

## 2. What goes in

`predict(images, intrinsics, extrinsics, ego2global, timestamp,
nav_cmd=None, ego_status=None)`: `images (6,3,256,704)` order
FRONT/FL/FR/BACK/BL/BR (shape-validated; uint8 or float32).
`_run_inference` builds (batched): `img (1,6,3,256,704)`
nuScenes-normalized (mean `[123.675,116.28,103.53]`, std
`[58.395,57.12,57.375]`); `projection_mat (1,6,4,4)` as K@[R|t]
embedded in 4x4 (RQ-2 fix — the old `(4,4)@(3,3)` form was a shape
crash); `image_wh=[[704,256]]×6`; `ego_status` zeros unless supplied
(cold inference ignores it); `gt_ego_fut_cmd=[1,0,0]` unless
`nav_cmd` given (head-selection semantics UNVERIFIED — see
`experiments/cmd_sweep_results.md`); `timestamp`. Model called as
`model(**inputs)` under `no_grad`.

Temporal state: the banks need per-frame `img_metas` (`T_global`)
that single-frame callers don't supply, so a warm bank crashes the
second call (`KeyError: 'img_metas'`). `predict()` therefore
cold-resets every call. `predict_frame()` (between
`begin_sequence()`/`end_sequence()`) supplies metas + per-frame
`ego_status` instead — the N-2 warm path. Never mix state across
scenes. `ego2global` IS the lidar-frame `T_global`.

## 3. What comes out (and what is actually used)

`_postprocess` reads `raw_output[0]['img_bbox']`, fail-loud
(`KeyError`/`ValueError`, never placeholder fallback):

| Key | Becomes | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| `final_planning` | `trajectory (6,2)` ego meters, t=0.5..3.0 s | raise |
| `boxes_3d` (N,10 `[x,y,z,w,l,h,yaw,vx,vy,vz]`) | `boxes (N,7)` + `velocities (N,3)` | `zeros((0,7))` / `zeros((0,3))` |
| `scores_3d` | `scores (N,)` (length must match boxes) | `zeros((0,))` |

Map vectors, motion forecasts, mode scores are ignored. Pinned by
the T-5 golden test (incl. sliced velocities).

## 4. Placeholder (offline stand-in)

`_generate_placeholder`: `seed(42)`; 5 m/s × 0.5 s cruise with
slight lateral drift; boxes `[20,0,.5,2,4.5,1.5,0]`,
`[40,5,.5,2,4.5,1.5,0]` (static, zero velocities); scores
`[.95,.87]`. Deterministic (pinned).

## 5. Canonical launch (upstream, for reference)

`SparseDrive/tools/test.py`: `Config.fromfile` → plugin import →
`build_detector` → `load_checkpoint` → dataloader + `single_gpu_test` →
`dataset.evaluate`. The wrapper shortcuts all of it (direct build +
`torch.load`, no Config/dataloader/`load_checkpoint`) — **VERIFIED**.

## 6. What each side knows

- SparseDrive knows **nothing** about ASP (no import, no predicate).
- ASP layer knows **nothing** about SparseDrive (only the `(6,2)`/`(N,7)`
  arrays plus calibration passthrough).
- Interface = `predict()` return dict. Change `final_planning` shape/keys
  and `_postprocess` breaks; change trajectory `(6,2)` and checker
  validation rejects it.
- Horizon fact: the head emits 6×0.5 s = 3 s (`plan_reg_branch`
  output dim 12 is baked in; longer horizons need retraining —
  N-3(c) verdict). The checker contract is 6×1.0 s; both the
  calibration harness and `pipeline.run()` resample with a T=0
  anchor and stamp `horizon_s` (unified — previously `run()` checked
  native and read speeds halved).

==> asp-safety/docs/test_coverage.md <==
# Test Coverage — ASP Safety Layer

Generated 2026-09-26 from `experiments/coverage_probe.py` (73/73),
refreshed 2026-10-21 (test-count refresh, S13-S17 suites).
The P-9 registry test (`test_rule_registry`) fails the suite if any
catalog `violation/`/`hazard`/`warning` head lacks a quoted firing
assertion under `tests/`.

## Headline

- 120 tests green (collected 2026-10-21): 104 fast (Clingo, no GPU)
  + 8 sparse_drive + 7 pipeline + 1 `test_all_components()` (RQ-6
  pytest entry). S13-S17 contributed 26: `test_constraint_emission.py`
  (12), `test_qp_feasibility.py` (13, incl. bound-family + one-sided
  pins), `test_pipeline_corrector_modes` (1, in pipeline file).
- 73/73 catalog findings fire on demand; every family has firing +
  quiet pairs except where noted.
- Solver-internal atoms (`ttc_band`, `threat_level`,
  `comfort_score`, `future_violation`, `any_violation`) are covered
  via `check(include_internal=True)` in `test_t2_coverage.py`.
- New S13-S17 coverage: constraint emission (head-coverage
  self-check, firing+emission per family, exemption pin), QP exact
  projections (v_max/a_max/box/spring), safe passthrough, speed +
  red end-to-end re-validation through real Clingo, bound-family
  caps/boxes routing, one-sided hold-back exact tuples.

## Per-family status

| Family | Firing tests | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| R1 speed | urban/hwy/zone/min/VRU/default | min-band feasibility assumed |
| R2 lanes | keeping/transition, illegal, gap, wrong-way, HOV, departure warning | transition exception tested |
| R3 following | time floor, tailgating, queue oscillation | R3.4 overflow fixed |
| R4 collision | lateral/longitudinal, bubble, cut-in, rear-end, sideswipe | R4.2 now along-track |
| R5 signals | red, yellow zone, green+obstacle, flashing red/yellow, arrow | RTOR 3 gates |
| R6 priority | uncontrolled (+tie/clear), roundabout, turn yield, emergency, crosswalk | R6.2 needed conflict-zone fix |
| R7 turns | RTOR x3, left-across, U-turn (sign + sightline), signal, radius | radius needs `min_turning_radius` extra |
| R8 overtake | solid, oncoming, zone, sightline, right-side, clearance | `overtaken_type` via extras |
| R9 VRU | unmarked corner, school guard, bike lane, blind spot, sidewalk | blind-spot stale clause via extras |
| R10 intersection | queue cut, box blocking, protected lane, right-turn ped | queue facts via extras |
| R11 emergency | brake feasibility, evasion bounds, guardrail, construction x2 | evasive/hazard via extras (no generator yet) |
| R12 comfort | accel/decel, lat accel/jerk, reversal, score | advisory-only score via internal atoms |
| R13 predictive | collisionecast, 3 TTC bands, arbitration cascade, horizon agg | cascade pinned single-label |
| R14 environment | wet, headlights, visibility (+weather variants) | caps via weather dict |
| R15 complex | platoon x2, merge, wave, multiway | merge order is a labeled heuristic |
| Checker | shape/cull/warnings/times/determinism/aliasing/duplicates/conflict-cull | edges file |
| Corrector | converge/no-op/raise/advisory/idempotent/min-boost/brake/cap/tail/dual-id/stagnation | 10 tests |
| Constraints | contract, head-coverage self-check, R1-R5/R6.5/R12/R13 firing+emission, exemption pin | emission file (S13) |
| QP corrector | exact v_max/a_max/box/spring projections, fail-loud, safe passthrough, speed+red re-validation, bounds families (full/caps/boxes), one-sided hold-back | test_qp_feasibility.py (S14-S17) |
| Loader | placeholder schema/determinism/hostile-9/reachable-red | real path via calibration |
| Metrics | formulas + yaw/validation/FDR/keys + real outputs | T-10 |
| Planner | shapes/golden/fail-loud/STEP_DT/determinism/temporal/status | GPU paths via harnesses |
| Pipeline | run/batch/red-E2E/detections/errors/raise/timing/modes | real inference (slow); `corrector_mode` pin S15 |
| Perf | bare <50 ms (best-of-5 min, two-tier breach retry; runs FIRST via tests/conftest.py cold-machine ordering) | 3 hardenings; full-suite flake fixed 2026-10-21 |

## Known gaps (not coverage debt)

- `evasive_path` has no generator (corrector holds instead) — P-6
  convergence guards shipped; a full generator remains future work
  (`evasive_path`/`hazard_distance` stay manual-extras, tested).
- Parked-vs-live distinction — answered without box classes (S10:
  tracked-series variance explains ~60% of collision hits on both
  GT and pred sides).
- Real-scene perf headroom — tile caps shipped (S10, real-scene
  checks ~100 ms); corrector latency under heavy agent load stays
  open (multi-shot deferred with reasons, S7/S8).
- Horizon-joint QP solving — open as P-23 (static per-timestep
  boxes whack-a-mole moving threats at high rounds, scene-8 class).

==> asp-safety/docs/testdoc.md <==
# Test Documentation — ASP Safety Layer

> All commands run from `asp-safety/` with the environment active.
> CPU-only; no GPU or dataset needed. All tests use the placeholder
> planner/loader path unless stated otherwise.

```powershell
conda activate asp-safety
cd <path-to>\asp-safety
```

---

## 1. tests/test_all.py — 7-block smoke suite

> Pytest entry: `def test_all_components():` runs the same 7 checks
> under `pytest tests/` (RQ-6, 2026-10-21); the file is
> CWD-independent (paths derive from the tests-dir parent).

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_all.py
```

**What we are doing:** One pass over every component: planner predict shape,
checker slow=safe, corrector fast→safe, loader sample shape, `l2 == 1/3`,
pipeline `run` keys, full 63-rule catalog grounding via `basic.lp`.

**What it demonstrates:** The pipeline is plumbed end to end
(predict → check → correct → batch → ground). A pass here does not prove
rule semantics — only that nothing is unwired.

---

## 2. tests/test_checker.py — safety discrimination + vacuous guard

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_checker.py
```

**What we are doing:**
- Slow straight trajectory `[0..5]` must return safe with zero violations.
- Fast trajectory `[0..100]` must return unsafe with a `speed_limit` finding.
- Vacuous guard: fast must yield ≥1 finding; overlapping pair must yield
  `collision_avoidance`; red-light geometry must yield `red_light`.
- Correction smoke via `get_safe_trajectory`.

**What it demonstrates:** The checker discriminates safe from unsafe on three
canonical hazards (speeding, overlap, red run) through real Clingo, and cannot
pass while silently reporting everything safe.

---

## 3. tests/test_corrector.py — repair convergence

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_corrector.py
```

**What we are doing:**
- Fast trajectory through `corrector.correct()` must re-`check` safe with
  `corrections[0] == speed_limit`.
- Safe trajectory must return zero corrections and an equal array.
- Multi-violation input (speed + nearby agent) must produce corrections.
- Uncorrectable input raises `RuntimeError` (or flagged hold with
  `on_failure='brake'`); advisory findings pass through untouched;
  correction is idempotent; min-boost converges without oscillation;
  group caps + stagnation break bound churn.

**What it demonstrates:** The fixpoint loop converges on the canonical speed
case and is a no-op when already safe, with honest failure modes and
P-6 convergence guards pinned.

---

## 4. tests/test_data_loader.py — placeholder contracts

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_data_loader.py
```

**What we are doing:** Init on a nonexistent path (placeholder mode), then
assert sample keys and shapes (`images (6,3,256,704)`, `intrinsics (6,3,3)`,
`extrinsics (6,4,4)`, `ego2global (4,4)`, float timestamp), trajectory shape
and dtype, map keys (`lanes`, `intersections`), lights key (`int_0.color`),
and `IndexError` on out-of-range scene index.

**What it demonstrates:** Downstream code receives the exact shapes the
planner and checker validate against. Scene 9 is hostile by design
(obstacle + reachable red) with fix-verified tests; the real nuScenes
branch is covered by calibration, not here.

---

## 5. tests/test_evaluation.py — metric formulas

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_evaluation.py
```

**What we are doing:** Hand-made arrays through `MetricsCalculator`:
collision 100% (box over path) vs 0% (distant box), `l2 == 1/3`, detection
rate 1.0, false-positive rate 1.0 on empty truth, average time 20.0 ms.

**What it demonstrates:** Metric arithmetic is correct, including
yaw-aware footprints, batch-length validation, NaN guards, FDR naming,
and key-aware matching. Real checker/planner outputs feed the T-10
test; pure formula cases never touch Clingo.

---

## 6. tests/test_sparse_drive.py — planner interface shape

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_sparse_drive.py
```

**What we are doing:** Init on the checkpoint path (falls back to placeholder),
`predict` must return `trajectory (6,2)`, `boxes (N,7)`, `scores (N,)`,
`velocities (N,3)`; a `(3,3,256,704)` input must raise `ValueError`.
Golden `_postprocess` values (incl. sliced velocities), fail-loud
matrix, RQ-2 projection math, STEP_DT pin, placeholder + temporal-API
determinism, and `estimate_ego_status` layout are pinned.

**What it demonstrates:** The neural↔symbolic interface contract (shapes and
keys). Real-model inference runs on the GPU build (planner/temporal
harnesses); the golden path keeps the schema honest without one.

---

## 7. tests/test_pipeline.py — end-to-end (most valuable)

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_pipeline.py
```

**What we are doing:**
- Init on CPU, `run` on synthetic images; assert result keys
  (`original_trajectory` (6,2) native, `safe_trajectory` (4,2)
  checked grid, `horizon_s`, `violations`, `is_safe`,
  `inference_time_ms`, `timing_ms` split, `boxes`, `scores`),
  boolean verdict, positive latency.
- Detections-to-trajectories unit test (score filter, cap, CV
  projection + `dt`; no model). Batch error-schema + corrector-raise
  propagation (T-11, placeholder-fast).
- `run_batch` on 3 placeholder scenes; assert length and `scene_idx` keys.
- Planted red-light run: lane built from the planner's own CHECKED
  waypoints (T=0..3 grid), red on T=3; assert `is_safe is False`
  with `red_light` fired, then re-`check` the returned
  `safe_trajectory` and assert it is clean.

**What it demonstrates:** The full loop — neural proposal, ASP veto, heuristic
repair, verified fix — through real Clingo on placeholder data, with
detections feeding agent context (P-18) and stable failure contracts.

---

## 8. tests/test_rules.py — per-category rule harness (14 functions)

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_rules.py
```

**What we are doing:** Safe/unsafe pairs per rule family through `check`,
plus co-firing priority and correction convergence:
helpers (bare slow safe), R1 speed, R2 lane, R3 following, R4 collision,
R5 red vs green, R6.5/R9 pedestrian, R11 obstacle + evasive handoff
(`insufficient` link pinned), R14 wet/night, R15 platoon + IF-2 wirings
(wave/multiway/merge), T-2 spot probes (6 extra firings), red+pedestrian
priority with corrected-safe check, correction shape+safety.

**What it demonstrates:** Core families fire on demand; the full
battery (turning, overtaking, predictive, intersection, comfort,
flags) lives in `test_t2_coverage.py` (§11) with the P-9 registry
(73/73 findings asserted) as the tripwire.

---

## 9. experiments/run_evaluation.py — offline batch demo

**Command:**

```powershell
python experiments/run_evaluation.py --num-scenes 10
```

**What we are doing:** Builds the CPU pipeline with placeholder planner and
loader, runs `run_batch` over 10 scenes, aggregates safe-rate, violation
breakdown, and average/p95 latency.

**What it demonstrates:** Batch orchestration and reporting on synthetic
scenes (hostile-by-design placeholder geometry fires every scene). Only
`average_inference_time` is wired; collision-rate and L2 improvement on real
data are measured by `run_calibration.py` (§12), not here.

---

## 12. experiments/run_calibration.py — real-data baseline (GPU)

**Command:**

```powershell
python experiments/run_calibration.py --out experiments/calibration_baseline.json --dump-trajs experiments/trajs.json
```

**What we are doing:** Per mini scene (CUDA): warm multi-frame walk
(default; `--cold` reproduces the old single-frame pass), predict,
L2 vs GT on the native grid, GT check (FP proxy) + anchored T=0 pred
check (T=1..3 from indices 1,3,5; `horizon_s` stamped), corrector
fix-rate, planner/checker/corrector timing split. Agent trajectories
come from planner detections (P-18). `--corrector heuristic|qp`
selects the corrector engine (S15; stamps `corrector_mode` in the
JSON summary). `--extrapolate` extends to T=4,5 by constant velocity
(N-3(b): rejected — artifact hits, fix-rate drop). `--dump-trajs`
saves maps+trajectories+boxes for GPU-free `sweep_thresholds.py` runs.

**What it demonstrates:** The honest warm numbers: L2 10.18 m, GT 0.3 /
pred 0.2 / fixed 0.5 (`calibration_baseline.json`, warm by default;
cold was L2 11.54 / GT 0.5 / pred 0.1 / fixed 0.7) with ledgered
residuals (no invented map data). `run_temporal.py` compares
warm-vs-cold (10.18 m warm); `cmd_sweep.py` probes command-head
semantics (weak — unverified mapping).

---

## 10. Quick Reference

| File | Real Clingo? | Real weights? | Proves what |
|------|--------------|---------------|-------------|
| test_all.py | yes (partial) | no | plumbing, not semantics |
| test_checker.py | yes | n/a | safe/unsafe discrimination |
| test_checker_edges.py | yes | n/a | edges: cull, warnings, times, P-4 geometry, aliasing |
| test_corrector.py | yes | n/a | fixpoint converges (+T-8 no-oscillation) |
| test_data_loader.py | no | no | placeholder schema |
| test_evaluation.py | no | n/a | metric formulas |
| test_sparse_drive.py | no (golden only) | no | interface shapes + STEP_DT + N-2 API/status |
| test_pipeline.py | yes | no | proposal→veto→fix loop |
| test_rules.py | yes | n/a | per-category firing |
| test_t2_coverage.py | yes | n/a | full battery firing+quiet + registry 73/73 |
| test_constraint_emission.py | yes | no | rules emit ASP bound atoms |
| test_qp_feasibility.py | yes (re-validate) | no | QP projections + bounds families + hold-back |
| run_evaluation.py | yes (via pipeline) | no | batch reporting |

## 11. tests/test_t2_coverage.py — full battery + registry (16 functions)

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_t2_coverage.py
```

**What we are doing:** Firing + quiet pairs for every family missing
from §8: R1.4/R1.5, R2.2/R2.4/transition, R3.4, R4.1/R4.3-R4.5,
R5.2-R5.5, R6.1-R6.3, R7.1/R7.3-sightline/R7.5, R8.1-R8.4,
R9.1/R9.3-R9.5, R10.1/R10.3/R10.4, R11.4, R12.1-R12.3, R13.1-R13.4
(+R12.4 score via `include_internal`), R14.2/R14.3, plus
`test_rule_registry` (every catalog head asserted somewhere).

**What it demonstrates:** 73/73 catalog findings fire on demand.
Rule-design fixes proven here: R3.4/R15.4 int32 overflows,
R6.2 roundabout conflict zone, pedestrian obstacles, R2.1
transitions, targeted min-boost (corrector).

---

## 13. tests/test_constraint_emission.py — ASP bounds for every rule (12 functions)

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_constraint_emission.py
```

**What we are doing:** With `include_constraints=True`, every rule
family on a violating trajectory emits ≥1 constraint atom (`v_max`/`
v_min`/`x_bounds`/`y_bounds`/`a_max`/`lane_center`): stop/hold boxes
(R5/R6/R7.1/R7.2/R9.1/R9.2/R10/R11.1/R15.1), corridors (R2.1 lane +
pull-back, gap bands R3.2/R15.2), exact caps (R3.1/R3.3, R14.1,
R5.4/R14.3), acceleration caps (R12.1/R3.4/R15.4 minus
`override_active`). Head-coverage self-check across the catalog;
documented exemptions (wrong-way, U-turn, signal timing, turn
radius, bike lane, sidewalk, evasion candidates, fixed obstacles,
TTC bands, threat arbitration, aggregation, parameter layer,
comfort shapes).

**What it demonstrates:** The solver hand-off for the QP corrector:
the ASP layer can express a violation as a continuous bound, not
just a label; constraints never affect the safe/unsafe verdict and
never pollute the violations list.

---

## 14. tests/test_qp_feasibility.py — CorrectorQP + optimizer (13 functions)

**Command:**

```powershell
python tests/test_qp_feasibility.py
```

**What we are doing:** Exact projected-gradient math on emitted
bounds (per-segment `v_max`/`v_min` rescale, `a_max` forward clamp,
tightest-box overlap, T=0 pinned, weak lane-center spring),
fail-loud on bad shape/non-finite input, safe-trajectory
passthrough; end-to-end re-validation: QP speed fix and QP red hold
both re-check clean through real Clingo; `bounds='full'|'caps'|
'boxes'` routing (caps fix pure speed where boxes honestly raise;
boxes-only converges a drift-out back in-lane across rounds);
one-sided hold-back vs centered boxes (exact `(t, -offset, cap)`
tuples, centered boxes absent — the optimizer cannot pull ego toward
the threat).

**What it demonstrates:** The QP path is honest: it never moves
T=0, it re-validates through ASP after updating, and it falls back
to the heuristic with a flagged head entry; the fallback never sees
`include_constraints`/`include_internal` (legacy 2-tuple contract
preserved by a pinned test).

==> asp-safety/docs/usage.md <==
# ASP Safety Layer - Usage Guide

## Quick Start

### 1. Setup Environment

```bash
# Run the setup script
./setup_env.sh

# Activate the environment
conda activate asp-safety
```

### 2. Run Basic Evaluation

```bash
cd asp-safety
python experiments/run_evaluation.py
```

### 3. Run Unit Tests

```bash
cd asp-safety
python tests/test_checker.py
```

## Using the Checker

### Basic Usage

```python
import numpy as np
from src.checker import TrajectoryChecker

# Initialize checker
checker = TrajectoryChecker(rules_dir="rules")

# Create a trajectory
trajectory = np.array([
    [0.0, 0.0],
    [1.0, 0.0],
    [2.0, 0.0],
    [3.0, 0.0],
    [4.0, 0.0],
    [5.0, 0.0],
])

# Check trajectory
is_safe, violations = checker.check(trajectory)

print(f"Is safe: {is_safe}")
print(f"Violations: {violations}")
```

### With Map Data

```python
# Define map data (lane boundaries)
map_data = {
    'lanes': {
        'lane_0': [
            {'x1': -10, 'y1': -2, 'x2': 100, 'y2': -2},
            {'x1': -10, 'y1': 2, 'x2': 100, 'y2': 2}
        ]
    }
}

# Check trajectory with map data
is_safe, violations = checker.check(trajectory, map_data=map_data)
```

### Trajectory Correction

```python
# Get corrected trajectory
corrected = checker.get_safe_trajectory(trajectory)

print(f"Original: {trajectory[-1]}")
print(f"Corrected: {corrected[-1]}")
```

## Project Structure

```
asp-safety/
├── rules/                    # ASP traffic rules (63-rule catalog, R1-R15)
│   ├── helpers.lp           # shared predicates (in_lane, speed, arrival, ...)
│   ├── speed_regulation.lp  # R1.1-R1.5
│   ├── lane_discipline.lp   # R2.1-R2.5
│   ├── following_behavior.lp# R3.1-R3.4
│   ├── collision_avoidance.lp # R4.1-R4.5
│   ├── traffic_control.lp   # R5.1-R5.5
│   ├── right_of_way.lp      # R6.1-R6.5
│   ├── turning_rules.lp     # R7.1-R7.5
│   ├── overtaking.lp        # R8.1-R8.4
│   ├── vru_safety.lp        # R9.1-R9.5
│   ├── intersection_behavior.lp # R10.1-R10.4
│   ├── emergency.lp         # R11.1-R11.4
│   ├── comfort_quality.lp   # R12.1-R12.4
│   ├── predictive_safety.lp # R13.1-R13.4
│   ├── environmental.lp     # R14.1-R14.4
│   ├── complex_interactions.lp # R15.1-R15.4
│   └── basic.lp            # Aggregates all rules
├── src/                      # Source code
│   ├── checker.py           # ASP safety checker
│   ├── corrector.py         # Trajectory fixpoint engine
│   ├── correction_strategies.py # rule->strategy dispatch table
│   ├── neural_planner.py    # SparseDrive wrapper (+temporal API)
│   ├── data_loader.py       # Data loading + real map extraction
│   ├── evaluation.py        # Metrics calculation
│   ├── pipeline.py          # End-to-end pipeline
│   └── utils.py            # Helper functions
├── experiments/              # Harnesses (calibration, temporal, sweeps)
│   ├── run_evaluation.py    # placeholder batch demo
│   ├── run_calibration.py   # real-data baseline + trajectory dumps
│   ├── run_temporal.py      # warm-vs-cold harness
│   ├── cmd_sweep.py         # nav-command semantics probe
│   └── sweep_thresholds.py  # GPU-free threshold sweeps
├── tests/                    # Unit tests (92 green)
│   ├── test_all.py
│   ├── test_checker.py
│   ├── test_checker_edges.py
│   ├── test_corrector.py
│   ├── test_data_loader.py
│   ├── test_evaluation.py
│   ├── test_performance.py
│   ├── test_pipeline.py
│   ├── test_rules.py
│   ├── test_sparse_drive.py
│   └── test_t2_coverage.py
└── configs/                  # Configuration files
    ├── default.yaml
    └── correction_strategies.yaml
```

## Adding New Rules

### 1. Extend a category file

New rules go in the matching `rules/<category>.lp`, not in new
files (one file per category keeps the blob assembly stable).
Integer-cm conventions are mandatory (`docs/conventions.md`):
no floats, no `sqrt()`, squared distances, 32-bit-safe products.

```asp
% Violation condition (positions are cm integers, speeds squared)
violation(my_new_rule, ego, T) :-
    pos(ego, T, X, Y), time(T),
    my_condition(X, Y).
```

No `#minimize` directives: findings are collected by the checker,
and `override_active/2` (comfort_quality.lp) is the only
cross-rule arbitration point besides `threat_level/3`.

### 2. Register the finding

Add the head name to `src/correction_strategies.py` (routing group)
and assert it in `tests/test_t2_coverage.py` — `test_rule_registry`
fails the suite for unasserted heads (P-9 policy).

## Evaluation Metrics

| Metric | Description | Target |
|--------|-------------|--------|
| Collision Rate | % of trajectories with collisions | <0.05% |
| L2 Error | Average displacement error | <0.7m |
| Inference Time | Time per trajectory check | <50ms |
| Violation Detection Rate | % of true violations detected | >90% |

## Troubleshooting

### Clingo Import Error

If you get an import error for clingo:

```bash
pip install clingo==5.7.1
```

### CUDA Out of Memory

If you get CUDA out of memory errors, construct the planner on CPU
(`TrajectoryChecker` itself has no `device` parameter — it runs Clingo
on CPU always):

```python
from src.pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline

pipeline = SafetyLayerPipeline(
    planner_path="SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth",
    rules_dir="rules",
    device="cpu",  # planner device; checker is CPU-only
)
```

### Rule Grounding Errors

If you get grounding errors in ASP rules:

1. Check syntax of `.lp` files
2. Ensure all variables are bound
3. Check for circular dependencies

## Next Steps

1. Closed-loop bridge on the installed CARLA server (0.9.15 at
   `C:\CARLA`, smoke-verified S12), then scripted scenarios
2. Re-sweep thresholds on tracked-agent + warm data
3. Real-scene perf headroom (corrector churn under agents)

==> asp-safety/experiments/ablate_corrector.py <==
"""P6-lite ablation: heuristic fixpoint vs CorrectorQP on frozen trajs.

GPU-free: replays experiments/trajs.json (GT + pred_1s + map + boxes
from a real calibration dump) through checker + both correctors and
reports fix rate, L2 delta, and corrector latency per engine. This is
the B1-vs-B2 evidence on logged plans; closed-loop rates need CARLA.

Usage (from asp-safety/):
    python experiments/ablate_corrector.py [--out FILE] [--scenes 0,1]
"""

import sys
import time
import json
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

import numpy as np

from checker import TrajectoryChecker
from corrector import TrajectoryCorrector, CorrectorQP
from pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline


def _l2(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    n = min(len(a), len(b))
    return float(np.mean(np.linalg.norm(a[:n] - b[:n], axis=1)))


def run_ablation(dump_path: str, scenes=None):
    base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    dump = json.loads(Path(dump_path).read_text())
    ids = sorted(dump.keys(), key=int)
    if scenes is not None:
        ids = [str(i) for i in scenes]

    checker = TrajectoryChecker(str(base_dir / "rules"))
    heur = TrajectoryCorrector(checker)
    qp = CorrectorQP(checker)
    # Bound-family variants run WITHOUT heuristic fallback so the fix
    # rate measures the family's own power (a fallback fix would be
    # credited to the heuristic, not the family).
    qp_caps = CorrectorQP(checker, bounds='caps',
                          heuristic_fallback=False)
    qp_boxes = CorrectorQP(checker, bounds='boxes',
                           heuristic_fallback=False)

    rows = []
    for sid in ids:
        s = dump[sid]
        gt = np.asarray(s['gt'], dtype=np.float32)
        pred = np.asarray(s['pred_1s'], dtype=np.float32)
        map_data = s['map_data']
        boxes = np.asarray(s['boxes'], dtype=np.float32)
        vels = np.asarray(s.get('velocities', np.zeros((0, 3))),
                          dtype=np.float32)
        scores = np.asarray(s.get('scores', np.zeros((0,))),
                            dtype=np.float32)
        agents = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            boxes, vels, scores, len(pred))
        kw = dict(map_data=map_data,
                  other_vehicles=agents or None)

        ok_p, v_p = checker.check(pred, **kw)
        l2_before = _l2(pred, gt[:len(pred)])
        row = {'scene': int(sid), 'pred_unsafe': not ok_p,
               'l2_before': l2_before,
               'pred_rules': sorted({v['rule'] for v in v_p})}

        for name, eng in (('heuristic', heur), ('qp', qp),
                            ('qp_caps', qp_caps), ('qp_boxes', qp_boxes)):
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            try:
                fixed, entries = eng.correct(pred, **kw)
                ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
                ok_f, v_f = checker.check(fixed, **kw)
                row[f'{name}_fixed'] = bool(ok_f)
                row[f'{name}_ms'] = ms
                row[f'{name}_l2'] = _l2(fixed, gt[:len(fixed)])
                row[f'{name}_entries'] = len(entries)
                row[f'{name}_residual'] = sorted(
                    {v['rule'] for v in v_f})
            except RuntimeError as e:
                ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
                row[f'{name}_fixed'] = False
                row[f'{name}_ms'] = ms
                row[f'{name}_l2'] = None
                row[f'{name}_entries'] = 0
                row[f'{name}_residual'] = [f'GAVE_UP:{e}']
        rows.append(row)
        print(f"  scene {sid}: unsafe={row['pred_unsafe']} "
              f"heur_fixed={row['heuristic_fixed']} "
              f"({row['heuristic_ms']:.0f} ms) "
              f"qp_fixed={row['qp_fixed']} ({row['qp_ms']:.0f} ms) "
              f"caps={row['qp_caps_fixed']} boxes={row['qp_boxes_fixed']}")

    unsafe = [r for r in rows if r['pred_unsafe']]

    def _rate(key):
        return (sum(r[key] for r in unsafe) / len(unsafe)
                if unsafe else 1.0)

    def _mean(key):
        return float(np.mean([r[key] for r in rows]))
    summary = {
        'scenes': len(rows),
        'pred_unsafe': len(unsafe),
        'heuristic_fixed_rate': _rate('heuristic_fixed'),
        'qp_fixed_rate': _rate('qp_fixed'),
        'qp_caps_fixed_rate': _rate('qp_caps_fixed'),
        'qp_boxes_fixed_rate': _rate('qp_boxes_fixed'),
        'heuristic_ms_mean': _mean('heuristic_ms'),
        'qp_ms_mean': _mean('qp_ms'),
        'qp_caps_ms_mean': _mean('qp_caps_ms'),
        'qp_boxes_ms_mean': _mean('qp_boxes_ms'),
        'rows': rows,
    }
    print(f"\nunsafe {len(unsafe)}/{len(rows)} | "
          f"heuristic fixed {summary['heuristic_fixed_rate']:.2f} "
          f"({summary['heuristic_ms_mean']:.0f} ms) | "
          f"qp fixed {summary['qp_fixed_rate']:.2f} "
          f"({summary['qp_ms_mean']:.0f} ms) | "
          f"caps {summary['qp_caps_fixed_rate']:.2f} "
          f"boxes {summary['qp_boxes_fixed_rate']:.2f}")
    return summary


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="QP vs heuristic ablation")
    ap.add_argument("--dump", default=str(
        Path(__file__).parent / "trajs.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", default=None,
                    help="comma-separated scene ids, default all")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="JSON summary path")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    scenes = ([int(x) for x in args.scenes.split(",")]
              if args.scenes else None)
    summary = run_ablation(args.dump, scenes)
    if args.out:
        Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
        print(f"wrote {args.out}")

==> asp-safety/experiments/cap_ab.py <==
"""A/B: map budget caps vs verdicts + timing (forward item 3)."""

import sys
import time
import json
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

import data_loader as DL
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader
from checker import TrajectoryChecker

CONFIGS = [
    ("res2", None),
    ("res4", None),
]


def build_maps(cw_polys, sw_polys, cells):
    DL.MAP_CROSSWALK_POLYS = cw_polys
    DL.MAP_WALKWAY_POLYS = sw_polys
    DL.MAP_TILE_CELLS = cells
    loader = NuScenesLoader("C:/data/sets/nuscenes", version="v1.0-mini")
    return loader


def main():
    trajs = json.loads(
        Path(__file__).parent.joinpath("trajs.json").read_text())
    checker = TrajectoryChecker(
        str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "rules"))
    for name, _ in CONFIGS:
        if name == "res2":
            DL.MAP_CENTERLINE_RES = 2.0
        else:
            DL.MAP_CENTERLINE_RES = 4.0
        DL.MAP_CROSSWALK_POLYS = 8
        DL.MAP_WALKWAY_POLYS = 8
        DL.MAP_TILE_CELLS = 48
        loader = NuScenesLoader("C:/data/sets/nuscenes",
                                version="v1.0-mini")
        n_tiles = 0
        times = []
        verdicts = {}
        for idx in ["0", "7", "1", "5", "8"]:
            m = loader.get_map_data(int(idx))
            n_tiles += len(m.get("crosswalks", [])) + len(
                m.get("sidewalks", []))
            gt = np.array(trajs[idx]["gt"], dtype=np.float32)
            pred = np.array(trajs[idx]["pred_1s"], dtype=np.float32)
            s = time.perf_counter()
            ok_g, v_g = checker.check(gt, map_data=m)
            ok_p, v_p = checker.check(pred, map_data=m)
            times.append((time.perf_counter() - s) * 1000.0)
            hard_g = sorted((d["rule"], d["time"]) for d in v_g
                            if d.get("type", "violation") != "warning")
            hard_p = sorted((d["rule"], d["time"]) for d in v_p
                            if d.get("type", "violation") != "warning")
            verdicts[idx] = (ok_g, ok_p, hard_g, hard_p)
        times.sort()
        print(f"{name}: tiles={n_tiles} "
              f"check_ms min={times[0]:.1f} p50={times[2]:.1f} "
              f"max={times[-1]:.1f}")
        for idx, (ok_g, ok_p, hard_g, hard_p) in verdicts.items():
            print(f"  scene {idx}: gt_safe={ok_g} pred_safe={ok_p} "
                  f"gt={hard_g} pred={hard_p}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_box_check.py <==
"""Detector mirror-sign check on CARLA.

Places a static vehicle at:
  A) (x+10, y+0, z) relative to ego heading (dead ahead)
  B) (x+10, y-5, z)  (5 m to the ego's LEFT in the planner frame --
     CARLA world y is right-handed w.r.t. heading, so left = minus-y)
and prints the planner's top-scoring detected box (lat, fwd, z).

Verdict rules (lat column after our (lat,fwd) emission convention):
  A box  ~ (0, 10)      -> correct center
  B box  ~ (+5, 10)     -> planner +lat = CARLA-LEFT (convention OK)
  B box  ~ (-5, 10)     -> mirrored detections (planner sees our
                           left as its right; every downstream
                           y-sign must be re-derived)

Run: python experiments/carla_box_check.py
"""
import math
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))

import carla  # noqa: E402
import carla_launch  # noqa: E402
from carla_probe import (  # noqa: E402
    CAM_CONFIGS, IMG_H, IMG_W, build_extrinsic, transform_to_mat,
)
from carla_context import (  # noqa: E402
    to_planner_ego_matrix, transform_matrix,
)
from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402

FIXED_DT = 0.05


def build_rig(world, bp_lib, ego):
    cams = []
    for name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz in CAM_CONFIGS:
        bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.camera.rgb")
        bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", str(IMG_W))
        bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", str(IMG_H))
        bp.set_attribute("fov", str(fov))
        tf = carla.Transform(
            carla.Location(x=dx, y=dy, z=dz),
            carla.Rotation(pitch=-8.0 if yaw == 0.0 else 0.0,
                           yaw=yaw))
        cam = world.spawn_actor(bp, tf, attach_to=ego)
        cams.append((name, cam))
    return cams


def grab_stack(world, queues):
    df = min(q[-1].frame for q in queues.values())
    stack = np.zeros((6, 3, IMG_H, IMG_W), np.float32)
    for idx, (name, *_rest) in enumerate(CAM_CONFIGS):
        ims = [im for im in queues[name] if im.frame == df]
        if not ims:
            return None
        a = (np.frombuffer(ims[-1].raw_data, np.uint8)
             .reshape(IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)[:, :, 2::-1])
        stack[idx] = np.ascontiguousarray(
            a.transpose(2, 0, 1)).astype(np.float32)
    return stack


def run_case(world, planner, K, C, ego, queues, dv, dy_left):
    """Spawn proxy at (10 m fwd, dy_left meters to planner-LEFT):
    CARLA world left-of-heading = rotate fwd vector +90 deg?? no:
    CARLA heading fwd f; the LEFT side is f rotated +90 deg
    counterclockwise in the XY plane about z. Compute explicitly."""
    f = ego.get_transform().get_forward_vector()
    f = np.array([f.x, f.y])
    if np.linalg.norm(f) < 1e-6:
        return
    f /= np.linalg.norm(f)
    left = np.array([-f[1], f[0]])  # +90 deg CCW = left side of heading
    pos = ego.get_location()
    p = pos.x + 10.0 * f[0] + dy_left * left[0]
    q = pos.y + 10.0 * f[1] + dy_left * left[1]

    bp = world.get_blueprint_library().find("vehicle.audi.a2")
    tf = carla.Transform(carla.Location(x=p, y=q, z=0.5))
    proxy = world.try_spawn_actor(bp, tf)
    if proxy is None:
        print(f"[boxcheck] spawn failed p=({p:.1f},{q:.1f})")
        return None
    for _ in range(6):
        world.tick()
        time.sleep(0.02)
    stack = grab_stack(world, queues)
    if stack is None:
        proxy.destroy()
        print("[boxcheck] stack miss")
        return None
    ego_g = to_planner_ego_matrix(
        transform_matrix(ego.get_transform())).astype(np.float32)
    out = planner.predict_frame(
        stack, K.astype(np.float32), C.astype(np.float32), ego_g,
        time.time())
    boxes = out["boxes"]
    scores = out["scores"]
    order = np.argsort(scores)[::-1]
    print(f"[boxcheck] proxy at fwd=10 left={dy_left:+.0f}", flush=True)
    for k in order[:5]:
        b = boxes[k]
        print(f"    box{int(k)} score={scores[k]:.2f} "
              f"lat={b[0]:+.2f} fwd={b[1]:+.2f} z={b[2]:+.2f}",
              flush=True)
    proxy.destroy()
    for _ in range(3):
        world.tick()
    return True


def main():
    carla_launch.launch_server(None)
    client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
    client.set_timeout(60.0)
    world = client.get_world()
    settings = world.get_settings()
    settings.synchronous_mode = True
    settings.fixed_delta_seconds = FIXED_DT
    world.apply_settings(settings)

    bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
    spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
                   key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]

    actors, queues = [], {}
    try:
        ego = world.spawn_actor(
            bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"), spawn)
        actors.append(ego)
        rigs = build_rig(world, bp_lib, ego)
        cams = []
        queues = {}
        for name, cam in rigs:
            cams.append((name, cam))
            actors.append(cam)
            queues[name] = []
            cam.listen(queues[name].append)

        deadline = time.time() + 30.0
        while time.time() < deadline and any(
                len(q) == 0 for q in queues.values()):
            world.tick()
            time.sleep(0.02)
        for q in queues.values():
            q.clear()
        world.tick()

        import math as _m
        K = np.zeros((6, 3, 3), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, (name, yaw, fov, *_r) in enumerate(CAM_CONFIGS):
            fx = (IMG_W / 2) / _m.tan(_m.radians(fov) / 2)
            K[idx] = [[fx, 0, IMG_W / 2], [0, fx, IMG_H / 2], [0, 0, 1]]
        t_world_veh = transform_to_mat(ego.get_transform())
        C = np.zeros((6, 4, 4), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, (name, cam) in enumerate(cams):
            C[idx] = build_extrinsic(
                t_world_veh, transform_to_mat(cam.get_transform()))

        model_path = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
                      / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth")
        planner = SparseDrivePlanner(str(model_path), device="cuda")
        planner.begin_sequence()

        # warm frame on empty scene first
        run_case(world, planner, K, C, ego, queues, 0.0, 0.0)
        run_case(world, planner, K, C, ego, queues, 10.0, 0.0)
        run_case(world, planner, K, C, ego, queues, 10.0, +5.0)
        run_case(world, planner, K, C, ego, queues, 10.0, -5.0)
        print("[boxcheck] DONE")
        return 0
    finally:
        for a in actors:
            try:
                a.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        settings.synchronous_mode = False
        world.apply_settings(settings)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_bridge.py <==
"""CARLA closed-loop bridge (P25-B/C). Shield OFF default; engines
off | heuristic | qp dispatched per replan (P25-C).

One planner (CUDA), one ASP checker, one corrector dispatch table
share the run; NO second model load (6 GB VRAM budget, nextstep.md
5.1). The bridge is the pipeline's closed-loop analogue, not a second
pipeline (no SafetyLayerPipeline instantiation).

Cadence rule (nextstep.md 1): replan every `replan_ticks` ticks
(default 2 s sim); the controller END-ANCHORS the executed plan:
steer alpha to the plan's last waypoint, target speed from the plan's
end-segment speed; intermediate waypoints are lookahead references
only. The 0.5 s native grid is therefore never played back too fast
(documented divergence from naive waypoint queuing).

Warm semantics (nextstep.md 5.3): begin_sequence() once per run;
predict_frame() on replans 2 s sim apart (coarser than nuScenes'
0.5 s cadence; real timestamps carried by ego_status/img_metas).

Steering sign convention (calibrated via experiments/carla_sign_
test.py, P25-B evidence: steer=+0.8 rotates the CARLA-world heading
+16.6 deg = planner-ego -16.6 deg, i.e. CARLA steer>0 turns
planner-RIGHT). The bridge computes alpha in planner-ego frame
(y left-positive), so the command needs cfg.steer_sign = -1.0
(default, frozen from the sign test); overridable for experimentation.

RunLog rows per replan: engine, is_safe_raw, violations (compact),
checker_ms, corrector_ms, plan_ms, fix_flags, corrections_applied_
count, min_ttc_s, agents_count, map_iids, executed waypoints, "
v_target"". Per-tick series: ego world pose/speed; arc length
accumulates from ego positions (sim_km).

CLI: use experiments/run_closed_loop.py (one run -> one JSON row).
"""

import math
import sys
import time
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

import carla  # noqa: E402
import carla_launch  # noqa: E402
from carla_probe import (  # noqa: E402
    CAM_CONFIGS, IMG_H, IMG_W, FLIP, build_extrinsic,
)
from carla_context import (  # noqa: E402
    build_agent_context, build_light_context, build_map_context,
    build_zone_context, transform_matrix, to_planner_ego_matrix,
)
from data_loader import estimate_ego_status  # noqa: E402
from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402
from checker import TrajectoryChecker  # noqa: E402
from corrector import TrajectoryCorrector, CorrectorQP  # noqa: E402
import correction_strategies as _cs  # noqa: E402

# Longitudinal findings are rear-end gaps: the effective remedy is
# slowing down (follow family), not a lateral tail shift. CARLA cut-in
# runs showed the lateral remedy ping-pongs forever -> give-ups. Move
# longitudinal_clearance to the follow family (experiments-side remap;
# frozen src/ untouched).
_cs.COLLISION_RULES = _cs.COLLISION_RULES - {'longitudinal_clearance'}
_cs.FOLLOW_RULES = _cs.FOLLOW_RULES | {'longitudinal_clearance'}
_cs.RULE_GROUPS = dict(_cs.RULE_GROUPS)
_cs.RULE_GROUPS['collision'] = ('_correct_collision', _cs.COLLISION_RULES)
_cs.RULE_GROUPS['follow'] = ('_correct_safe_following', _cs.FOLLOW_RULES)

FIXED_DT = 0.05
CAM_ORDER = ["FRONT", "FRONT_LEFT", "FRONT_RIGHT",
             "BACK", "BACK_LEFT", "BACK_RIGHT"]

CKPT = ROOT / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth"
RULES = ROOT / "rules"


@dataclass
class BridgeConfig:
    scenario: str = "S1"
    seed: int = 1
    engine: str = "off"          # off | heuristic | qp
    plan_engine: str = "neural"  # neural | lane (analytic reference)
    replan_ticks: int = 40       # 40 * 0.05 s = 2 s sim (plan 1)
    max_minutes: float = 6.0     # wall cap
    max_distance_m: float = 600.0
    device: str = "cuda"
    keep_server: bool = True
    # controller constants (nextstep.md 4.3; tuned once on S1)
    wheelbase_m: float = 2.85
    lookahead_factor: float = 0.6
    lookahead_min_m: float = 2.0
    lookahead_max_m: float = 6.0
    v_target_cap_ms: float = 13.9
    steer_gain: float = 2.0      # steer = steer_gain * wheelbase * sin(a)/L
    steer_deadband: float = 0.02
    throttle_gain: float = 1.2
    throttle_smooth: float = 0.2
    steer_sign: float = -1.0     # calibrated: steer sign test P25-B
    dbg_lines: bool = False      # [ctrl] diagnostics every ~2 s sim
    view_ego: bool = False       # spectator ego-follow (windowed server)


class CarlaBridge:
    """Sync-mode loop: CARLA -> planner -> [shield] -> controller."""

    def __init__(self, cfg: BridgeConfig):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.client = None
        self.world = None
        self.ego = None
        self.cams: List = []
        self.collision_sensor = None
        self.col_queue: deque = deque()
        self.col_seen: set = set()
        self.queues: Dict[str, deque] = {}
        self.planner = None
        self.checker = None
        self.correctors: Dict[str, object] = {}
        self.scenario_obj = None
        self.K = None
        self.C = None
        self.stacks: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
        self.last_plan_ego: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
        self.last_plan_world: Optional[List[Tuple[float, float]]] = None
        self.prev_ego_global: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
        self.last_v_target = 0.0
        self.last_throttle = 0.0
        self.prev_world_pos: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
        self.last_progress_pos: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None
        self.stuck_tick: Optional[int] = None
        self.arc_len_m = 0.0
        self.sim_time_s = 0.0
        self.rows: List[dict] = []
        self.tick_series: List[dict] = []
        self.replans = 0
        self.frame_drops = 0
        self.give_ups = 0
        self.collision_events: List[dict] = []
        self.wall0 = 0.0
        self.dbg_count = 0
        self.hold_ticks = 0
        self.spectator = None
        self._final_summary: Optional[dict] = None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # setup / teardown

    def setup(self):
        if not carla_launch.launch_server(None):
            raise RuntimeError("CARLA server unavailable")
        self.client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
        self.client.set_timeout(300.0)
        self.world = self.client.get_world()
        print(f"[bridge] map: {self.world.get_map().name}")

        s = self.world.get_settings()
        s.synchronous_mode = True
        s.fixed_delta_seconds = FIXED_DT
        self.world.apply_settings(s)

        # Defensive sweep: crashed/wall-capped runs and ad-hoc probes
        # can leak NPC vehicles, walkers, cameras and collision
        # sensors; any leftover sits on the shared spawn routes and
        # poisons the next run (stalled/collided). Destroy everything
        # except lights and the spectator before spawning the ego.
        for a in self.world.get_actors():
            if a.type_id.startswith(("traffic.", "spectator")):
                continue
            if a.id < 1000:  # map-built props/signs keep their ids
                continue
            try:
                a.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        bp_lib = self.world.get_blueprint_library()
        from carla_scenarios import get_scenario
        scenario = get_scenario(self.cfg.scenario)
        spawn, actor_hook = scenario.resolve_setup(self.world, self.cfg.seed)
        self.scenario_obj = scenario
        ego_bp = bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3")
        self.ego = self.world.try_spawn_actor(ego_bp, spawn)
        if self.ego is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"ego spawn failed at {spawn}")
        print(f"[bridge] ego at {spawn.location.x:.1f}, "
              f"{spawn.location.y:.1f}")

        self.cams = []
        for name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz in CAM_CONFIGS:
            bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.camera.rgb")
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", str(IMG_W))
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", str(IMG_H))
            bp.set_attribute("fov", str(fov))
            tf = carla.Transform(
                carla.Location(x=dx, y=dy, z=dz),
                carla.Rotation(pitch=-8.0 if yaw == 0.0 else 0.0, yaw=yaw))
            cam = self.world.spawn_actor(bp, tf, attach_to=self.ego)
            self.cams.append((name, cam))
        self.queues = {name: deque(maxlen=4) for name, _ in self.cams}
        for name, cam in self.cams:
            cam.listen(self.queues[name].append)

        c_bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.other.collision")
        self.collision_sensor = self.world.spawn_actor(
            c_bp, carla.Transform(), attach_to=self.ego)
        self.collision_sensor.listen(self.col_queue.append)

        # calibration is attachment-relative: pose-independent
        self.world.tick()
        t_world_veh = transform_matrix(self.ego.get_transform())
        self.K = np.zeros((6, 3, 3), dtype=np.float32)
        self.C = np.zeros((6, 4, 4), dtype=np.float32)
        for idx, (name, cam) in enumerate(self.cams):
            fov = next(f for n, _, f, _, _, _ in CAM_CONFIGS if n == name)
            fx = (IMG_W / 2) / math.tan(math.radians(fov) / 2)
            cx, cy = IMG_W / 2.0, IMG_H / 2.0
            self.K[idx] = [[fx, 0, cx], [0, fx, cy], [0, 0, 1]]
            self.C[idx] = build_extrinsic(
                t_world_veh, transform_matrix(cam.get_transform()))
        names = [n for n, _ in self.cams]
        assert names == CAM_ORDER, f"cam order {names}"

        print(f"[bridge] loading planner ({self.cfg.device})...")
        self.planner = SparseDrivePlanner(str(CKPT), device=self.cfg.device)
        self.checker = TrajectoryChecker(str(RULES))
        self.correctors = {
            "heuristic": TrajectoryCorrector(
                self.checker,
                params={"lateral_shift_m": -2.0},
                on_failure="brake"),
            "qp": CorrectorQP(self.checker, on_failure="brake"),
        }
        self.planner.begin_sequence()
        actor_hook(self.world, self.ego, self)
        self.world.tick()
        loc = self.ego.get_location()
        self.prev_world_pos = np.array([loc.x, loc.y])
        self.wall0 = time.time()
        if self.cfg.view_ego:
            self.spectator = self.world.get_spectator()
            self._view_apply()
            print("[bridge] view: ego-follow enabled")
        print("[bridge] setup complete")

    def teardown(self):
        if getattr(self, "scenario_obj", None) is not None:
            try:
                self.scenario_obj.cleanup(self.world)
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.scenario_obj = None
        try:
            if self.planner is not None:
                self.planner.end_sequence()
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.planner = None
        self.correctors = {}
        try:
            import torch
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
        except Exception:
            pass
        for _, cam in reversed(self.cams):
            try:
                cam.stop()
                cam.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.cams = []
        if self.collision_sensor is not None:
            try:
                self.collision_sensor.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.collision_sensor = None
        if self.ego is not None:
            try:
                self.ego.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.ego = None
        if self.world is not None:
            s = self.world.get_settings()
            s.synchronous_mode = False
            s.fixed_delta_seconds = None
            self.world.apply_settings(s)
        print("[bridge] teardown complete (server kept alive)")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # main loop

    def run(self) -> dict:
        if self.ego is None:
            self.setup()
        status = "ok"
        try:
            status = self._main_loop()
        finally:
            self.teardown()
        return self._summary(status)

    def _main_loop(self) -> str:
        tick_i = 0
        while True:
            self.world.tick()
            tick_i += 1
            self.sim_time_s = tick_i * FIXED_DT

            while self.col_queue:
                ev = self.col_queue.popleft()
                other = getattr(ev, "other_actor", None)
                other_id = getattr(other, "id", None)
                other_type = getattr(other, "type_id", str(other))
                if other_id is not None and other_id in self.col_seen:
                    continue
                if other_id is not None:
                    self.col_seen.add(other_id)
                i_vec = getattr(ev, "normal_impulse", None)
                loc = self.ego.get_location() if self.ego else None
                self.collision_events.append({
                    "tick": tick_i,
                    "sim_s": round(self.sim_time_s, 2),
                    "other": other_type,
                    "other_id": other_id,
                    "ego_xyz": (None if loc is None
                                else (round(loc.x, 1), round(loc.y, 1),
                                      round(loc.z, 1))),
                    "impulse": float(np.linalg.norm(
                        np.array([getattr(i_vec, "x", 0.0),
                                  getattr(i_vec, "y", 0.0),
                                  getattr(i_vec, "z", 0.0)]))),
                })
                print(f"[bridge] COLLISION sim_s={self.sim_time_s:.2f} "
                      f"with {other_type}", flush=True)

            if self.scenario_obj is not None:
                self.scenario_obj.tick(self.world, self.sim_time_s)

            if self.cfg.view_ego:
                self._view_apply()

            if tick_i % self.cfg.replan_ticks == 0:
                self._replan(tick_i)

            self._control_tick()
            wall_min = (time.time() - self.wall0) / 60.0
            if self.arc_len_m >= self.cfg.max_distance_m:
                print(f"[bridge] distance target reached "
                      f"({self.arc_len_m:.0f} m)", flush=True)
                return "distance_reached"
            if wall_min >= self.cfg.max_minutes:
                print(f"[bridge] wall cap reached ({wall_min:.1f} min)",
                      flush=True)
                return "wall_cap"
            # stuck-break: no meaningful progress while throttle is up.
            # Skips windows that include planned holds (v <= 1 m/s, e.g.
            # red-light / jaywalker waits): the zero progress there is
            # intended, and the resume boundary would otherwise false-
            # positive a stall.
            if tick_i % 100 == 0 and self.hold_ticks == 0:
                loc = self.ego.get_location() if self.ego else None
                cur = (loc.x, loc.y) if loc else None
                if cur is None:
                    continue
                if self.last_progress_pos is not None:
                    d = float(np.hypot(
                        cur[0] - self.last_progress_pos[0],
                        cur[1] - self.last_progress_pos[1]))
                    if d < 0.5 and self.last_v_target > 1.0:
                        print(f"[bridge] stalled: {d:.2f} m progress "
                              f"in 5 s sim (target "
                              f"{self.last_v_target:.1f} m/s)",
                              flush=True)
                        return "stalled"
                self.last_progress_pos = cur

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # spectator ego-follow (visible server)

    def _view_apply(self):
        """Point the spectator 12 m behind the ego at 7 m height.

        Applied per tick; the pose change takes effect on the next
        render tick (one 50 ms sim step behind -- imperceptible).
        No physics effect: pure rendering convenience.
        """
        if self.spectator is None or self.ego is None:
            return
        t = self.ego.get_transform()
        yaw = math.radians(t.rotation.yaw)
        loc = t.location
        self.spectator.set_transform(carla.Transform(
            carla.Location(
                x=loc.x - 12.0 * math.cos(yaw),
                y=loc.y - 12.0 * math.sin(yaw),
                z=loc.z + 7.0),
            carla.Rotation(pitch=-25.0, roll=0.0, yaw=t.rotation.yaw)))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # replan

    def _replan(self, tick_i):
        frames = self._sync_frames()
        if frames is None:
            self.frame_drops += 1
            print(f"[bridge] frame drop #{self.frame_drops} "
                  f"(previous plan kept)", flush=True)
            return
        t_start = time.perf_counter()

        ego_tf = self.ego.get_transform()
        ego_g = to_planner_ego_matrix(transform_matrix(ego_tf))
        if self.prev_ego_global is None:
            status = np.zeros(18, dtype=np.float32)
        else:
            dt_s = self.cfg.replan_ticks * FIXED_DT
            status = estimate_ego_status(self.prev_ego_global, ego_g, dt_s)
        self.prev_ego_global = ego_g
        ts = time.time()

        if self.cfg.plan_engine == "lane":
            plan_1s = self._lane_plan(ego_tf, ego_g)
            plan_ms = 0.0
            plan_native = None
        else:
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            out = self.planner.predict_frame(
                self.stacks, self.K, self.C, ego_g, ts, ego_status=status)
            plan_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
            plan_native = out["trajectory"]
            # Emission axis order (verified vs nuScenes gt + CARLA drive,
            # see experiments/plan_axis_check.py): plan columns are
            # (lateral, forward) at 0.5 s cadence. Swap to planner-ego
            # (x forward, y left) before any downstream consumption.
            plan_1s = np.vstack([
                np.zeros((1, 2), dtype=np.float32),
                plan_native[1::2][:3, ::-1],
            ])

        mapd = build_map_context(self.world.get_map(), ego_tf.location,
                                 ego_transform=ego_tf)
        inter, lights = build_light_context(
            self.world, ego_tf.location, ego_transform=ego_tf)
        if inter:
            merged = dict(mapd.get("intersections", {}))
            merged.update(inter)
            mapd["intersections"] = merged
        agents = build_agent_context(self.world, self.ego, plan_1s)
        zones = build_zone_context(self.cfg.scenario)
        ctx = dict(
            other_vehicles=agents or None,
            map_data=mapd or None,
            traffic_lights=lights or None,
            zones=zones,
        )

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            is_safe_raw, violations = self.checker.check(plan_1s, **ctx)
        except Exception as e:
            is_safe_raw, violations = None, [{
                "rule": "check_error", "vehicle": "ego", "time": -1,
                "error": str(e)[:120],
            }]
        checker_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0

        fix_flags: List[str] = []
        corrections_count = 0
        corrector_ms = 0.0
        executed = plan_1s
        if self.cfg.engine != "off":
            if is_safe_raw:
                fix_flags.append("already_safe_corrector_skipped")
            else:
                t0 = time.perf_counter()
                try:
                    executed, applied = self.correctors.get(
                        self.cfg.engine,
                        TrajectoryCorrector(self.checker)).correct(
                            plan_1s, **ctx)
                    corrections_count = len(applied)
                except Exception as e:
                    fix_flags.append("revert_to_plan")
                    self.give_ups += 1
                    print(f"[bridge] corrector give-up: "
                          f"{e}", flush=True)
                corrector_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0

        min_ttc = self._min_ttc(plan_1s, agents)

        t_wv = transform_matrix(ego_tf)
        M = t_wv @ FLIP
        exec_arr = np.asarray(executed, dtype=float)
        world_pts = []
        for (x, y) in exec_arr:
            w = M @ np.array([x, y, 0.0, 1.0])
            world_pts.append((float(w[0]), float(w[1])))
        # up-sample the executed path at ~1 m resolution so the
        # controller's lookahead projection can track curves instead
        # of cutting from the plan end anchor.
        path = []
        for i, pt in enumerate(world_pts):
            if i == 0:
                path.append(pt)
                continue
            a = np.array(world_pts[i - 1])
            b = np.array(pt)
            seg = float(np.linalg.norm(b - a))
            n = max(1, int(seg / 1.0))
            for k in range(1, n + 1):
                path.append(tuple(a + (b - a) * (k / n)))
        self.last_plan_ego = exec_arr.astype(np.float32)
        self.last_plan_world = path

        if len(exec_arr) >= 2:
            self.last_v_target = float(np.linalg.norm(
                exec_arr[-1] - exec_arr[-2]))
        safe_delta = None
        if self.cfg.engine != "off" and "revert_to_plan" not in fix_flags:
            safe_delta = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(
                np.sum((exec_arr - plan_1s) ** 2, axis=1))))

        self.replans += 1
        self.rows.append({
            "replan": self.replans,
            "tick": tick_i,
            "sim_s": round(self.sim_time_s, 3),
            "engine": self.cfg.engine,
            "is_safe_raw": is_safe_raw,
            "violations": [
                {k: v.get(k)
                 for k in ("rule", "vehicle", "vehicle2", "time")}
                if isinstance(v, dict) else {"raw": str(v)}
                for v in violations
            ],
            "checker_ms": round(checker_ms, 2),
            "corrector_ms": round(corrector_ms, 2),
            "plan_ms": round(plan_ms, 2),
            "exec_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - t_start) * 1000.0, 2),
            "fix_flags": fix_flags,
            "corrections_applied_count": corrections_count,
            "min_ttc_s": min_ttc,
            "agents_count": len(agents),
            "map_iids": list(mapd.get("lanes", {})) + list(lights or {}),
            "plan_ego": exec_arr.round(2).tolist(),
            "executed_ego": (
                np.asarray(executed, dtype=float).round(2).tolist()),
            "v_target_ms": round(self.last_v_target, 3),
            "safe_vs_plan_l2": (
                None if safe_delta is None else round(safe_delta, 3)),
        })

    def _lane_plan(
        self, ego_tf, ego_g: np.ndarray,
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Analytic plan: current-lane centerline at a fixed v_ref.

        Fallback / reference-plan engine (plan doc fallback arm):
        walks the CARLA waypoint chain from the ego's road waypoint,
        samples the centerline at 1/2/3 s * v_ref along-path, and
        converts each world point into the planner-ego frame via
        inv(ego_g) so downstream ASP/consumption semantics stay
        identical to the neural plan path.
        """
        v_ref = float(min(7.0, self.cfg.v_target_cap_ms))
        mp = self.world.get_map()
        p0 = ego_tf.location
        wpt = mp.get_waypoint(
            carla.Location(x=p0.x, y=p0.y, z=p0.z + 0.3))
        chain = []
        if wpt is not None:
            acc = 0.0
            prev = (p0.x, p0.y)
            limit = v_ref * 3.0 + 2.0
            while acc < limit:
                nxt = wpt.next(0.5)
                if not nxt:
                    break
                wpt = nxt[0]
                wl = wpt.transform.location
                acc += float(np.hypot(wl.x - prev[0], wl.y - prev[1]))
                prev = (float(wl.x), float(wl.y))
                junction_speed = 5.0 if wpt.is_junction else v_ref
                chain.append((prev[0], prev[1], acc, junction_speed))

        def speed_at(d: float) -> float:
            return v_ref if not chain or d >= chain[-1][2] else (
                chain[0][3] if d <= chain[0][2] else next(
                    s for (x, y, a, s) in chain if a >= d))

        targets = []
        d_plan = 0.0
        for _ in range(3):
            d_plan = min(d_plan + speed_at(d_plan),
                         (chain[-1][2] if chain else v_ref * 3.0))
            targets.append(d_plan)
        inv = np.linalg.inv(ego_g)
        rows = []
        for d_target in targets:
            pt = None
            for (x, y, acc_d, _s) in chain:
                if acc_d >= d_target:
                    pt = (x, y)
                    break
            if pt is None and chain:
                pt = chain[-1][:2]
            if pt is None:
                rows.append([0.0, 0.0])
                continue
            w = np.array([pt[0], pt[1], p0.z, 1.0])
            e = inv @ w
            rows.append([float(e[0]), float(e[1])])
        return np.vstack([
            np.zeros((1, 2), dtype=np.float32),
            np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.float32),
        ])

    def _min_ttc(self, plan: np.ndarray,
                 agents: List[np.ndarray]) -> Optional[float]:
        """nextstep.md 6: min over agents, t in {1..3}, of
        dist(agent_t, plan_t) / closing_speed (m per ASP-step = s)."""
        ttc = []
        for agent in agents:
            n = min(len(plan), len(agent))
            for t in range(1, n):
                d_now = float(np.hypot(*(agent[t] - plan[t])))
                d_prev = float(np.hypot(*(agent[t - 1] - plan[t - 1])))
                closing = (d_prev - d_now) / 1.0
                if closing > 0.1:
                    ttc.append(d_now / closing)
        return min(ttc) if ttc else None

    def _sync_frames(self) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
        deadline = time.time() + 0.5
        while time.time() < deadline:
            if all(self.queues[n] for n, _ in self.cams):
                f = min(self.queues[n][-1].frame for n, _ in self.cams)
                imgs = []
                for name, _ in self.cams:
                    got = [im for im in self.queues[name] if im.frame == f]
                    if got:
                        imgs.append(got[-1])
                if len(imgs) == len(self.cams):
                    stack = np.zeros((6, 3, IMG_H, IMG_W), dtype=np.float32)
                    for idx, im in enumerate(imgs):
                        arr = np.frombuffer(im.raw_data, np.uint8).reshape(
                            IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)
                        stack[idx] = np.ascontiguousarray(
                            arr[:, :, 2::-1].transpose(2, 0, 1)).astype(
                                np.float32)
                    self.stacks = stack
                    return stack
            time.sleep(0.02)
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # controller (plan 4.3; consumption cadence note 1)

    def _control_tick(self):
        if self.last_plan_world is None or self.ego is None:
            return
        loc = self.ego.get_location()
        tr = self.ego.get_transform()
        fwd = tr.get_forward_vector()
        v = self.ego.get_velocity()
        v_now = float(np.hypot(v.x, v.y))
        pos = np.array([loc.x, loc.y])
        lookahead = float(np.clip(
            self.cfg.lookahead_factor * max(v_now, 1.0),
            self.cfg.lookahead_min_m, self.cfg.lookahead_max_m))

        path = np.asarray(self.last_plan_world, dtype=float)
        # Lookahead target: point on the plan path at arc-length
        # (lookahead) ahead of the NEAREST path point to the current
        # ego position. Falls back to the path end.
        diffs = np.linalg.norm(path - pos[None, :], axis=1)
        i_near = int(np.argmin(diffs))
        arc = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(
            np.linalg.norm(np.diff(path, axis=0), axis=1))])
        s0 = float(arc[i_near])
        s_target = s0 + lookahead
        j = int(np.searchsorted(arc, s_target))
        target = path[min(j, len(path) - 1)]
        end_arc = float(arc[-1])
        gap = s_target - end_arc
        if j >= len(path) and len(path) >= 2 and gap > 0:
            d0 = path[-1] - path[-2]
            d0 = d0 / max(np.linalg.norm(d0), 1e-6)
            target = path[-1] + d0 * gap

        # planner-ego convention: y_left = -y_carla; alpha signed so that
        # alpha > 0 means the target lies to the planner-LEFT of heading.
        h = np.array([fwd.x, -fwd.y])
        d = target - pos
        d = np.array([d[0], -d[1]])
        norm_h = np.hypot(*h)
        norm_d = np.hypot(*d)
        if norm_h < 1e-6 or norm_d < 1e-6:
            alpha = 0.0
        else:
            cosang = float(np.clip(
                np.dot(h, d) / (norm_h * norm_d), -1.0, 1.0))
            cross = h[0] * d[1] - h[1] * d[0]
            alpha = math.acos(cosang)
            if cross < 0:
                alpha = -alpha

        steer_norm = float(np.clip(
            self.cfg.steer_gain * self.cfg.wheelbase_m * math.sin(alpha)
            / max(lookahead, 0.5), -1.0, 1.0))
        if abs(steer_norm) < self.cfg.steer_deadband:
            steer_norm = 0.0
        steer = float(np.clip(self.cfg.steer_sign * steer_norm, -1.0, 1.0))

        v_target = float(np.clip(self.last_v_target, 0.0,
                                 self.cfg.v_target_cap_ms))
        throttle = 0.0
        brake = 0.0
        if v_target >= 0.2:
            raw = float(np.clip(
                self.cfg.throttle_gain * (v_target - v_now)
                / max(v_target, 4.0), 0.0, 1.0))
            self.last_throttle = (
                self.cfg.throttle_smooth * raw
                + (1.0 - self.cfg.throttle_smooth) * self.last_throttle)
            throttle = self.last_throttle
        else:
            brake = 1.0

        self.ego.apply_control(carla.VehicleControl(
            steer=steer, throttle=throttle, brake=brake, reverse=False))
        if v_target <= 1.0:
            self.hold_ticks += 1
        else:
            self.hold_ticks = 0

        if self.cfg.dbg_lines and self.dbg_count % 40 == 0:
            print(f"[ctrl] tick={self.sim_time_s:.0f} pos=({loc.x:.1f},"
                  f"{loc.y:.1f}) alpha={math.degrees(alpha):+.0f}deg "
                  f"steer={steer:+.2f} thr={throttle:.2f} "
                  f"v={v_now:.2f}/{v_target:.1f} "
                  f"tgt=({target[0]:.1f},{target[1]:.1f})", flush=True)
        self.dbg_count += 1

        if self.prev_world_pos is not None:
            self.arc_len_m += float(np.hypot(pos[0] - self.prev_world_pos[0],
                                             pos[1] - self.prev_world_pos[1]))
        self.prev_world_pos = pos
        self.tick_series.append({
            "tick": int(round(self.sim_time_s / FIXED_DT)),
            "x": round(float(loc.x), 2), "y": round(float(loc.y), 2),
            "v": round(v_now, 2),
        })

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # summary

    def _summary(self, status: str) -> dict:
        wall = time.time() - self.wall0
        give_rate = self.give_ups / self.replans if self.replans else 0.0
        return {
            "status": status,
            "scenario": self.cfg.scenario,
            "seed": self.cfg.seed,
            "engine": self.cfg.engine,
            "device": self.cfg.device,
            "map": (self.world.get_map().name
                    if self.world is not None else "n/a"),
            "replans": self.replans,
            "frame_drops": self.frame_drops,
            "sim_time_s": round(self.sim_time_s, 1),
            "wall_min": round(wall / 60.0, 2),
            "sim_km": round(self.arc_len_m / 1000.0, 3),
            "collision_events": self.collision_events,
            "collided": len(self.collision_events) > 0,
            "give_ups": self.give_ups,
            "give_up_rate": round(give_rate, 3),
            "rows": self.rows,
            "tick_series": self.tick_series,
            "config": asdict(self.cfg),
        }

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_context.py <==
"""CARLA sim state -> checker-ingress context builders (P25-B).

Converts live CARLA actors/lights/map into the dicts TrajectoryChecker
and the correctors consume at `check(...)`:

  build_agent_context  -> List[(6,2) float32] CV rollouts, ego frame
  build_light_context  -> (intersections, lights) dicts, ego frame
  build_map_context    -> lanes meta dict, ego frame
  build_zone_context   -> None in v1 (no scripted zones)

UNITS: every length in the exported dicts is METERS.
TrajectoryChecker scales to integer centimetres internally
(checker.SCALE_FACTOR = 100); the bridge passes dicts straight
through (deviation from nextstep.md's "x100 before export" phrasing
— the checker does the x100 — recorded in docs/carla_harness.md
Deviations).

FRAMES: CARLA world (x fwd, y RIGHT, z up) vs planner ego (x fwd,
y LEFT, z up): points convert with FLIP = diag(1,-1,1,1),
    p_ego = FLIP @ inv(T_world_veh) @ p_world.
Builders accept an optional ego_transform (carla.Transform); when it
is omitted they emit WORLD-frame coordinates (inspection only).
The bridge always passes the ego transform.

Selftest (no server, no GPU):
    python experiments/carla_context.py --selftest
runs duck-typed fixtures through the builders into a real
TrajectoryChecker ("rules"; clingo CPU) and prints check() verdicts
for one canned unsafe fixture.
"""

import fnmatch
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import numpy as np

FLIP = np.diag([1.0, -1.0, 1.0, 1.0])
FLIP3 = np.diag([1.0, -1.0, 1.0])
CULL_RANGE_M = 160.0
MAX_AGENTS = 20
AGENT_PATTERNS = ("vehicle.*", "walker.*")
LIGHT_COLOR_MAP = {"Green": "green", "Yellow": "yellow", "Red": "red"}


def world_point_to_ego(p_world: np.ndarray, t_world_veh: np.ndarray):
    """World point (3,) -> planner-ego point (3,)."""
    p_veh = np.linalg.inv(t_world_veh) @ np.append(p_world[:3], 1.0)
    return (FLIP @ p_veh)[:3]


def world_vector_to_ego(v_world: np.ndarray, t_world_veh: np.ndarray):
    """World velocity (3,) -> planner-ego velocity (3,)."""
    r = np.linalg.inv(t_world_veh)[:3, :3]
    return FLIP3 @ (r @ v_world[:3])


def to_planner_ego_matrix(t_world_veh: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """CARLA vehicle pose -> nuScenes ego2global (p_world = M @ p_ego)."""
    return t_world_veh @ FLIP


def transform_matrix(t) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.asarray(t.get_matrix(), dtype=np.float64)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# agents


def build_agent_context(world, ego_vehicle, ego_plan,
                        horizon_s: float = 3.0, dt: float = 1.0,
                        cull_range_m: float = CULL_RANGE_M,
                        max_agents: int = MAX_AGENTS,
                        force_ids=None) -> List[np.ndarray]:
    """Tracked vehicles/walkers -> CV-rollout (6,2) ego-frame trajs.

    Mirrors SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories semantics
    (steps = arange(6) * dt from the T=0 anchor; parked actors keep
    whatever velocity they have) and the checker's range/closing
    conflict retention (_cull_agents policy; range 160 m per
    nextstep.md 4.1). force_ids keeps scenario actors regardless of
    range and sorts them first.
    """
    del ego_plan, horizon_s  # rollout length is fixed at 6 @ dt
    try:
        t_world_veh = transform_matrix(ego_vehicle.get_transform())
        actors = world.get_actors()
    except Exception:
        return []

    force = set(force_ids or [])
    rows = []
    for a in actors:
        try:
            type_id = getattr(a, "type_id", "")
            if not any(fnmatch.fnmatch(type_id, p) for p in AGENT_PATTERNS):
                continue
            if a.id == ego_vehicle.id:
                continue
            loc = a.get_location()
            p0 = world_point_to_ego(
                np.array([loc.x, loc.y, loc.z], dtype=np.float64),
                t_world_veh)
            vel = a.get_velocity()
            v3 = world_vector_to_ego(
                np.array([vel.x, vel.y, vel.z], dtype=np.float64),
                t_world_veh)
            vx, vy = float(v3[0]), float(v3[1])
        except (RuntimeError, AttributeError):
            continue  # actor died mid-read (garbage collection race)

        steps = np.arange(6, dtype=np.float32) * dt
        traj = np.column_stack([
            p0[0] + vx * steps, p0[1] + vy * steps]).astype(np.float32)

        d0 = float(np.hypot(p0[0], p0[1]))
        in_conflict = bool(
            np.min(np.hypot(traj[:, 0], traj[:, 1])) <= cull_range_m)
        if a.id in force:
            d_sort = 0.0
        elif not in_conflict:
            continue
        else:
            d_sort = d0
        rows.append((d_sort, a.id, traj))

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[0])
    return [traj for _, _, traj in rows[:max_agents]]
