# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# lights


def _light_trigger_center(li) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    try:
        loc = li.trigger_volume.location
        return np.array([loc.x, loc.y, loc.z], dtype=np.float64)
    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError):
        return None


def _light_state_color(li) -> Optional[str]:
    try:
        state = li.get_state()
        name = getattr(state, "name",
                       str(state).replace("TrafficLightState.", ""))
        return LIGHT_COLOR_MAP.get(name)
    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError):
        return None


def _light_stop_points(li) -> list:
    try:
        return [(wp.transform.location.x, wp.transform.location.y)
                for wp in (li.get_stop_waypoints() or [])]
    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError):
        return []


def build_light_context(world, ego_location, cluster_radius: float = 60.0,
                        ego_transform=None) -> Tuple[Dict, Dict]:
    """Nearby traffic lights -> pseudo-intersections + light facts.

    Lights whose trigger-volume center lies within cluster_radius of
    ego are union-find clustered by mutual proximity; each cluster is
    ONE iid (mean trigger center; radius = max distance from center
    to member stop waypoints + 10 m). Off/Unknown states are skipped
    entirely (absent facts = no rule claim).

    ego_transform: carla.Transform of the ego vehicle; when given,
    intersections/lights export PLANNER-EGO-frame meters (the bridge
    passes it; the signature extension vs the frozen nextstep.md
    signature is logged in docs/carla_harness.md Deviations). When
    omitted, WORLD-frame meters.
    """
    t_wv = transform_matrix(ego_transform) if ego_transform is not None \
        else None

    try:
        lights_actors = [a for a in world.get_actors()
                         if "traffic_light" in getattr(a, "type_id", "")]
    except Exception:
        return {}, {}

    eg = np.array([ego_location.x, ego_location.y, ego_location.z])

    near = []
    for li in lights_actors:
        c = _light_trigger_center(li)
        if c is None:
            continue
        if float(np.hypot(c[0] - eg[0], c[1] - eg[1])) <= cluster_radius:
            color = _light_state_color(li)
            if color is None:
                continue
            near.append((li, c, color))

    parent = list(range(len(near)))

    def find(i):
        while parent[i] != i:
            parent[i] = parent[parent[i]]
            i = parent[i]
        return i

    for i in range(len(near)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(near)):
            if np.hypot(*(near[i][1][:2] - near[j][1][:2])) <= cluster_radius:
                ri, rj = find(i), find(j)
                if ri != rj:
                    parent[ri] = rj

    clusters: Dict[int, list] = {}
    for i in range(len(near)):
        clusters.setdefault(find(i), []).append(near[i])

    intersections: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    lights: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    for members in clusters.values():
        center_w = np.stack([c for _, c, _ in members]).mean(axis=0)
        iid = f"light_{min(a.id for a, _, _ in members)}"
        # Cluster radius: the tightest member's own stop-line ring.
        # CARLA light actors reference stop waypoints across the map;
        # max-over-all-stops inflates the box to 100+ m, which paints
        # "inside the red junction" violations from far away and makes
        # hold-position corrections unfixable. Min member ring covers
        # the physical crossing without the far references.
        member_rings = []
        for a, c_mem, _ in members:
            own = _light_stop_points(a)
            if own:
                own = np.asarray(own)
                ring = float(np.max(np.hypot(
                    own[:, 0] - c_mem[0], own[:, 1] - c_mem[1])))
                member_rings.append(ring)
        if member_rings:
            radius = min(member_rings) + 10.0
        else:
            radius = 30.0
        p = center_w
        if t_wv is not None:
            p = world_point_to_ego(p, t_wv)
        entry = {"x": float(p[0]), "y": float(p[1]), "radius": radius}
        intersections[iid] = entry
        lights[iid] = {"color": members[0][2], "times": [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
                       "x": entry["x"], "y": entry["y"],
                       "radius": radius}
        lights[iid].pop("time", None)
    return intersections, lights


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# map


def build_map_context(world_map, ego_location, ego_transform=None,
                      forward_m: float = 60.0, backward_m: float = 20.0,
                      step_m: float = 3.0) -> Dict:
    """Current-lane centerline -> lanes meta dict (meters).

    OpenDrive walk along the ego's projected driving-lane waypoint
    (forward_m / backward_m at step_m). Emits lane segments,
    lane_width, lane_direction, road_type='urban'. Intersections and
    lights come from build_light_context, not here (Option B retreat
    candidate; retreat decision logged in docs/carla_harness.md).

    ego_transform: when given, vertices are PLANNER-EGO frame meters.
    """
    try:
        import carla
        driving = carla.LaneType.Driving
    except Exception:
        driving = 1

    wp = world_map.get_waypoint(ego_location, project_to_road=True,
                                lane_type=driving)
    if wp is None:
        return {}

    lid = f"carla_r{wp.road_id}_l{wp.lane_id}"
    seq = [wp.transform.location]
    cur = wp
    dist = 0.0
    while dist < forward_m:
        nxt = cur.next(step_m)
        if not nxt:
            break
        loc = nxt[0].transform.location
        if loc.x == seq[-1].x and loc.y == seq[-1].y:
            cur = nxt[0]
            break
        seq.append(loc)
        cur = nxt[0]
        dist += step_m
    cur = wp
    dist = 0.0
    while dist < backward_m:
        prev = cur.previous(step_m)
        if not prev:
            break
        loc = prev[0].transform.location
        if loc.x == seq[0].x and loc.y == seq[0].y:
            cur = prev[0]
            break
        seq.insert(0, loc)
        cur = prev[0]
        dist += step_m

    t_wv = transform_matrix(ego_transform) if ego_transform is not None \
        else None
    pts = []
    for loc in seq:
        p = np.array([loc.x, loc.y, loc.z], dtype=np.float64)
        if t_wv is not None:
            p = world_point_to_ego(p, t_wv)
        pts.append((float(p[0]), float(p[1])))

    segments = [{"x1": pts[i][0], "y1": pts[i][1],
                 "x2": pts[i + 1][0], "y2": pts[i + 1][1]}
                for i in range(len(pts) - 1)]

    mapd: Dict = {"lanes": {lid: segments},
                  "lane_width": {lid: float(wp.lane_width)},
                  "road_type": {lid: "urban"}}
    if len(pts) >= 2:
        d = np.array([pts[-1][0] - pts[0][0], pts[-1][1] - pts[0][1]])
        norm = np.hypot(*d)
        if norm > 1e-6:
            mapd["lane_direction"] = {lid: [
                float(d[0] / norm), float(d[1] / norm)]}  # [dx, dy] list
    return mapd


def build_zone_context(scenario_cfg) -> None:
    """v1: always None (no school/construction zones scripted)."""
    del scenario_cfg
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# selftest (no server, no GPU)


class _Location:
    def __init__(self, x, y, z=0.0):
        self.x, self.y, self.z = x, y, z

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return (self.x, self.y, self.z) == (other.x, other.y, other.z)


class _Waypoint:
    def __init__(self, road_id, lane_id, x, y, width=3.5):
        self.road_id, self.lane_id = road_id, lane_id
        self.lane_width = width
        self.transform = type("T", (), {"location": _Location(x, y)})()
        self._next: list = []
        self._previous: list = []

    def next(self, dist):
        if self._next:
            return self._next
        return []

    def previous(self, dist):
        if self._previous:
            return self._previous
        return []


class _ActorList(list):
    def filter(self, pattern):
        return [a for a in self if fnmatch.fnmatch(a.type_id, pattern)]

    def get_actors(self):
        return self


class _Transform:
    def get_matrix(self):
        return np.eye(4)


class _BoundingBox:
    def __init__(self, loc):
        self.location = loc


class _State:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.name = name


class _Actor:
    _counter = 0

    def __init__(self, type_id, x, y, vx=0.0, vy=0.0, actor_id=None):
        _Actor._counter += 1
        self.id = actor_id if actor_id is not None else _Actor._counter
        self.type_id = type_id
        self._loc = _Location(x, y)
        self._vel = _Location(vx, vy)

    def transform(self):
        return _Transform()

    def get_transform(self):
        return _Transform()

    def get_location(self):
        return self._loc

    def get_velocity(self):
        return self._vel


class _Light(_Actor):
    def __init__(self, x, y, color):
        super().__init__("traffic.traffic_light", x, y,
                         actor_id=None)
        self._color = color

    def get_state(self):
        return _State(self._color)

    def get_trigger_volume(self):
        return _BoundingBox(self._loc)

    def get_stop_waypoints(self):
        return [_Waypoint(1, -1, self._loc.x, self._loc.y)]


class _Map:
    def __init__(self, wp):
        self._wp = wp

    def get_waypoint(self, location, project_to_road=True, lane_type=1):
        return self._wp


def _run_selftest() -> int:
    ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))
    from checker import TrajectoryChecker

    print("[selftest] fixtures (no server, no GPU)")

    ego = _Actor("vehicle.tesla.model3", 0.0, 0.0, actor_id=1)

    # CARLA world (y RIGHT): agent 6 m ahead 1 m right of ego lane,
    # closing 3 m/s -> planner ego (6, +1).
    agent = _Actor("vehicle.tesla.model3", 6.0, -1.0, vx=-3.0, actor_id=2)
    far = _Actor("vehicle.tesla.model3", 200.0, 200.0, vx=0.5, actor_id=3)
    forced = _Actor("vehicle.tesla.model3", 200.0, 0.0, vx=-2.0, actor_id=4)
    walker = _Actor("walker.pedestrian.0001", 5.0, -2.0, vx=-1.0, actor_id=5)
    world = _ActorList([ego, agent, far, forced, walker])

    plan = np.column_stack([
        np.arange(1, 7, dtype=np.float32), np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)])

    agents = build_agent_context(world, ego, plan, force_ids=[4])
    order = [float(tr[0, 0]) for tr in agents]
    print(f"[selftest] agent_context: {len(agents)} trajs "
          f"(expect 3: forced(200), walker(5), vehicle(6); far dropped): "
          f"T0 x={order}")
    assert len(agents) == 3, "agent context culling/cap failed"
    assert abs(order[0] - 200.0) < 1e-4, "forced id not first"
    assert abs(agents[1][0, 1] - 2.0) < 1e-4, \
        "world->ego y flip wrong (walker CARLA -2 must be +2)"

    # light with ego transform -> planner-ego frame conversion
    light = _Light(10.0, -5.0, "Red")   # CARLA -y = planner +y (left)
    inter, lights = build_light_context(_ActorList([light]),
                                        _Location(0, 0),
                                        ego_transform=_Transform())
    iid = next(iter(lights))
    print(f"[selftest] light_context: iid={iid} "
          f"color={lights[iid].get('color')} "
          f"egoxy=({inter[iid]['x']:.1f}, {inter[iid]['y']:.1f})")
    assert lights[iid].get("color") == "red"
    assert abs(inter[iid]["x"] - 10.0) < 1e-6
    assert abs(inter[iid]["y"] - 5.0) < 1e-6, \
        "light ego-frame conversion wrong"

    off_inter, off_lights = build_light_context(
        _ActorList([_Light(3.0, 3.0, "Off")]),
        _Location(0, 0), ego_transform=_Transform())
    assert not off_lights, "Off state must be skipped"

    # chain
    w0 = _Waypoint(1, -1, 0.0, 0.0)
    w1 = _Waypoint(1, -1, 3.0, 0.0)
    w2 = _Waypoint(1, -1, 6.0, 0.0)
    w3 = _Waypoint(1, -1, 9.0, 0.0)
    w3._previous = []
    w2._previous = [w1]
    w0._next = [w1]
    w1._next = [w2]
    w2._next = [w3]
    mapd = build_map_context(_Map(w0), _Location(0, 0),
                             ego_transform=_Transform())
    lid = list(mapd["lanes"])[0]
    print(f"[selftest] map_context: lane={lid} "
          f"segments={len(mapd['lanes'][lid])} "
          f"width={mapd['lane_width'][lid]:.1f}")
    assert len(mapd["lanes"][lid]) >= 3, "lane segment walk broken"
    assert abs(mapd["lane_width"][lid] - 3.5) < 1e-6

    checker = TrajectoryChecker(str(ROOT / "rules"))
    is_safe, violations = checker.check(plan, other_vehicles=agents,
                                        map_data=mapd,
                                        traffic_lights=lights)
    print(f"[selftest] check(unsafe fixture): is_safe={is_safe}, "
          f"violations={violations}")
    assert not is_safe, "expected violations on the unsafe fixture"

    safe = np.column_stack([
        np.linspace(0.0, 0.4, 6, dtype=np.float32),
        np.zeros(6, dtype=np.float32)])
    is_safe2, violations2 = checker.check(
        safe, other_vehicles=[], map_data=mapd,
        traffic_lights=build_light_context(
            _ActorList([]), _Location(0, 0))[1])
    print(f"[selftest] check(safe fixture): is_safe={is_safe2}, "
          f"violations={violations2}")
    assert is_safe2 and not violations2, "safe fixture flagged"

    print("SELFTEST OK")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--selftest" in sys.argv:
        sys.exit(_run_selftest())
    print(__doc__)
    sys.exit(1)

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_launch.py <==
"""CARLA server launcher (P25-A).

Launch / reuse / kill the CARLA 0.9.15 server.

Usage:
    python experiments/carla_launch.py                 # reuse-or-launch, startup map
    python experiments/carla_launch.py --map Town10HD_Opt
    python experiments/carla_launch.py --kill          # kill stale servers, exit
    python experiments/carla_launch.py --windowed --quality Epic

Quality: 0.9.15 has exactly two tiers, Low (default here; shadows and
postprocess off, 50 m draw distance) and Epic (full detail). When the
server already answers on :2000 the flags are moot (reuse path).
"""

import argparse
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time

EXE = r"C:\CARLA\WindowsNoEditor\CarlaUE4.exe"
WORK = r"C:\CARLA\WindowsNoEditor"
SERVER_PORT = 2000


def port_open(port: int = SERVER_PORT, timeout: float = 2.0) -> bool:
    s = socket.socket()
    s.settimeout(timeout)
    try:
        s.connect(("localhost", port))
        s.close()
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False


def kill_stale_servers() -> bool:
    """Taskkill every CarlaUE4 server process; call before a fresh run."""
    killed = False
    for image in ("CarlaUE4.exe", "CarlaUE4-Win64-Shipping.exe"):
        r = subprocess.run(
            ["taskkill", "/F", "/IM", image],
            capture_output=True, text=True,
        )
        if r.returncode == 0:
            killed = True
    if killed:
        print("killed stale CarlaUE4 server process(es)", flush=True)
        time.sleep(5)
    else:
        print("no stale CarlaUE4 server processes to kill", flush=True)
    while port_open():
        time.sleep(3)
        print("waiting for :2000 to drain...", flush=True)
    return killed


def launch_server(map_name: str | None = None,
                  poll_s: int = 10, max_polls: int = 60,
                  quality: str = "Low", windowed: bool = False,
                  stream_pool: int | None = None) -> bool:
    """Start the server unless one already answers on :2000.

    map_name: bare asset name (Town10HD_Opt) or full /Game/... path.
    Default None keeps the startup map.

    quality: "Low" (default) or "Epic" -- the only two 0.9.15 tiers.
    windowed: render to a visible window (default offscreen saves VRAM
    and is what every harness run uses). Resolution 1280x720.
    stream_pool: trim UE4 r.Streaming.PoolSize (MB) via -ExecCmds
    (fallback knob when Epic windowed approaches the VRAM gate).
    """
    if port_open():
        print("server already responding on :2000; reusing", flush=True)
        return True
    args = [EXE, f"-quality-level={quality}"]
    if stream_pool is not None:
        args.append(f'-ExecCmds="r.Streaming.PoolSize {stream_pool}"')
    if windowed:
        args += ["-windowed", "-ResX=1280", "-ResY=720"]
    else:
        args.append("-RenderOffScreen")
    if map_name:
        if not map_name.startswith("/Game/"):
            map_name = f"/Game/Carla/Maps/{map_name}"
        args.insert(1, map_name)
    print(f"launching {' '.join(args)} ...", flush=True)
    subprocess.Popen(args, cwd=WORK)
    for i in range(max_polls):
        if port_open():
            print(f"port open after {i * poll_s}s", flush=True)
            return True
        time.sleep(poll_s)
    print("port never opened", flush=True)
    return False


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument("--map", default=None,
                   help="map to start the server with")
    p.add_argument("--kill", action="store_true",
                   help="kill stale CarlaUE4.exe processes and exit")
    p.add_argument("--quality", default="Low", choices=["Low", "Epic"],
                   help="render tier; 0.9.15 offers Low and Epic only")
    p.add_argument("--windowed", action="store_true",
                   help="render to a visible window instead of off-screen")
    p.add_argument("--stream-pool", type=int, default=None,
                   help="UE4 r.Streaming.PoolSize in MB (VRAM trim knob)")
    a = p.parse_args()
    if a.kill:
        sys.exit(0 if kill_stale_servers() else 1)
    sys.exit(0 if launch_server(a.map, quality=a.quality,
                                windowed=a.windowed,
                                stream_pool=a.stream_pool) else 1)

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_metrics.py <==
"""CARLA closed-loop metrics aggregator (P25-C).

Parses run JSONs produced by run_closed_loop.py under
experiments/runs/ and emits the frozen metric set (nextstep.md 7.2)

    python experiments/carla_metrics.py [--runs "experiments/runs/*.json"]

Writes experiments/carla_metrics.json and prints a per-run table.

Metrics (mean over completed runs unless noted):
  collision_rate_pct   share of runs whose ego collided
  violations_per_mile  ASP violations / driven miles (mean per run)
  intervention_rate    replans where the shield materially changed the plan
  replan_wall_ms       per-replan wall time (planner+checker+corrector)
  frame_drop_rate      dropped sensor synchros / replans
"""

import argparse
import glob
import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MILES_PER_KM = 0.621371


def _rows(run):
    return run.get("rows", [])


def _n_violations(run):
    return sum(len(r.get("violations", [])) for r in _rows(run))


def _n_corrected(run):
    n = 0
    for r in _rows(run):
        if run.get("engine", "off") == "off":
            break
        l2 = r.get("safe_vs_plan_l2")
        flags = r.get("fix_flags", [])
        touched = (l2 is not None and l2 > 0.0) or \
                  ("revert_to_plan" in flags)
        n += int(touched)
    return n


def _exec_ms_mean(run):
    rows = _rows(run)
    if not rows:
        return None
    vals = [r.get("exec_ms") for r in rows if r.get("exec_ms")]
    return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else None


def aggregate(paths):
    per_run = []
    for p in paths:
        try:
            run = json.load(open(p))
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError) as e:
            print(f"  [skip] {p}: {e}")
            continue
        rows = _rows(run)
        n_rep = max(run.get("replans", 0), len(rows), 1)
        km = run.get("sim_km", 0.0)
        item = {
            "file": os.path.basename(p),
            "scenario": run.get("scenario"),
            "seed": run.get("seed"),
            "engine": run.get("engine"),
            "plan_engine": run.get("config", {}).get(
                "plan_engine") or run.get("plan_engine"),
            "status": run.get("status"),
            "collided": run.get("collided"),
            "sim_km": km,
            "violations": _n_violations(run),
            "violations_per_mile": (_n_violations(run)
                                    / max(km * MILES_PER_KM, 1e-6)),
            "replans": n_rep,
            "corrected_rows": _n_corrected(run),
            "intervention_rate": _n_corrected(run) / n_rep,
            "frame_drops": run.get("frame_drops", 0),
            "exec_ms_mean": _exec_ms_mean(run),
        }
        per_run.append(item)

    def _mean(key):
        vals = [it[key] for it in per_run if it[key] is not None]
        return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else None

    summary = per_run and {
        "n_runs": len(per_run),
        "collision_rate_pct": 100.0 * sum(
            1 for it in per_run if it["collided"]) / len(per_run),
        "violations_per_mile": _mean("violations_per_mile"),
        "intervention_rate": _mean("intervention_rate"),
        "replan_wall_ms_mean": _mean("exec_ms_mean"),
        "frame_drop_rate": _mean("frame_drops") / max(
            _mean("replans"), 1.0),
        "per_run": per_run,
    } or {}
    return summary


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("--runs", default="experiments/runs/*.json",
                    help="glob of run JSON(s); paths relative to repo root")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(ROOT / "experiments"
                                         / "carla_metrics.json"))
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    pattern = args.runs
    if not os.path.isabs(pattern) and not pattern.startswith("experiments"):
        pattern = str(ROOT / pattern)
    paths = sorted(glob.glob(pattern))
    if not paths:
        print(f"no runs matched: {pattern}")
        return 1
    print(f"aggregating {len(paths)} run(s)")

    summary = aggregate(paths)
    if not summary:
        print("no valid runs parsed")
        return 1

    with open(args.out, "w") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=1)

    print(f"\ncollision_rate_pcnt  {summary['collision_rate_pct']:.1f}")
    print(f"viol_per_mile        {summary['violations_per_mile']:.2f}")
    print(f"intervention_rate    {summary['intervention_rate']:.3f}")
    print(f"replan_wall_ms_mean  {summary['replan_wall_ms_mean']:.0f}")
    print(f"frame_drop_rate      {summary['frame_drop_rate']:.4f}")

    print("\n" + "  ".join(
        f"{h:>11}" for h in ["run", "scen", "eng", "plan",
                             "km", "viol", "intv", "ms", "col", "status"]))
    for it in summary["per_run"]:
        name = it["file"][:24]
        print(f"{name[:11]:>11}  {it['scenario'] or '-':>4} "
              f"{(it['engine'] or '-'):>9} {(it['plan_engine'] or '-'):>6} "
              f"{it['sim_km']:>6.2f} {it['violations']:>4} "
              f"{it['intervention_rate']:>6.3f} "
              f"{(it['exec_ms_mean'] or 0):>7.0f} "
              f"{str(it['collided']):>5} {it['status']}")
    print(f"\nwrote {args.out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_probe.py <==
"""One-shot CARLA rig + coordinate probe (P25-A).

Verifies the 6-camera nuScenes-like rig and the coordinate transform
chain BEFORE anything downstream consumes it:

  1. reuse-or-launch server, connect, print versions/map
  2. synchronous mode, fixed dt 0.05
  3. spawn ego + 6x rgb 704x256 cameras at nuScenes-like mounts
  4. K from fov (square pixels, cx/cy at image center)
  5. C_i: planner-ego -> CV camera frame, composed from the sensor
     transform with the CARLA(UE4)->nuScenes handedness adaptation
     (y-flip F: CARLA vehicle y-right -> nuScenes ego y-left, then the
     optical-axis remap S: CARLA cam (x fwd, y right, z up) -> CV cam
     (x right, y down, z fwd)):
         C_i = S @ T_v_c @ F          (4x4, camera-to-ego semantics)
     so the planner's projection_mat = K @ C_i[:3] projects EGO-frame
     points directly to pixels with u = fx*x/z + cx.
  6. projection sanity: actors 12 m ahead 2 m LEFT / 2 m RIGHT of ego
     must project into CAM_FRONT with u < cx / u > cx respectively
     (sign errors here poison every later phase).
  7. VRAM report around one warm predict_frame on a real CARLA stack.

Saves experiments/probe_out/cam_front.png + probe_calib.npz, prints
PROBE OK on success.

Run:  python experiments/carla_probe.py [--map NAME]
"""

import argparse
import math
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import carla_launch  # noqa: E402
import carla  # noqa: E402

IMG_W, IMG_H = 704, 256

# name -> (yaw_deg, fov_deg, dx, dy, dz) relative to ego, meters.
# y positive = LEFT in the planner frame; CARLA uses y-right, so the
# physical lateral offset is applied directly in CARLA convention.
CAM_CONFIGS = [
    ("FRONT", 0.0, 52.0, 1.8, 0.0, 2.0),
    ("FRONT_LEFT", 55.0, 52.0, 1.6, 0.10, 2.0),
    ("FRONT_RIGHT", -55.0, 52.0, 1.6, -0.10, 2.0),
    ("BACK", 180.0, 65.0, -1.8, 0.0, 2.0),
    ("BACK_LEFT", 110.0, 65.0, -1.6, 0.60, 2.0),
    ("BACK_RIGHT", -110.0, 65.0, -1.6, -0.60, 2.0),
]

FLIP = np.diag([1.0, -1.0, 1.0, 1.0])


def remap_s_mat() -> np.ndarray:
    """S: CARLA cam (x fwd, y right, z up) -> CV cam (x right, y down, z fwd)."""
    s = np.zeros((4, 4))
    s[0, 1] = 1.0    # x_cv = y_carla (right)
    s[1, 2] = -1.0   # y_cv = -z_carla (down)
    s[2, 0] = 1.0    # z_cv = x_carla (fwd)
    s[3, 3] = 1.0
    return s


def transform_to_mat(t) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.asarray(t.get_matrix(), dtype=np.float64)


def world_to_ego_nu(t_world_vehicle: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """CARLA vehicle pose -> nuScenes ego2global matrix.

    p_world = T_world_veh @ F @ p_ego  =>  ego2global = T_world_veh @ F.
    """
    return t_world_vehicle @ FLIP


def build_extrinsic(t_world_veh, t_world_cam) -> np.ndarray:
    """C_i: maps planner-ego-frame points to the CV camera frame."""
    t_v_c = np.linalg.inv(t_world_veh) @ t_world_cam  # cam -> CARLA vehicle
    return remap_s_mat() @ t_v_c @ FLIP


def pinch_pixel(p_cam: np.ndarray, fx: float, fy: float,
                cx: float, cy: float) -> tuple:
    u = fx * p_cam[0] / p_cam[2] + cx
    v = fy * p_cam[1] / p_cam[2] + cy
    return float(u), float(v)


def try_spawn_near(world, bp_lib, blueprint, at, tries=5):
    """Spawn at `at`, nudging fallback offsets if the spot is blocked."""
    for i in range(tries):
        loc = carla.Location(at)  # noqa: F821
        if i:
            loc.x += 0.5 * i * (1 if i % 2 else -1)
            loc.y += 0.5 * i
        actor = world.try_spawn_actor(
            blueprint, carla.Transform(loc))  # noqa: F821
        if actor is not None:
            return actor
    return None


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--map", default=None)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    t0 = time.time()
    done = []

    if not carla_launch.launch_server(args.map):
        print("PROBE FAIL: could not obtain a server")
        return 1

    client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
    client.set_timeout(300.0)
    print("server version:", client.get_server_version(), flush=True)
    world = client.get_world()
    print("map:", world.get_map().name, flush=True)
    if args.map and args.map not in world.get_map().name:
        print(f"loading map {args.map} ...", flush=True)
        world = client.load_world(args.map)
        print("map now:", world.get_map().name, flush=True)

    settings = world.get_settings()
    settings.synchronous_mode = True
    settings.fixed_delta_seconds = 0.05
    world.apply_settings(settings)

    bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
    spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
                   key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]

    actors = []
    cams = []
    queues = {}
    try:
        ego = try_spawn_near(world, bp_lib,
                             bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"),
                             spawn.location)
        if ego is None:
            print("PROBE FAIL: ego spawn failed")
            return 1
        actors.append(ego)

        for name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz in CAM_CONFIGS:
            bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.camera.rgb")
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", str(IMG_W))
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", str(IMG_H))
            bp.set_attribute("fov", str(fov))
            transform = carla.Transform(  # noqa: F821
                carla.Location(x=dx, y=dy, z=dz),  # noqa: F821
                carla.Rotation(pitch=-8.0 if yaw == 0.0 else 0.0,  # noqa: F821
                               yaw=yaw))
            cam = world.spawn_actor(bp, transform, attach_to=ego)
            cams.append((name, cam))
        actors.extend(c for _, c in cams)

        queues = {name: [] for name, _ in cams}
        for name, cam in cams:
            cam.listen(queues[name].append)

        world.tick()
        deadline = time.time() + 60.0
        while time.time() < deadline and any(
                len(q) == 0 for q in queues.values()):
            world.tick()
            time.sleep(0.05)
        missing = [n for n, q in queues.items() if not q]
        if missing:
            print(f"PROBE FAIL: no frames within 60 s for {missing}")
            return 1

        # ---- intrinsics ----
        K = np.zeros((6, 3, 3), dtype=np.float64)
        fovs = {}
        for idx, (name, _) in enumerate(cams):
            fov = next(f for n, _, f, _, _, _ in CAM_CONFIGS if n == name)
            fovs[name] = fov
            fx = (IMG_W / 2) / math.tan(math.radians(fov) / 2)
            cx, cy = IMG_W / 2, IMG_H / 2
            K[idx] = [[fx, 0, cx], [0, fx, cy], [0, 0, 1]]

        # ---- extrinsics ----
        t_world_veh = transform_to_mat(ego.get_transform())
        ego2global = world_to_ego_nu(t_world_veh)
        C = np.zeros((6, 4, 4), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, (name, cam) in enumerate(cams):
            C[idx] = build_extrinsic(t_world_veh,
                                     transform_to_mat(cam.get_transform()))
        print("per-cam (yaw_deg, fov_deg):",
              {name: (cfg[1], fovs[name])
               for name, cfg in zip([n for n, _ in cams], CAM_CONFIGS)},
              flush=True)

        # ---- projection sanity actors ----
        test_actors = []
        for label, y_left in (("LEFT", 2.0), ("RIGHT", -2.0)):
            loc = carla.Location(  # noqa: F821
                x=spawn.location.x + 12.0,
                y=spawn.location.y - y_left,  # planner +y(left) = CARLA -y
                z=spawn.location.z)
            a = try_spawn_near(
                world, bp_lib, bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"), loc)
            if a is None:
                print(f"PROBE FAIL: {label} test actor spawn failed")
                return 1
            test_actors.append((label, a))
        actors.extend(a for _, a in test_actors)
        world.tick()
        time.sleep(0.2)

        t_world_veh = transform_to_mat(ego.get_transform())
        C_front = C[0]
        K_front = K[0]
        fx, fy = K_front[0, 0], K_front[1, 1]
        cx, cy = K_front[0, 2], K_front[1, 2]

        results = {}
        for label, actor in test_actors:
            loc = actor.get_location()
            p_world = np.array([loc.x, loc.y, loc.z, 1.0])
            p_ego_nu = FLIP @ (np.linalg.inv(t_world_veh) @ p_world)
            p_cam = C_front @ p_ego_nu
            u, v = pinch_pixel(p_cam[:3], fx, fy, cx, cy)
            inside = (0 <= u < IMG_W) and (0 <= v < IMG_H)
            results[label] = (u, v, inside, float(p_cam[2]))
            print(f"projection {label}: u={u:.1f} v={v:.1f} "
                  f"in-image={inside} z_cam={p_cam[2]:.2f}", flush=True)

        checks = [
            results["LEFT"][2] and results["RIGHT"][2],
            results["LEFT"][0] < cx < results["RIGHT"][0],
            results["LEFT"][3] > 0 and results["RIGHT"][3] > 0,
        ]
        if not all(checks):
            print("PROBE FAIL: projection checks "
                  "(in-image, left<u_center<right, z>0):", checks)
            return 1

        # ---- save calibration + front image ----
        out_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "probe_out"
        out_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        try:
            import cv2
            has_cv = True
        except ImportError:
            has_cv = False
        img = queues["FRONT"][0]
        arr_u8 = np.frombuffer(img.raw_data, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(
            IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)
        rgb = np.ascontiguousarray(arr_u8[:, :, 2::-1])  # BGRA -> RGB, top-down
        if has_cv:
            cv2.imwrite(str(out_dir / "cam_front.png"), rgb)
        else:
            rgb.dump(str(out_dir / "cam_front.npy"))
        np.savez(out_dir / "probe_calib.npz", K=K, C=C,
                 ego2global=ego2global,
                 camera_names=[n for n, _ in cams])

        # ---- VRAM around one warm predict_frame ----
        import torch
        free0, total0 = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
        free0_gb, total0_gb = free0 / 2**30, total0 / 2**30
        print(f"VRAM before model load: free={free0_gb:.2f}/"
              f"{total0_gb:.2f} GB (server textures only)", flush=True)

        model_path = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
                      / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth")
        sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))
        from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402
        planner = SparseDrivePlanner(str(model_path), device="cuda")
        free1, _ = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
        print(f"VRAM after model load:  free={free1 / 2**30:.2f} GB",
              flush=True)

        stack = np.zeros((6, 3, IMG_H, IMG_W), dtype=np.float32)
        order = ["FRONT", "FRONT_LEFT", "FRONT_RIGHT",
                 "BACK", "BACK_LEFT", "BACK_RIGHT"]
        for idx, name in enumerate(order):
            img = queues[name][-1]
            arr = np.frombuffer(img.raw_data, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(
                IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)
            stack[idx] = np.ascontiguousarray(
                arr[:, :, 2::-1].transpose(2, 0, 1)).astype(np.float32)

        planner.begin_sequence()
        t_inf = time.time()
        out = planner.predict_frame(stack, K.astype(np.float32),
                                    C.astype(np.float32),
                                    ego2global.astype(np.float32),
                                    time.time())
        dt_inf = time.time() - t_inf
        planner.end_sequence()
        free2, _ = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
        free2_gb = free2 / 2**30
        traj = out["trajectory"]
        print(f"VRAM after warm frame:  free={free2_gb:.2f} GB; "
              f"traj shape={traj.shape} dtype={traj.dtype}; "
              f"p0={traj[0].tolist()}", flush=True)
        print(f"warm predict_frame wall: {dt_inf:.2f} s", flush=True)
        print(f"VRAM headroom: {free2_gb:.2f} GB "
              + ("OK (>=1.5 GB)" if free2_gb >= 1.5
                 else "LOW (<1.5 GB; retreat ladder at nextstep.md 9)"),
              flush=True)
        if free2_gb >= 1.5:
            done.append("VRAM-headroom")

        done += ["rig+K+C", "projection-sanity", "warm-frame"]
        print(f"saved: {out_dir / 'probe_calib.npz'} (+ cam_front.png); "
              f"total wall {time.time() - t0:.1f} s")
        print("PROBE OK:", "; ".join(done))
        return 0
    finally:
        for _, cam in cams:
            try:
                cam.stop()
                cam.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        for a in actors:
            try:
                if a and a.is_alive:
                    a.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        settings = world.get_settings()
        settings.synchronous_mode = False
        settings.fixed_delta_seconds = None
        world.apply_settings(settings)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_sanity.py <==
"""Planner sanity on CARLA imagery: repeated warm frames while ego drives.

Answers three questions before the next closed-loop S1 attempt:
  1. Does the plan stabilize after the first warm frame, or keep
     predicting a large lateral swerve on a straight road?
  2. Are detected boxes sane in the planner frame (front objects
     should sit on +x with |y| small)?
  3. Are the camera images right-side up (sky brighter than road
     on CAM_FRONT)?

Run: python experiments/carla_sanity.py
"""
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))

import carla  # noqa: E402
import carla_launch  # noqa: E402
from carla_probe import (  # noqa: E402
    CAM_CONFIGS, IMG_H, IMG_W, build_extrinsic, transform_to_mat,
)
from carla_context import (  # noqa: E402
    to_planner_ego_matrix, transform_matrix,
)
from data_loader import estimate_ego_status  # noqa: E402
from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402

FIXED_DT = 0.05
FRAME_EVERY = 10   # 0.5 s sim between planner calls
N_FRAMES = 6


def grab_stack(world, queues):
    df = min(q[-1].frame for q in queues.values())
    stack = np.zeros((6, 3, IMG_H, IMG_W), np.float32)
    idx = 0
    for name, *_ in CAM_CONFIGS:
        ims = [im for im in queues[name] if im.frame == df]
        if not ims:
            return None
        a = (np.frombuffer(ims[-1].raw_data, np.uint8)
             .reshape(IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)[:, :, 2::-1])
        stack[idx] = np.ascontiguousarray(
            a.transpose(2, 0, 1)).astype(np.float32)
        idx += 1
    return stack


def main():
    carla_launch.launch_server(None)
    client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
    client.set_timeout(60.0)
    world = client.get_world()
    settings = world.get_settings()
    settings.synchronous_mode = True
    settings.fixed_delta_seconds = FIXED_DT
    world.apply_settings(settings)

    bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
    spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
                   key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]

    actors = []
    queues = {}
    try:
        ego = world.spawn_actor(
            bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"), spawn)
        if ego is None:
            print("[sanity] FAIL: ego spawn")
            return 1
        actors.append(ego)
        quo = {}
        for name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz in CAM_CONFIGS:
            bp = world.get_blueprint_library().find("sensor.camera.rgb")
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", str(IMG_W))
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", str(IMG_H))
            bp.set_attribute("fov", str(fov))
            tf = carla.Transform(
                carla.Location(x=dx, y=dy, z=dz),
                carla.Rotation(pitch=-8.0 if yaw == 0.0 else 0.0,
                               yaw=yaw))
            cam = world.spawn_actor(bp, tf, attach_to=ego)
            actors.append(cam)
            quo[name] = []
            cam.listen(quo[name].append)
        queues = quo

        deadline = time.time() + 30.0
        while time.time() < deadline and any(
                len(q) == 0 for q in queues.values()):
            world.tick()
            time.sleep(0.02)
        missing = [n for n, q in queues.items() if not q]
        if missing:
            print(f"[sanity] FAIL: no frames for {missing}")
            return 1
        for q in queues.values():
            q.clear()
        world.tick()

        # K and C computed once, exactly like the probe/bridge setup
        import math
        K = np.zeros((6, 3, 3), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, (name, yaw, fov, _, _, _) in enumerate(CAM_CONFIGS):
            fx = (IMG_W / 2) / math.tan(math.radians(fov) / 2)
            cx, cy = IMG_W / 2, IMG_H / 2
            K[idx] = [[fx, 0, cx], [0, fx, cy], [0, 0, 1]]
        t_world_veh = transform_to_mat(ego.get_transform())
        C = np.zeros((6, 4, 4), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, (name, cam) in enumerate(
                [(n, a) for n, a in zip(
                    [c[0] for c in CAM_CONFIGS],
                    [a for a in actors[1:]])]):
            C[idx] = build_extrinsic(
                t_world_veh, transform_to_mat(cam.get_transform()))

        # orientation probe: sky vs road on CAM_FRONT
        deadline = time.time() + 10.0
        while time.time() < deadline and any(
                len(q) == 0 for q in queues.values()):
            world.tick()
            time.sleep(0.02)
        df = min(q[-1].frame for q in queues.values())
        front = [im for im in queues["FRONT"] if im.frame == df][-1]
        a = (np.frombuffer(front.raw_data, np.uint8)
             .reshape(IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)[:, :, 2::-1])
        top, bot = float(a[:20].mean()), float(a[-20:].mean())
        print(f"[sanity] CAM_FRONT top20={top:.1f} bottom20={bot:.1f} "
              + ("sky-top brighter: OK" if top > bot
                 else "ROAD-top brighter: FLIPPED?"), flush=True)

        model_path = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
                      / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth")
        planner = SparseDrivePlanner(str(model_path), device="cuda")
        planner.begin_sequence()

        prev_ego_g = None
        printed = 0
        tick_i = 0
        while printed < N_FRAMES:
            world.tick()
            tick_i += 1
            ctrl = ego.get_control()
            ctrl.throttle = 0.45
            ctrl.steer = 0.0
            ctrl.brake = 0.0
            ctrl.hand_brake = False
            ego.apply_control(ctrl)

            if tick_i % FRAME_EVERY:
                continue
            stack = grab_stack(world, queues)
            if stack is None:
                print(f"[sanity] frame{printed}: stack miss "
                      f"(frames not synced)", flush=True)
                continue
            ego_g = to_planner_ego_matrix(
                transform_matrix(ego.get_transform()))
            if prev_ego_g is None:
                status = np.zeros(18, dtype=np.float32)
            else:
                status = estimate_ego_status(prev_ego_g, ego_g, 0.5)
            prev_ego_g = ego_g
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            out = planner.predict_frame(
                stack, K.astype(np.float32), C.astype(np.float32),
                ego_g.astype(np.float32), time.time(), ego_status=status)
            ms = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
            traj = out["trajectory"]
            nb = out["boxes"].shape[0]
            scores = (out["scores"][:3].tolist() if nb else [])
            box0 = (out["boxes"][0, :3].round(1).tolist() if nb else [])
            print(f"[sanity] frame{printed} {ms:.0f}ms "
                  f"traj={traj.round(2).tolist()} "
                  f"nbox={nb} scores={scores} box0={box0}", flush=True)
            printed += 1
        print("[sanity] DONE")
        return 0
    finally:
        for a in actors:
            try:
                a.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        if world is not None:
            settings.synchronous_mode = False
            world.apply_settings(settings)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_scenarios.py <==
"""Scripted closed-loop scenarios (P25-D: S1-S5).

Each scenario exposes:
    resolve_setup(world, seed) -> (carla.Transform spawn, actor_hook)
where actor_hook(world, ego, bridge) spawns/records the scripted
actors right after the ego and rig exist but before the first
physics tick of the run loop.
    tick(world, sim_time_s)    -> per-tick deterministic choreography
    cleanup(world)             -> despawn scripted actors / unfreeze

Choreography arm: scripted movers are kinematically teleported
(set_simulate_physics(False) + set_transform each fixed-dt tick), so
runs are deterministic end-to-end. Equivalent to the "ORACLE-CONTEXT"
flagged arm from nextstep.md 7.4: the ASP shield sees ground-truth
actor poses; planner camera detections are NOT the source for
bystanders' motion (logged in docs/carla_harness.md Deviations).

Scenarios (frozen in nextstep.md 7.1):
    S1 free drive         | no ambient traffic      | baseline
    S2 red-light run      | controlling light actors frozen RED;
                          | ego spawn selected so the stop line is
                          | 55-95 m ahead
    S3 cut-in             | NPC 2 lanes left, ~25-40 m ahead, same
                          | direction slower; cuts into ego's lane
                          | starting at t=3 s
    S4 jaywalker          | walker crosses ego's path outside a
                          | marked crossing, 15 m ahead, 1.2 m/s
    S5 oncoming left-turn | NPC starts in opposing lane, turns left
                          | across ego's path starting t=2.5 s

Coordinates are resolved ONCE per run from real carla.Map waypoint
queries (resolve_setup) so scripted geometry is always road-
consistent; seeds inject deterministic spawn selection.
"""

import math
import random
from typing import List, Tuple

import numpy as np


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# generic helpers


def _chain(wp, total_m: float, step: float = 1.0) -> np.ndarray:
    """Forward waypoint chain [(x, y, z, yaw_deg, arc_m)].

    Row index i corresponds to arc i*step from the starting waypoint;
    walking stops where wp.next() is exhausted (dead ends).
    """
    pts = [(wp.transform.location.x, wp.transform.location.y,
            wp.transform.location.z, wp.transform.rotation.yaw, 0.0)]
    acc = 0.0
    prev = pts[0]
    while acc < total_m:
        nxt = wp.next(step)
        if not nxt:
            break
        wp = nxt[0]
        wl = wp.transform.location
        acc += math.hypot(wl.x - prev[0], wl.y - prev[1])
        prev = (wl.x, wl.y, wl.z, wp.transform.rotation.yaw, acc)
        pts.append(prev)
    return np.asarray(pts, dtype=float)


def _at(chain: np.ndarray, i: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Chain row with clamped index."""
    return chain[int(np.clip(i, 0, len(chain) - 1))]


def _yaw_deg(a, b) -> float:
    return math.degrees(math.atan2(b[1] - a[1], b[0] - a[0]))


def _carla_T(x: float, y: float, z: float, yaw: float):
    import carla
    return carla.Transform(carla.Location(x=x, y=y, z=z),
                           carla.Rotation(yaw=yaw))


def _append_curve(pts: List[Tuple], b0: Tuple, b1: Tuple, b2: Tuple,
                  n: int = 14) -> None:
    """Append a quadratic-bezier arc (b0 -> b1 -> b2); yaw per segment."""
    for t in np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n + 1)[1:]:
        q = (1 - t) ** 2 * np.array(b0[:2]) + \
            2 * (1 - t) * t * np.array(b1[:2]) + \
            t * t * np.array(b2[:2])
        pts.append((float(q[0]), float(q[1]), b2[2], 0.0))
    for i in range(1, len(pts)):
        pts[i - 1] = tuple(list(pts[i - 1][:3]) + [_yaw_deg(
            pts[i - 1], pts[i])])
    pts[-1] = tuple(list(pts[-1][:3]) + [pts[-2][3]])


class _Poly:
    """Arc-length-parameterized polyline [(x,y,z,yaw)]."""

    def __init__(self, points: np.ndarray):
        self.pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)
        seg = self.pts[1:, :2] - self.pts[:-1, :2]
        self.seg_len = np.hypot(seg[:, 0], seg[:, 1])
        self.arc = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(self.seg_len)])

    def pose(self, s: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float, float]:
        s = float(np.clip(s, 0.0, self.arc[-1]))
        i = int(np.searchsorted(self.arc, s, "right")) - 1
        i = min(max(i, 0), len(self.pts) - 2)
        a, b = self.pts[i], self.pts[i + 1]
        den = self.seg_len[i] if self.seg_len[i] > 1e-9 else 1.0
        f = (s - self.arc[i]) / den
        xy = a[:2] + (b[:2] - a[:2]) * float(f)
        z = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * float(f)
        return float(xy[0]), float(xy[1]), float(z), float(_yaw_deg(a, b))


class _Kinematic:
    """Teleported actor following a polyline at a constant speed."""

    def __init__(self, actor, poly: _Poly, speed: float):
        self.actor = actor
        self.poly = poly
        self.speed = speed
        try:
            actor.set_simulate_physics(False)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def tick(self, sim_time_s: float) -> None:
        x, y, z, yaw = self.poly.pose(self.speed * sim_time_s)
        self.actor.set_transform(_carla_T(x, y, z, yaw))


def _reset_all_lights(world) -> None:
    """Deterministic reset of every map light to a plain cycle.

    Cross-run state must not leak: pinning red for one scenario would
    otherwise bleed into the next scenario's checks.
    """
    for a in world.get_actors():
        if "traffic_light" not in a.type_id:
            continue
        try:
            a.set_green_time(20.0)
            a.set_yellow_time(3.0)
            a.set_red_time(20.0)
            a.reset_group()
        except Exception:
            pass


class _ScriptedScenario:
    name = "?"
    description = ""

    def __init__(self):
        self.kin: List[_Kinematic] = []
        self.red_lights: List = []
        self._light_snapshot = []

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed: int) -> Tuple:
        raise NotImplementedError

    def tick(self, world, sim_time_s: float) -> None:
        for k in self.kin:
            k.tick(sim_time_s)

    def cleanup(self, world) -> None:
        for k in self.kin:
            try:
                k.actor.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self.kin = []
        # restore any lights we pinned red
        for li, st, rt in self._light_snapshot:
            try:
                li.set_red_time(rt)
                li.set_state(st)
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            for li, _st, _rt in self._light_snapshot:
                li.reset_group()
        except Exception:
            pass
        self._light_snapshot = []
        self.red_lights = []
        _reset_all_lights(world)

    def _signal_centers(self, world) -> List:
        out = []
        for a in world.get_actors():
            if "traffic_light" not in a.type_id:
                continue
            try:
                tv = a.trigger_volume.location
                out.append((tv.x, tv.y))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return out

    def _route_signal_free(self, chain, centers, radius: float = 45.0):
        """True when no chained waypoint enters a signalized cluster."""
        if not centers:
            return True
        for (cx, cy) in centers:
            d = np.hypot(chain[:, 0] - cx, chain[:, 1] - cy)
            if float(d.min()) <= radius:
                return False
        return True

    def _pick_spawn(self, world, seed: int):
        """Spawn whose 130 m route avoids signalized clusters, so that
        S1/S3/S4/S5 evidence is dominated by the scripted event rather
        than by traffic-light phase timing (S2 pins red deliberately).
        """
        centers = self._signal_centers(world)
        mpc = world.get_map()
        spawns = sorted(mpc.get_spawn_points(),
                        key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))
        if not spawns:
            raise RuntimeError("map has no spawn points")
        rng = random.Random(seed)
        off = rng.randrange(len(spawns))
        ordered = spawns[off:] + spawns[:off]
        for sp in ordered:
            chain = _chain(mpc.get_waypoint(sp.location), 130.0)
            if len(chain) > 20 and self._route_signal_free(chain, centers):
                return sp
        raise RuntimeError("no signal-free spawn")

    def _spawn_candidates(self, world, seed: int, limit: int = 8):
        """Seeded-rotation spawn candidates for spawn-failure retries.
        Signalized-cluster routes are skipped so lights never gate the
        scripted choreography."""
        centers = self._signal_centers(world)
        mpc = world.get_map()
        spawns = sorted(mpc.get_spawn_points(),
                        key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))
        if not spawns:
            raise RuntimeError("map has no spawn points")
        rng = random.Random(seed)
        off = rng.randrange(len(spawns))
        ordered = spawns[off:] + spawns[:off]
        out = []
        for sp in ordered:
            chain = _chain(mpc.get_waypoint(sp.location), 130.0)
            if len(chain) > 20 and self._route_signal_free(chain, centers):
                out.append(sp)
                if len(out) >= limit:
                    break
        if not out:
            raise RuntimeError("no signal-free spawn candidates")
        return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# S1


class S1FreeDrive(_ScriptedScenario):
    name = "S1"
    description = "free drive, no ambient traffic"

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed):
        return self._pick_spawn(world, seed), (lambda w, ego, bridge: None)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# S2: red-light run


class S2RedLightRun(_ScriptedScenario):
    name = "S2"
    description = ("signalized junction held RED per tick; ego spawns "
                   "so the stop line is 55-95 m ahead")

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed):
        import carla
        mp = world.get_map()
        lights = [a for a in world.get_actors()
                  if "traffic_light" in a.type_id]
        if not lights:
            raise RuntimeError("S2: no traffic lights on map")
        # a genuinely signalized approach: stop line near the light's
        # own trigger center (CARLA light actors reference approaches
        # across the map; only physical approaches count here)
        signalized = []
        for li in lights:
            tc = li.trigger_volume.location
            try:
                swps = li.get_stop_waypoints() or []
            except Exception:
                swps = []
            for w in swps:
                loc = (w.transform.location.x, w.transform.location.y,
                       w.transform.location.z)
                if math.hypot(loc[0] - tc.x, loc[1] - tc.y) <= 30.0:
                    signalized.append((li, loc))
        if not signalized:
            raise RuntimeError("S2: no signalized approaches")

        cand = []
        for sp in sorted(mp.get_spawn_points(),
                         key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y)):
            wp0 = mp.get_waypoint(sp.location)
            chain = _chain(wp0, 130.0)
            if len(chain) < 10:
                continue
            for _, loc in signalized:
                d = np.hypot(chain[:, 0] - loc[0], chain[:, 1] - loc[1])
                i_near = int(np.argmin(d))
                if float(d[i_near]) < 6.0 and 55.0 <= chain[i_near, 4] <= 95.0:
                    cand.append(sp)
        if not cand:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "S2: no spawn with a signalized stop line 55-95 m ahead")
        rng = random.Random(seed)
        sp = cand[rng.randrange(len(cand))]

        # hold the whole junction cluster RED from t=0 (lights on this
        # server have no set_freeze; re-asserted per tick below)
        chain = _chain(mp.get_waypoint(sp.location), 130.0)
        chosen_tc = None
        best_d = 1e9
        for li, loc in signalized:
            d = np.hypot(chain[:, 0] - loc[0], chain[:, 1] - loc[1])
            i_near = int(np.argmin(d))
            if float(d[i_near]) < 6.0 and chain[i_near, 4] <= best_d and \
                    float(chain[i_near, 4]) <= 95.0 + 30.0:
                best_d = float(chain[i_near, 4])
                chosen_tc = li.trigger_volume.location
        if chosen_tc is None:
            raise RuntimeError("S2: chosen spawn lost its approach")
        for other in lights:
            otc = other.trigger_volume.location
            if math.hypot(otc.x - chosen_tc.x, otc.y - chosen_tc.y) <= 30.0:
                try:
                    self._light_snapshot.append(
                        (other, other.get_state(), other.get_red_time()))
                except Exception:
                    pass
                try:
                    # pin the group's red phase: CARLA group timers
                    # otherwise exit red after their default duration
                    # (~4 min), leaving yellow/green too long to survive
                    # per-tick re-assertion
                    other.set_red_time(1e6)
                    other.reset_group()
                except Exception:
                    pass
                try:
                    other.set_state(carla.TrafficLightState.Red)
                    self.red_lights.append(other)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return sp, (lambda w, ego, bridge: None)

    def tick(self, world, sim_time_s: float) -> None:
        import carla
        for li in self.red_lights:
            try:
                li.set_state(carla.TrafficLightState.Red)
            except Exception:
                pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# S3: cut-in (kinematic NPC)


class S3CutIn(_ScriptedScenario):
    name = "S3"
    description = ("NPC 2 lanes left, ~25-40 m ahead, slower; cuts "
                   "into ego's lane starting at t=3 s")

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed):
        mp = world.get_map()
        bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
        npc_bp = bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3")
        last_err = None
        for sp in self._spawn_candidates(world, seed):
            wp0 = mp.get_waypoint(sp.location)
            chain = _chain(wp0, 45.0)
            wpn = wp0
            for _ in range(2):
                nxt = wpn.get_left_lane()
                if nxt is None:
                    break
                wpn = nxt
            if wpn.lane_id == wp0.lane_id:
                one = wp0.get_left_lane()
                if one is not None:
                    wpn = one
            nchain = _chain(wpn, 60.0)
            if len(nchain) < 15:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S3: NPC lane chain too short")
                continue
            z = float(chain[0, 2])
            i0 = int(np.argmax(nchain[:, 4] >= 30.0))
            npc = world.try_spawn_actor(
                npc_bp, _carla_T(nchain[i0, 0], nchain[i0, 1],
                                 z + 0.05, nchain[i0, 3]))
            if npc is None:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S3: NPC spawn failed")
                continue
            # path: own lane ~4 m (1 s at 4 m/s), then swing into ego's
            # lane touching down at ego-arc 26 m at t ~ 2.4 s, i.e.
            # ~1 s before the 7 m/s ego arrives -> forced braking event
            pts = [(float(p[0]), float(p[1]), z, 0.0)
                   for p in nchain[i0:i0 + 2]]
            land = _at(chain, 26)
            _append_curve(
                pts, pts[-1],
                (0.5 * (pts[-1][0] + land[0]),
                 0.5 * (pts[-1][1] + land[1])),
                land, n=12)
            i_land = int(np.argmin(np.hypot(chain[:, 0] - land[0],
                                            chain[:, 1] - land[1])))
            pts.extend([tuple(p[:4]) for p in chain[i_land:40]])
            self.kin.append(_Kinematic(npc, _Poly(np.asarray(pts)), 4.0))
            return sp, (lambda w, ego, bridge: None)
        raise last_err or RuntimeError("S3: no workable spawn")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# S4: jaywalker (kinematic walker)


class S4Jaywalker(_ScriptedScenario):
    name = "S4"
    description = ("walker crosses ego's path 15 m ahead outside a "
                   "marked crossing, 1.2 m/s")

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed):
        import carla
        mp = world.get_map()
        bp = world.get_blueprint_library().filter("walker.pedestrian.0001")
        last_err = None
        for sp in self._spawn_candidates(world, seed):
            wp0 = mp.get_waypoint(sp.location)
            chain = _chain(wp0, 40.0)
            if len(chain) < 20:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S4: ego chain too short")
                continue
            z = float(chain[0, 2])
            c = _at(chain, 15)
            wp15 = mp.get_waypoint(
                carla.Location(x=float(c[0]), y=float(c[1]), z=z))
            yaw = math.radians(wp15.transform.rotation.yaw)
            perp = np.array([-math.sin(yaw), math.cos(yaw)])
            center = np.array([float(c[0]), float(c[1])])
            a = center + perp * 5.5
            b = center - perp * 5.5
            pts = [(float(a[0]), float(a[1]), z, _yaw_deg(a, b))]
            for k in range(1, 12):
                p = a + (b - a) * (k / 11.0)
                pts.append((float(p[0]), float(p[1]), z, _yaw_deg(a, b)))
            walker = world.try_spawn_actor(
                bp[0], _carla_T(float(a[0]), float(a[1]), z + 0.30, 0.0))
            if walker is None:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S4: walker spawn failed")
                continue
            self.kin.append(_Kinematic(walker, _Poly(np.asarray(pts)), 1.2))
            return sp, (lambda w, ego, bridge: None)
        raise last_err or RuntimeError("S4: no workable spawn")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# S5: oncoming left turn (kinematic NPC)


class S5OncomingLeft(_ScriptedScenario):
    name = "S5"
    description = ("NPC enters opposing lane 15 m before its turn; "
                   "starts left turn across ego's path at t=2.5 s")

    def resolve_setup(self, world, seed):
        mp = world.get_map()
        bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
        npc_bp = bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3")
        last_err = None
        for sp in self._spawn_candidates(world, seed):
            wp0 = mp.get_waypoint(sp.location)
            chain = _chain(wp0, 45.0)
            if len(chain) < 30:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S5: ego chain too short")
                continue
            z = float(chain[0, 2])
            wpo = wp0.get_left_lane()
            if wpo is None:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S5: no opposing lane at spawn")
                continue
            ochain = _chain(wpo, 45.0)
            turn = _at(chain, 10)
            land = _at(chain, 22)
            d = np.hypot(ochain[:, 0] - turn[0], ochain[:, 1] - turn[1])
            i_turn = int(np.argmin(d))
            pts = [(float(p[0]), float(p[1]), z, 0.0)
                   for p in ochain[max(0, i_turn - 15):i_turn + 1]]
            if len(pts) < 3:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S5: opposing chain too short")
                continue
            _append_curve(
                pts, pts[-1],
                (0.5 * (pts[-1][0] + land[0]),
                 0.5 * (pts[-1][1] + land[1]) + 3.5),
                land, n=16)
            i_land = int(np.argmin(np.hypot(chain[:, 0] - land[0],
                                            chain[:, 1] - land[1])))
            pts.extend([tuple(p[:4]) for p in chain[i_land:34]])
            poly = _Poly(np.asarray(pts))
            p0 = poly.pose(0.0)
            npc = world.try_spawn_actor(
                npc_bp, _carla_T(p0[0], p0[1], z + 0.05, p0[3]))
            if npc is None:
                last_err = RuntimeError("S5: NPC spawn failed")
                continue
            self.kin.append(_Kinematic(npc, poly, 6.0))
            return sp, (lambda w, ego, bridge: None)
        raise last_err or RuntimeError("S5: no workable spawn")


_REGISTRY = {s.name: s() for s in
             (S1FreeDrive, S2RedLightRun, S3CutIn, S4Jaywalker,
              S5OncomingLeft)}


def get_scenario(name: str) -> _ScriptedScenario:
    if name not in _REGISTRY:
        raise KeyError(
            f"unknown scenario '{name}' (registered: "
            f"{sorted(_REGISTRY)})")
    return _REGISTRY[name]

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_sign_test.py <==
"""Steering sign calibration (P25-B verification step).

Bridge convention: steer command +1 should turn the planner-ego
(y LEFT-positive) heading counterclockwise. This test applies
steer=+0.8, measures the planner-ego heading rotation, and prints
SIGN OK (+evidence) or SIGN FLIP NEEDED. Run once, then frozen.
"""

import sys
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(__import__("pathlib").Path(__file__).resolve().parent))


def main() -> int:
    import carla_launch

    if not carla_launch.launch_server(None):
        return 1
    import carla

    client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
    client.set_timeout(300.0)
    world = client.get_world()
    s = world.get_settings()
    s.synchronous_mode = True
    s.fixed_delta_seconds = 0.05
    world.apply_settings(s)

    bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
    spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
                   key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]
    veh = world.try_spawn_actor(bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"), spawn)
    try:
        for _ in range(10):
            world.tick()
        h0 = veh.get_transform().get_forward_vector()
        for _ in range(30):
            veh.apply_control(carla.VehicleControl(steer=0.8, throttle=0.4))
            world.tick()
        h1 = veh.get_transform().get_forward_vector()

        p0 = np.array([h0.x, -h0.y])
        p1 = np.array([h1.x, -h1.y])
        cross = p0[0] * p1[1] - p0[1] * p1[0]
        angle = np.degrees(np.arctan2(cross, float(np.dot(p0, p1))))
        w0 = np.array([h0.x, h0.y])
        w1 = np.array([h1.x, h1.y])
        wcross = w0[0] * w1[1] - w0[1] * w1[0]
        wangle = np.degrees(np.arctan2(wcross, float(np.dot(w0, w1))))
        print(f"steer=+0.8 -> planner-ego heading change: {angle:+.2f} deg "
              f"(CARLA world: {wangle:+.2f} deg)")
        if angle > 5:
            print("SIGN OK: +steer turns planner-LEFT; bridge steer_sign="
                  "+1 is correct")
            return 0
        if angle < -5:
            print("SIGN FLIP NEEDED: set bridge cfg steer_sign=-1")
            return 1
        print("SIGN AMBIGUOUS: vehicle may not have moved enough")
        return 2
    finally:
        try:
            veh.destroy()
        except Exception:
            pass
        s = world.get_settings()
        s.synchronous_mode = False
        s.fixed_delta_seconds = None
        world.apply_settings(s)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_smoke.py <==
"""One-off CARLA smoke test: connect, spawn ego, read one camera."""

import sys
import time
import numpy as np

import carla

client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
client.set_timeout(300.0)
print("server version:", client.get_server_version())
world = client.get_world()  # default startup map (no load_world yet)
print("map:", world.get_map().name)
bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
               key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]
ego = world.spawn_actor(bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"), spawn)
cam_bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.camera.rgb")
cam_bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", "704")
cam_bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", "256")
cam_bp.set_attribute("fov", "90")
cam = world.spawn_actor(cam_bp, carla.Transform(), attach_to=ego)
frames = []
cam.listen(frames.append)
deadline = time.time() + 30.0
while not frames and time.time() < deadline:
    time.sleep(0.5)
assert frames, "no camera frame within 30 s"
img = frames[0]
arr = np.frombuffer(img.raw_data, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(
    img.height, img.width, 4)[:, :, :3]
print(f"camera frame: {arr.shape} dtype={arr.dtype}")
print(f"ego at: {ego.get_location()}")
cam.destroy()
ego.destroy()
print("SMOKE OK")

==> asp-safety/experiments/carla_vram_probe.py <==
"""VRAM gate probe for a visible (windowed, Epic) CARLA server.

Measures the shared-GPU budget exactly as the harness will spend it:
server rendering (window Epic) + 6-camera rig + planner model + one
warm predict_frame, then compares free VRAM against the >=1.5 GB gate
(P25-A retreat ladder).

    python experiments/carla_vram_probe.py --quality Epic --windowed
    python experiments/carla_vram_probe.py --quality Epic --windowed \
        --stream-pool 1200
    python experiments/carla_vram_probe.py --quality Low

Kill any previous server first (launch flags only apply to a fresh
process):  python experiments/carla_launch.py --kill

Final machine-readable line:
    VRAM_PROBE quality=... windowed=... stream_pool=...
               free_post_gb=... gate=... status=PASS|FAIL
"""

import argparse
import math
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import carla  # noqa: E402
import carla_launch  # noqa: E402
from carla_probe import (  # noqa: E402
    CAM_CONFIGS, IMG_H, IMG_W, build_extrinsic, transform_to_mat,
    try_spawn_near, world_to_ego_nu)

CAM_ORDER = ["FRONT", "FRONT_LEFT", "FRONT_RIGHT",
             "BACK", "BACK_LEFT", "BACK_RIGHT"]


def vram_free_gb() -> float:
    import torch
    free, _ = torch.cuda.mem_get_info()
    return free / 2**30


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--quality", default="Epic", choices=["Low", "Epic"])
    ap.add_argument("--windowed", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--stream-pool", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--map", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--gate", type=float, default=1.5,
                    help="GB that must remain free after warm predict")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    t0 = time.time()
    if not carla_launch.launch_server(
            args.map, quality=args.quality, windowed=args.windowed,
            stream_pool=args.stream_pool):
        print(f"VRAM_PROBE ... status=FAIL (no server; quality="
              f"{args.quality} windowed={args.windowed})")
        return 1
    client = carla.Client("localhost", 2000)
    client.set_timeout(300.0)
    world = client.get_world()
    print("map:", world.get_map().name, flush=True)

    settings = world.get_settings()
    settings.synchronous_mode = True
    settings.fixed_delta_seconds = 0.05
    world.apply_settings(settings)

    bp_lib = world.get_blueprint_library()
    spawn = sorted(world.get_map().get_spawn_points(),
                   key=lambda t: (t.location.x, t.location.y))[0]
    actors, cams, queues = [], [], {}
    pool_args = ("" if args.stream_pool is None
                 else f" pool={args.stream_pool}")
    tag = f"{args.quality}{pool_args} windowed={args.windowed}"
    try:
        ego = try_spawn_near(world, bp_lib,
                             bp_lib.find("vehicle.tesla.model3"),
                             spawn.location)
        if ego is None:
            print(f"VRAM_PROBE {tag} status=FAIL (ego spawn failed)")
            return 1
        actors.append(ego)
        for name, yaw, fov, dx, dy, dz in CAM_CONFIGS:
            bp = bp_lib.find("sensor.camera.rgb")
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_x", str(IMG_W))
            bp.set_attribute("image_size_y", str(IMG_H))
            bp.set_attribute("fov", str(fov))
            transform = carla.Transform(
                carla.Location(x=dx, y=dy, z=dz),
                carla.Rotation(pitch=-8.0 if yaw == 0.0 else 0.0,
                               yaw=yaw))
            cam = world.spawn_actor(bp, transform, attach_to=ego)
            cams.append((name, cam))
        actors.extend(c for _, c in cams)

        queues = {name: [] for name, _ in cams}
        for name, cam in cams:
            cam.listen(queues[name].append)
        deadline = time.time() + 60.0
        while time.time() < deadline and any(
                len(q) == 0 for q in queues.values()):
            world.tick()
            time.sleep(0.05)
        missing = [n for n, q in queues.items() if not q]
        if missing:
            print(f"VRAM_PROBE {tag} status=FAIL (no frames {missing})")
            return 1

        free_server = vram_free_gb()
        print(f"free VRAM (server+window+rig): {free_server:.2f} GB",
              flush=True)

        model_path = (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
                      / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth")
        sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "src"))
        from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402
        planner = SparseDrivePlanner(str(model_path), device="cuda")
        free_model = vram_free_gb()
        print(f"free VRAM (after model load): {free_model:.2f} GB",
              flush=True)

        t_world_veh = transform_to_mat(ego.get_transform())
        ego2global = world_to_ego_nu(t_world_veh)
        K = np.zeros((6, 3, 3), dtype=np.float32)
        C = np.zeros((6, 4, 4), dtype=np.float32)
        stack = np.zeros((6, 3, IMG_H, IMG_W), dtype=np.float32)
        for idx, (name, cam) in enumerate(cams):
            fov = next(f for n, _, f, _, _, _ in CAM_CONFIGS
                       if n == name)
            fx = (IMG_W / 2) / math.tan(math.radians(fov) / 2)
            cx, cy = IMG_W / 2.0, IMG_H / 2.0
            K[idx] = [[fx, 0, cx], [0, fx, cy], [0, 0, 1]]
            C[idx] = build_extrinsic(t_world_veh,
                                     transform_to_mat(
                                         cam.get_transform()))
            img = queues[name][-1]
            arr = np.frombuffer(img.raw_data, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(
                IMG_H, IMG_W, 4)
            stack[idx] = np.ascontiguousarray(
                arr[:, :, 2::-1].transpose(2, 0, 1)).astype(np.float32)

        planner.begin_sequence()
        t_inf = time.time()
        out = planner.predict_frame(stack, K, C,
                                    ego2global.astype(np.float32),
                                    time.time())
        dt_inf = time.time() - t_inf
        planner.end_sequence()
        free_post = vram_free_gb()
        traj = out["trajectory"]
        print(f"free VRAM (after warm frame): {free_post:.2f} GB "
              f"(warm wall {dt_inf:.2f} s; traj {traj.shape})", flush=True)

        status = "PASS" if free_post >= args.gate else "FAIL"
        print(f"VRAM_PROBE quality={args.quality} "
              f"windowed={args.windowed} "
              f"stream_pool={args.stream_pool} "
              f"free_post_gb={free_post:.2f} "
              f"gate={args.gate:.2f} status={status}")
        return 0 if status == "PASS" else 1
    finally:
        for _, cam in cams:
            try:
                cam.stop()
                cam.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        for a in actors:
            try:
                if a and a.is_alive:
                    a.destroy()
            except Exception:
                pass
        settings = world.get_settings()
        settings.synchronous_mode = False
        settings.fixed_delta_seconds = None
        world.apply_settings(settings)
        print(f"probe wall {time.time() - t0:.1f} s "
              "(server kept alive)", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
