==> asp-safety/experiments/cmd_sweep.py <==
"""Nav-command sweep (P-13): which cmd head matches which GT geometry?

For each mini scene, runs real inference under cmd 0/1/2 and reports L2
vs GT plus the GT lateral displacement at +3 s. Goals:
  1. Verify the index convention (NUM_COMMANDS claims 0=straight,1=left,2=right).
  2. Measure how much of the 11.5 m L2 baseline is wrong-intent vs motion error.

Usage: python experiments/cmd_sweep.py [--scenes 0,1,2]
"""

import sys
import time
import argparse
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader
from evaluation import MetricsCalculator
from run_calibration import interp_gt


def gt_intent(gt: np.ndarray) -> str:
    """Intent label from GT lateral displacement at +3 s (ego frame, y=left)."""
    y3 = float(gt[min(3, len(gt) - 1), 1])
    if y3 > 2.0:
        return "left"
    if y3 < -2.0:
        return "right"
    return "straight"


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--data-root", default="C:/data/sets/nuscenes")
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", default=None)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    base = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    loader = NuScenesLoader(args.data_root, version="v1.0-mini")
    scenes = ([int(x) for x in args.scenes.split(",")]
              if args.scenes else list(range(loader.num_scenes)))
    planner = SparseDrivePlanner(
        str(base / "SparseDrive" / "ckpt" / "sparsedrive_stage2.pth"),
        device="cuda")

    cmds = [np.array([1, 0, 0], dtype=np.float32),
            np.array([0, 1, 0], dtype=np.float32),
            np.array([0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32)]
    print("scene | gt_y3 | intent   | L2(cmd0) L2(cmd1) L2(cmd2) | winner")
    for idx in scenes:
        sample = loader.get_scene_sample(idx)
        gt = loader.get_trajectory(idx)
        intent = gt_intent(gt)
        y3 = float(gt[min(3, len(gt) - 1), 1])
        l2s = []
        for c in cmds:
            out = planner.predict(
                sample['images'].astype(np.float32),
                sample['intrinsics'], sample['extrinsics'],
                sample['ego2global'], sample['timestamp'], nav_cmd=c)
            pred = out['trajectory']
            pred_times = (np.arange(len(pred)) + 1) * 0.5
            l2s.append(MetricsCalculator.l2_error(
                pred, interp_gt(gt, pred_times)))
            time.sleep(0.2)
        winner = int(np.argmin(l2s))
        print(f"{idx:5d} | {y3:+6.2f} | {intent:8s} | "
              f"{l2s[0]:7.2f} {l2s[1]:7.2f} {l2s[2]:7.2f} | cmd{winner}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==> asp-safety/experiments/coverage_probe.py <==
"""Coverage probe (T-2/P-9): catalog finding names vs test assertions."""

import re
import glob

findings = set()
for f in glob.glob("rules/*.lp"):
    src = open(f).read()
    for m in re.finditer(
            r"(?:violation|hazard|warning)\(\s*([a-z][a-z_0-9]+)", src):
        findings.add(m.group(1))
# insufficient() first arg is a hazard name, not a rule predicate.
findings.discard("emergency_brake")

asserted = set()
for f in glob.glob("tests/test_*.py"):
    src = open(f).read()
    for m in re.finditer(r"['\"]([a-z_]+)['\"]", src):
        if m.group(1) in findings:
            asserted.add(m.group(1))

missing = sorted(findings - asserted)
print(f"catalog findings: {len(findings)}, asserted in tests: {len(asserted)}")
print("MISSING firing assertions:")
for m in missing:
    print("  ", m)

==> asp-safety/experiments/plan_axis_check.py <==
"""Axis-semantics check: model plan columns vs nuScenes GT future.

Question: does SparseDrive final_planning emit [x_forward, y_left]
(default assumption used by the ASP surface) or [y_lateral, x_forward]
(the swap seen on CARLA)?  Each scene's middle sample drives one cold
predict() call; its future ego poses (0.5 s cadence) are decomposed
into forward (ego +x) and lateral-left (ego +y) displacements and
printed next to the plan columns.

Run: python experiments/plan_axis_check.py [scene_start scene_end]
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "src"))

from data_loader import NuScenesLoader  # noqa: E402
from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner  # noqa: E402

DATA = "C:/data/sets/nuscenes"


def main():
    s0 = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0
    s1 = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 5
    loader = NuScenesLoader(DATA, version="v1.0-mini")
    if loader.use_placeholder:
        raise RuntimeError(f"no nuScenes data at {DATA}")
    nusc = loader.nusc

    planner = SparseDrivePlanner(
        str(ROOT / "SparseDrive/ckpt/sparsedrive_stage2.pth"),
        device="cuda")

    for scene_idx in range(s0, min(s1, loader.num_scenes)):
        sample = loader.get_scene_sample(scene_idx)
        token = loader._sample_tokens[scene_idx]
        row = nusc.get("sample", token)

        lidar_sd = nusc.get(
            "sample_data", row["data"]["LIDAR_TOP"])
        ego_now = nusc.get("ego_pose", lidar_sd["ego_pose_token"])
        p_now = np.array(ego_now["translation"], dtype=np.float64)

        # walk the future chain (0.5 s per sample)
        fut, token_f = [], token
        for _ in range(6):
            token_f = nusc.get("sample", token_f)["next"]
            if not token_f:
                break
            r = nusc.get("sample", token_f)
            sd = nusc.get("sample_data", r["data"]["LIDAR_TOP"])
            ep = nusc.get("ego_pose", sd["ego_pose_token"])
            fut.append(np.array(ep["translation"], dtype=np.float64))

        out = planner.predict(
            sample["images"].astype(np.float32),
            sample["intrinsics"], sample["extrinsics"],
            sample["ego2global"], sample["timestamp"])
        plan = out["trajectory"]
        nb = out["boxes"].shape[0]

        print(f"\nscene {scene_idx} (token {token[:8]}) nbox={nb}")
        print("  t     plan[c0]  plan[c1]   gt_fwd   gt_lat"
              "     err_std  err_swap")
        for i in range(1, 4):
            t_s = i * 1.0  # row mapping: row k = (k+1)*0.5 s
            idx = 2 * i - 1  # rows 1,3,5 = 1/2/3 s (replay convention)
            dpart = (fut[idx - 1] - p_now) if idx <= len(fut) else None
            if dpart is not None and sample["ego2global"] is not None:
                e2g = np.asarray(sample["ego2global"], np.float64)
                R = e2g[:3, :3]
                heading = R[:, 0]
                left = R[:, 1]
                fwd = float(dpart @ (heading / np.linalg.norm(heading)))
                lat = float(dpart @ (left / np.linalg.norm(left)))
            else:
                fwd = lat = np.nan
            c0, c1 = float(plan[idx][0]), float(plan[idx][1])
            err_std = (abs(c0 - fwd) + abs(c1 - lat)
                       if not np.isnan(fwd) else float("nan"))
            err_swap = (abs(c0 - lat) + abs(c1 - fwd)
                        if not np.isnan(fwd) else float("nan"))
            print(f"  {t_s:.1f}s  {c0:8.2f}  {c1:8.2f}"
                  f"  {fwd:8.2f}  {lat:8.2f}"
                  f"  {err_std:8.2f}  {err_swap:8.2f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==> asp-safety/experiments/replay_eval.py <==
"""Annotation-replay evaluation (forward plan item 1).

Same schema as run_calibration.py, but agent context comes from
TRACKED annotation histories (loader.get_tracked_agents) instead of
CV-projected single-frame detections. Parked agents (displacement
< 0.5 m) are reported separately and excluded from the moving-only
pass, which answers the parked-vs-live question without box classes.

Predictions: warm multi-frame walk by default (`--cold` reproduces the
old single-frame behavior). Corrector engine selectable (`--corrector
heuristic|qp`) for the P-22 warm tracked-replay ablation.

Usage: python experiments/replay_eval.py [--scenes 0,1] [--out JSON]
"""

import sys
import time
import json
import argparse
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner
from checker import TrajectoryChecker, STEP_DT
from corrector import TrajectoryCorrector, CorrectorQP
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader
from evaluation import MetricsCalculator
from run_calibration import interp_gt


def _count(violations, counts):
    for v in violations:
        if v.get('type', 'violation') != 'warning':
            counts[v['rule']] += 1


def run_replay(data_root, device="cuda", scenes=None,
               rules_dir=None, model_path=None,
               warm=True, corrector_mode="heuristic"):
    base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    if rules_dir is None:
        rules_dir = str(base_dir / "rules")
    if model_path is None:
        model_path = str(
            base_dir / "SparseDrive" / "ckpt" / "sparsedrive_stage2.pth")

    loader = NuScenesLoader(data_root, version="v1.0-mini")
    if loader.use_placeholder:
        raise RuntimeError(f"no nuScenes data at {data_root}")
    if scenes is None:
        scenes = list(range(loader.num_scenes))

    planner = SparseDrivePlanner(model_path, device=device)
    checker = TrajectoryChecker(rules_dir)
    if corrector_mode == "qp":
        corrector = CorrectorQP(checker)
    elif corrector_mode == "heuristic":
        corrector = TrajectoryCorrector(checker)
    else:
        raise ValueError("corrector_mode must be 'heuristic' or 'qp'")

    assert abs(STEP_DT - 1.0) < 1e-9, "thresholds assume STEP_DT=1.0"

    gt_counts = Counter()
    gt_counts_moving = Counter()
    pred_counts = Counter()
    pred_counts_moving = Counter()
    l2_errors = []
    t_plan, t_check, t_correct = [], [], []
    n_gt_safe = n_pred_safe = n_pred_fixed = 0
    n_parked = n_moving = 0

    print(f"Replay eval on {len(scenes)} scenes (device={device}, "
          f"{'warm' if warm else 'cold'} predictions, "
          f"corrector={corrector_mode})...")
    for idx in scenes:
        planner.reset_temporal()
        sample = loader.get_scene_sample(idx)
        gt = loader.get_trajectory(idx)
        map_data = loader.get_map_data(idx)
        agents = loader.get_tracked_agents(idx)
        moving = [a['trajectory'] for a in agents.values()
                  if not a['parked']]
        parked = [a['trajectory'] for a in agents.values()
                  if a['parked']]
        n_moving += len(moving)
        n_parked += len(parked)
        all_agents = moving + parked

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if warm:
            # N-2 warm path (same as run_calibration): walk 4 history
            # frames + eval frame with pose-derived ego status.
            from data_loader import estimate_ego_status
            frames = loader.get_scene_frames(idx, n_past=4)
            planner.begin_sequence()
            out = None
            prev_g, prev_t = None, None
            for fr in frames:
                status = (np.zeros(18, dtype=np.float32)
                          if prev_g is None else estimate_ego_status(
                              prev_g, fr['ego2global'],
                              fr['timestamp'] - prev_t))
                out = planner.predict_frame(
                    fr['images'].astype(np.float32), fr['intrinsics'],
                    fr['extrinsics'], fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp'],
                    ego_status=status)
                prev_g, prev_t = fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp']
            planner.end_sequence()
        else:
            out = planner.predict(
                sample['images'].astype(np.float32),
                sample['intrinsics'], sample['extrinsics'],
                sample['ego2global'], sample['timestamp'])
        t_plan.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        pred = out['trajectory']

        pred_times = (np.arange(len(pred)) + 1) * 0.5
        l2_errors.append(MetricsCalculator.l2_error(
            pred, interp_gt(gt, pred_times)))

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        ok_gt, v_gt = checker.check(
            gt, map_data=map_data, other_vehicles=all_agents or None)
        t_check.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        n_gt_safe += ok_gt
        _count(v_gt, gt_counts)
        _, v_gtm = checker.check(
            gt, map_data=map_data,
            other_vehicles=moving or None)
        _count(v_gtm, gt_counts_moving)

        pred_1s = np.vstack([
            np.zeros((1, 2), dtype=np.float32),
            pred[1::2][:3],
        ])
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        ok_p, v_p = checker.check(
            pred_1s, map_data=map_data,
            other_vehicles=all_agents or None)
        t_check.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        n_pred_safe += ok_p
        _count(v_p, pred_counts)
        _, v_pm = checker.check(
            pred_1s, map_data=map_data,
            other_vehicles=moving or None)
        _count(v_pm, pred_counts_moving)

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            fixed, _ = corrector.correct(
                pred_1s, map_data=map_data,
                other_vehicles=all_agents or None)
            t_correct.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
            ok_f, _ = checker.check(
                fixed, map_data=map_data,
                other_vehicles=all_agents or None)
        except RuntimeError as e:
            t_correct.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
            ok_f = False
            print(f"    corrector gave up: {e}")
        n_pred_fixed += ok_f
        print(f"  scene {idx}: L2={l2_errors[-1]:.2f}m "
              f"agents={len(all_agents)} (moving {len(moving)}) "
              f"gt_safe={ok_gt} pred_safe={ok_p} fixed={ok_f}")

    summary = {
        'scenes': len(scenes),
        'horizon_s': 3,
        'horizon_steps_pred': 4,
        'agents': 'tracked-annotations',
        'warm_predictions': bool(warm),
        'corrector_mode': corrector_mode,
        'n_tracked_total': n_moving + n_parked,
        'n_moving': n_moving,
        'n_parked': n_parked,
        'l2_mean': float(np.mean(l2_errors)),
        'l2_values': [float(x) for x in l2_errors],
        'gt_safe_rate': n_gt_safe / len(scenes),
        'pred_safe_rate': n_pred_safe / len(scenes),
        'pred_fixed_rate': n_pred_fixed / len(scenes),
        'gt_rule_counts': dict(gt_counts),
        'gt_rule_counts_moving_only': dict(gt_counts_moving),
        'pred_rule_counts': dict(pred_counts),
        'pred_rule_counts_moving_only': dict(pred_counts_moving),
        'ms_planner_mean': float(np.mean(t_plan)),
        'ms_checker_mean': float(np.mean(t_check)),
        'ms_corrector_mean': float(np.mean(t_correct)),
    }
    print("\n=== Replay baseline (tracked agents) ===")
    print(f"  agents: {n_moving + n_parked} tracked "
          f"({n_moving} moving, {n_parked} parked)")
    print(f"  L2 mean: {summary['l2_mean']:.2f} m")
    print(f"  GT safe rate: {summary['gt_safe_rate']:.2f}")
    print(f"  pred safe rate: {summary['pred_safe_rate']:.2f}")
    print(f"  pred fixed rate: {summary['pred_fixed_rate']:.2f}")
    print("  GT rule hits:")
    for rule, cnt in gt_counts.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print("  GT rule hits (moving agents only):")
    for rule, cnt in gt_counts_moving.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print("  pred rule hits (all agents):")
    for rule, cnt in pred_counts.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print("  pred rule hits (moving agents only):")
    for rule, cnt in pred_counts_moving.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print(f"  timing ms planner/checker/corrector: "
          f"{summary['ms_planner_mean']:.1f} / "
          f"{summary['ms_checker_mean']:.1f} / "
          f"{summary['ms_corrector_mean']:.1f}")
    return summary


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Annotation-replay evaluation")
    ap.add_argument("--data-root", default="C:/data/sets/nuscenes")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", default=None,
                    help="comma-separated scene indices, default all")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="JSON summary path")
    ap.add_argument("--cold", action="store_true",
                    help="single-frame cold predictions (default is the "
                         "N-2 warm path)")
    ap.add_argument("--corrector", default="heuristic",
                    choices=["heuristic", "qp"],
                    help="correction engine: heuristic fixpoint or "
                         "CorrectorQP constrained solve + re-validation")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    scenes = ([int(x) for x in args.scenes.split(",")]
              if args.scenes else None)
    summary = run_replay(args.data_root, args.device, scenes,
                         warm=not args.cold,
                         corrector_mode=args.corrector)
    if args.out:
        Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
        print(f"wrote {args.out}")

==> asp-safety/experiments/run_calibration.py <==
"""Open-loop calibration harness (P-5 option b, v1 baseline).

For each nuScenes mini scene this script measures, on REAL data:
  - L2(pred, GT): pred is 6x0.5 s waypoints; GT is interpolated to the
    same 0.5 s grid for comparison.
  - Rule findings on GT (6x1.0 s waypoints, T=0 is now): a proxy for the
    FALSE-POSITIVE rate, since log trajectories are assumed safe-ish.
  - Rule findings on pred RESAMPLED to the checker grid: T=0 is the
    current ego pose ([0, 0] in ego frame) plus pred indices 1,3,5 as
    T=1,2,3 (N-3a anchor). The checker contract is STEP_DT=1.0 s (see
    checker.STEP_DT); feeding raw 0.5 s waypoints would halve every
    computed speed (P-1). Without the T=0 anchor, pred[1] (true +1.0 s)
    would be asserted as T=0 ("now"), shifting every speed/braking/TTC
    computation by a second.
  - Horizon: the resampled pred path spans true 0..3 s (4 points), not
    0..5 s -- a product limitation surfaced here, not hidden: blind
    beyond 3 s. Every summary carries horizon_s=3 so GT-vs-pred
    comparisons stay honest.
  - Timing split: planner (GPU) vs checker vs corrector.

Output: per-rule finding table + JSON summary for threshold tuning.
GT findings point at over-sensitive thresholds; pred findings point at
planner behavior. Neither is ground truth -- both are inputs to the
calibration decision, not conclusions.
"""

import sys
import time
import json
import argparse
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter, defaultdict

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner
from checker import TrajectoryChecker, STEP_DT
from corrector import TrajectoryCorrector, CorrectorQP
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader
from evaluation import MetricsCalculator
from pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline


def interp_gt(gt: np.ndarray, times: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linear-interpolate GT (1 s grid from t=0) to query times."""
    gt_t = np.arange(len(gt), dtype=float)
    x = np.interp(times, gt_t, gt[:, 0].astype(float))
    y = np.interp(times, gt_t, gt[:, 1].astype(float))
    return np.column_stack([x, y]).astype(np.float32)


def run_calibration(data_root, device="cuda", scenes=None,
                    rules_dir=None, model_path=None, extrapolate=False,
                    dump_trajs=None, warm=True,
                    corrector_mode="heuristic"):
    base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    if rules_dir is None:
        rules_dir = str(base_dir / "rules")
    if model_path is None:
        model_path = str(
            base_dir / "SparseDrive" / "ckpt" / "sparsedrive_stage2.pth")

    loader = NuScenesLoader(data_root, version="v1.0-mini")
    if loader.use_placeholder:
        raise RuntimeError(f"no nuScenes data at {data_root}")
    if scenes is None:
        scenes = list(range(loader.num_scenes))

    planner = SparseDrivePlanner(model_path, device=device)
    checker = TrajectoryChecker(rules_dir)
    if corrector_mode == "qp":
        corrector = CorrectorQP(checker)
    elif corrector_mode == "heuristic":
        corrector = TrajectoryCorrector(checker)
    else:
        raise ValueError("corrector_mode must be 'heuristic' or 'qp'")

    assert abs(STEP_DT - 1.0) < 1e-9, "thresholds assume STEP_DT=1.0"

    gt_rule_counts = Counter()
    pred_rule_counts = Counter()
    pred_rule_counts_meas = Counter()  # N-3(b): violations at T<=3
    pred_rule_counts_extrap = Counter()  # N-3(b): violations at T>=4
    l2_errors = []
    t_plan, t_check, t_correct = [], [], []
    n_gt_safe = n_pred_safe = n_pred_fixed = 0

    print(f"Calibrating on {len(scenes)} scenes (device={device}, "
          f"{'warm' if warm else 'cold'} predictions, "
          f"corrector={corrector_mode})...")
    for idx in scenes:
        planner.reset_temporal()  # scenes are independent; no carryover
        sample = loader.get_scene_sample(idx)
        gt = loader.get_trajectory(idx)
        map_data = loader.get_map_data(idx)

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        if warm:
            # N-2 warm path: walk 4 history frames + eval frame with
            # pose-derived ego status; evaluate the last output.
            from data_loader import estimate_ego_status
            frames = loader.get_scene_frames(idx, n_past=4)
            planner.begin_sequence()
            out = None
            prev_g, prev_t = None, None
            for fr in frames:
                status = (np.zeros(18, dtype=np.float32)
                          if prev_g is None else estimate_ego_status(
                              prev_g, fr['ego2global'],
                              fr['timestamp'] - prev_t))
                out = planner.predict_frame(
                    fr['images'].astype(np.float32), fr['intrinsics'],
                    fr['extrinsics'], fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp'],
                    ego_status=status)
                prev_g, prev_t = fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp']
            planner.end_sequence()
        else:
            out = planner.predict(
                sample['images'].astype(np.float32),
                sample['intrinsics'], sample['extrinsics'],
                sample['ego2global'], sample['timestamp'])
        t_plan.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        pred = out['trajectory']

        # L2 on the model's native 0.5 s grid (GT interpolated).
        pred_times = (np.arange(len(pred)) + 1) * 0.5
        l2_errors.append(MetricsCalculator.l2_error(
            pred, interp_gt(gt, pred_times)))

        # GT check at native 1 s steps. Agent context included: the log
        # ego should not collide with detected traffic either (R4 FP
        # check). Detections are single-frame; project them over the GT
        # horizon the same way.
        gt_agents = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            out.get('boxes'), out.get('velocities'), out.get('scores'),
            len(gt))
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        ok_gt, v_gt = checker.check(gt, map_data=map_data,
                                    other_vehicles=gt_agents or None)
        t_check.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        n_gt_safe += ok_gt
        for v in v_gt:
            if v.get('type', 'violation') != 'warning':
                gt_rule_counts[v['rule']] += 1

        # Pred check resampled to the checker grid (N-3a): T=0 is the
        # current ego pose, then pred indices 1,3,5 (true +1/+2/+3 s)
        # as T=1,2,3. Native 6x0.5 s schema is asserted, not assumed.
        assert len(pred) == 6 and pred.shape[1] == 2, \
            f"native pred schema drifted: {pred.shape}"
        pred_1s = np.vstack([
            np.zeros((1, 2), dtype=np.float32),  # T=0: ego pose, now
            pred[1::2][:3],                      # T=1,2,3: true +1/+2/+3 s
        ])
        horizon_s = 3
        if extrapolate:
            # N-3(b) LABELED experiment: constant-velocity extension to
            # T=4,5 from the last two measured seconds. Invented physics
            # for the blind half -- every summary stamps horizon_s=6 and
            # the report splits violations by measured (T<=3) vs
            # extrapolated (T>=4) timesteps. Keep only if signal > noise.
            vel = pred_1s[3] - pred_1s[2]
            extra = np.array([pred_1s[3] + vel, pred_1s[3] + 2 * vel],
                             dtype=np.float32)
            pred_1s = np.vstack([pred_1s, extra])
            horizon_s = 6
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        # Tracked agents from planner detections (P-18): without these,
        # R3/R4/R13 never see real traffic. Same projection as
        # pipeline.run (score-filtered, capped, checker-culled).
        agent_trajs = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            out.get('boxes'), out.get('velocities'), out.get('scores'),
            len(pred_1s))
        ok_p, v_p = checker.check(pred_1s, map_data=map_data,
                                  other_vehicles=agent_trajs or None)
        t_check.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
        n_pred_safe += ok_p
        for v in v_p:
            if v.get('type', 'violation') != 'warning':
                pred_rule_counts[v['rule']] += 1
                if extrapolate and v.get('time', 0) >= 4:
                    pred_rule_counts_extrap[v['rule']] += 1
                else:
                    pred_rule_counts_meas[v['rule']] += 1

        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        try:
            fixed, _ = corrector.correct(pred_1s, map_data=map_data,
                                         other_vehicles=agent_trajs or None)
            t_correct.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
            ok_f, _ = checker.check(fixed, map_data=map_data,
                                    other_vehicles=agent_trajs or None)
        except RuntimeError as e:
            # Uncorrectable within budget (common on extrapolated
            # horizons): record the failure, don't crash the sweep.
            t_correct.append((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0)
            ok_f = False
            print(f"    corrector gave up: {e}")
        n_pred_fixed += ok_f
        if dump_trajs is not None:
            dump_trajs[str(idx)] = {
                'gt': gt.tolist(),
                'pred_1s': pred_1s.tolist(),
                'map_data': map_data,
                'boxes': np.asarray(
                    out.get('boxes', np.zeros((0, 7)))).tolist(),
                'velocities': np.asarray(
                    out.get('velocities', np.zeros((0, 3)))).tolist(),
                'scores': np.asarray(
                    out.get('scores', np.zeros((0,)))).tolist(),
            }
        print(f"  scene {idx}: L2={l2_errors[-1]:.2f}m "
              f"gt_safe={ok_gt} pred_safe={ok_p} fixed={ok_f}")

    summary = {
        'scenes': len(scenes),
        'extrapolated': bool(extrapolate),
        'warm_predictions': bool(warm),
        'corrector_mode': corrector_mode,
        'horizon_s': 6 if extrapolate else 3,
        'horizon_steps_pred': 6 if extrapolate else 4,
        'l2_mean': float(np.mean(l2_errors)),
        'l2_values': [float(x) for x in l2_errors],
        'gt_safe_rate': n_gt_safe / len(scenes),
        'pred_safe_rate': n_pred_safe / len(scenes),
        'pred_fixed_rate': n_pred_fixed / len(scenes),
        'gt_rule_counts': dict(gt_rule_counts),
        'pred_rule_counts': dict(pred_rule_counts),
        'pred_rule_counts_measured_Tle3': dict(pred_rule_counts_meas),
        'pred_rule_counts_extrapolated_Tge4': dict(pred_rule_counts_extrap),
        'ms_planner_mean': float(np.mean(t_plan)),
        'ms_checker_mean': float(np.mean(t_check)),
        'ms_corrector_mean': float(np.mean(t_correct)),
    }
    print("\n=== Calibration baseline ===")
    print(f"  horizon: {summary['horizon_s']} s pred "
          f"({summary['horizon_steps_pred']} steps, T=0 anchored)")
    print(f"  L2 mean: {summary['l2_mean']:.2f} m")
    print(f"  GT safe rate (want ~1.0): {summary['gt_safe_rate']:.2f}")
    print(f"  pred safe rate: {summary['pred_safe_rate']:.2f}")
    print(f"  pred fixed rate: {summary['pred_fixed_rate']:.2f}")
    print("  GT rule hits (false-positive suspects):")
    for rule, cnt in gt_rule_counts.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print("  pred rule hits:")
    for rule, cnt in pred_rule_counts.most_common():
        print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    if extrapolate:
        print("  pred hits at measured T<=3:")
        for rule, cnt in pred_rule_counts_meas.most_common():
            print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
        print("  pred hits at EXTRAPOLATED T>=4 (invented physics):")
        for rule, cnt in pred_rule_counts_extrap.most_common():
            print(f"    {rule}: {cnt}")
    print(f"  timing ms planner/checker/corrector: "
          f"{summary['ms_planner_mean']:.1f} / "
          f"{summary['ms_checker_mean']:.1f} / "
          f"{summary['ms_corrector_mean']:.1f}")
    return summary


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Open-loop calibration baseline")
    ap.add_argument("--data-root", default="C:/data/sets/nuscenes")
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", default=None,
                    help="comma-separated scene indices, default all")
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="JSON summary path")
    ap.add_argument("--extrapolate", action="store_true",
                    help="N-3(b): extend pred to T=4,5 by constant velocity "
                         "(labeled experiment; see summary 'extrapolated')")
    ap.add_argument("--cold", action="store_true",
                    help="single-frame cold predictions (default is the "
                         "N-2 warm path)")
    ap.add_argument("--corrector", default="heuristic",
                    choices=["heuristic", "qp"],
                    help="correction engine: heuristic fixpoint (default) "
                         "or CorrectorQP constrained solve + re-validation")
    ap.add_argument("--dump-trajs", default=None,
                    help="P-5: save per-scene {gt, pred_1s, map_data} JSON "
                         "for GPU-free threshold sweeps")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    scenes = ([int(x) for x in args.scenes.split(",")]
              if args.scenes else None)
    dump = {} if args.dump_trajs else None
    summary = run_calibration(args.data_root, args.device, scenes,
                               extrapolate=args.extrapolate,
                               dump_trajs=dump,
                               warm=not args.cold,
                               corrector_mode=args.corrector)
    if args.out:
        Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
        print(f"wrote {args.out}")
    if args.dump_trajs and dump is not None:
        Path(args.dump_trajs).write_text(json.dumps(dump))
        print(f"wrote {args.dump_trajs} "
              f"({len(dump)} scenes for threshold sweeps)")

==> asp-safety/experiments/run_closed_loop.py <==
"""CLI driver for the CARLA closed-loop harness (P25-B/C/D).

One invocation = one closed-loop run -> one JSON artifact under
experiments/runs/{scenario}_{seed}_{engine}.json with a
run-id timestamp dedupe suffix.

    python experiments/run_closed_loop.py \
        --scenario S1 --seed 1 --engine off \
        [--replan-ticks 40 --max-minutes 6 --max-distance 600 \
         --device cuda]

The frozen pilot matrix (nextstep.md 7.2) is driven by callers doing
5 scenarios x 3 engines x 1 seed; per-scenario triples run
back-to-back for the deterministic sim common across the triple.
"""

import argparse
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from carla_bridge import BridgeConfig, CarlaBridge  # noqa: E402
from carla_context import (  # noqa: E402,F401 (kept so --smoke-context
    build_agent_context)     # uses the full builders path)


def cli_main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--scenario", default="S1")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--engine", default="off",
                    choices=["off", "heuristic", "qp"])
    ap.add_argument("--replan-ticks", type=int, default=40)
    ap.add_argument("--plan-engine", default="neural",
                    choices=["neural", "lane"])
    ap.add_argument("--max-minutes", type=float, default=6.0)
    ap.add_argument("--max-distance", type=float, default=600.0)
    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
    ap.add_argument("--dbg", action="store_true",
                    help="emit per-~2s control diagnostics ([ctrl] lines)")
    ap.add_argument("--view", action="store_true",
                    help="spectator ego-follow CAMERA (visible server)")
    ap.add_argument("--log-dir", default=str(ROOT / "experiments" / "runs"))
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None,
                    help="override output JSON path")
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)

    cfg = BridgeConfig(
        scenario=args.scenario, seed=args.seed, engine=args.engine,
        plan_engine=args.plan_engine, replan_ticks=args.replan_ticks,
        max_minutes=args.max_minutes,
        max_distance_m=args.max_distance, device=args.device,
        dbg_lines=args.dbg, view_ego=args.view)
    bridge = CarlaBridge(cfg)
    t0 = time.time()
    summary = bridge.run()

    out_dir = Path(args.log_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stamp = time.strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out_path = (Path(args.out) if args.out else
                out_dir / f"{cfg.scenario}_{cfg.seed}_{cfg.engine}.json"
                ).with_name(
        (Path(args.out).stem if args.out
         else f"{cfg.scenario}_{cfg.seed}_{cfg.engine}") + f"_{stamp}.json")
    with open(out_path, "w") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=1, default=lambda o: "unserializable")

    wall = time.time() - t0
    print(f"[run_closed] {cfg.scenario} seed={cfg.seed} "
          f"engine={cfg.engine}: status={summary['status']} "
          f"sim_km={summary['sim_km']} replans={summary['replans']} "
          f"collided={summary['collided']} give_up_rate="
          f"{summary['give_up_rate']} wall={wall:.0f}s "
          f"-> {out_path.name}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(cli_main())

==> asp-safety/experiments/run_evaluation.py <==
"""Placeholder batch demo for ASP Safety Layer (P-20: honest scope).

Runs the full pipeline (SparseDrive placeholder -> ASP Checker ->
Corrector) over synthetic scenes and reports safe-rate, violation
breakdown, and average/p95 latency. This exercises orchestration
only: collision-rate and L2 improvement on real data are measured
by run_calibration.py, not here. Placeholder scenes are
hostile-by-design (scene 9 plants an obstacle; the red at (12, 0)
is reachable), so expect violations on every scene -- the point is
the veto->fix loop, not a clean sheet.
"""

import sys
import time
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader
from evaluation import MetricsCalculator


def run_evaluation(num_scenes=10, rules_dir=None, model_path=None):
    """Run full evaluation on placeholder or real data.
    
    Args:
        num_scenes: Number of scenes to evaluate
        rules_dir: Path to ASP rule files
        model_path: Path to SparseDrive weights
    """
    print("=" * 60)
    print("ASP Safety Layer - Evaluation")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    if rules_dir is None:
        rules_dir = str(base_dir / "rules")
    if model_path is None:
        model_path = str(base_dir / "SparseDrive" / "ckpt" / "sparsedrive_stage2.pth")
    
    # Initialize components
    print("\nInitializing pipeline...")
    pipeline = SafetyLayerPipeline(
        planner_path=model_path,
        rules_dir=rules_dir,
        device="cpu"
    )
    loader = NuScenesLoader("nonexistent")
    calculator = MetricsCalculator()
    
    # Evaluate
    print(f"\nEvaluating {num_scenes} scenes...")
    results = pipeline.run_batch(loader, list(range(num_scenes)))
    
    # Collect metrics
    all_violations = []
    all_detection_times = []
    safe_count = 0
    total_count = 0
    
    for result in results:
        if "error" in result:
            print(f"  Scene {result['scene_idx']}: ERROR - {result['error']}")
            continue
        
        total_count += 1
        is_safe = result["is_safe"]
        violations = result["violations"]
        inf_time = result["inference_time_ms"]
        
        if is_safe:
            safe_count += 1
        
        all_violations.extend(violations)
        all_detection_times.append(inf_time)
        
        status = "SAFE" if is_safe else f"VIOLATION({len(violations)})"
        print(f"  Scene {result['scene_idx']}: {status} "
              f"({inf_time:.1f}ms, {len(result.get('scores', []))} detections)")
    
    # Summary
    print("\n" + "=" * 60)
    print("Evaluation Summary")
    print("=" * 60)
    
    if total_count > 0:
        safety_rate = safe_count / total_count * 100
        avg_time = np.mean(all_detection_times) if all_detection_times else 0
        p95_time = np.percentile(all_detection_times, 95) if all_detection_times else 0
        
        violation_types = {}
        for v in all_violations:
            rule = v.get("rule", "unknown")
            violation_types[rule] = violation_types.get(rule, 0) + 1
        
        print(f"  Total scenes evaluated: {total_count}")
        print(f"  Safe trajectories: {safe_count} ({safety_rate:.1f}%)")
        print(f"  Unsafe trajectories: {total_count - safe_count}")
        print(f"  Total violations: {len(all_violations)}")
        if violation_types:
            print(f"  Violation breakdown:")
            for rule, count in sorted(violation_types.items()):
                print(f"    {rule}: {count}")
        print(f"  Average inference time: {avg_time:.2f} ms")
        print(f"  P95 inference time: {p95_time:.2f} ms")
        print(f"  Safety check latency: {calculator.average_inference_time(all_detection_times):.2f} ms")
    else:
        print("  No scenes evaluated successfully.")
    
    print("=" * 60)
    
    return {
        "total": total_count,
        "safe": safe_count,
        "violations": all_violations,
        "times": all_detection_times
    }


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Evaluate ASP Safety Layer")
    parser.add_argument("--num-scenes", type=int, default=10,
                       help="Number of scenes to evaluate")
    parser.add_argument("--rules-dir", type=str, default=None,
                       help="Path to ASP rule files")
    parser.add_argument("--model-path", type=str, default=None,
                       help="Path to SparseDrive weights")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    run_evaluation(
        num_scenes=args.num_scenes,
        rules_dir=args.rules_dir,
        model_path=args.model_path
    )

==> asp-safety/experiments/run_matrix.py <==
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run multi-seed CARLA closed-loop matrix.

Usage: python experiments/run_matrix.py [--seeds 1,2,3] [--scenarios S1,S2,S3,S4,S5] [--engines off,heuristic,qp] [--plan-engine lane]
"""
import sys
import json
import time
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
PY = r"C:\Users\chand\AppData\Local\Microsoft\WindowsApps\python.exe"  # fallback
# Use the actual python from the env
import sys
PY = sys.executable

SCENARIOS = ['S1', 'S2', 'S3', 'S4', 'S5']
ENGINES = ['off', 'heuristic', 'qp']
SEEDS = [1, 2, 3]

def run_one(scenario, seed, engine, plan_engine='lane', max_distance=600, max_minutes=6, log_dir=None):
    """Run a single closed-loop experiment via subprocess."""
    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(ROOT / "run_closed_loop.py"),
        "--scenario", scenario,
        "--seed", str(seed),
        "--engine", engine,
        "--plan-engine", plan_engine,
        "--max-distance", str(max_distance),
        "--max-minutes", str(max_minutes),
    ]
    if log_dir:
        cmd.extend(["--log-dir", str(log_dir)])
    
    print(f"  RUN: {' '.join(cmd)}")
    t0 = time.time()
    result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=max_minutes * 60 + 120)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    
    if result.returncode != 0:
        print(f"  FAILED (exit {result.returncode}): {result.stderr[:200]}")
        return None
    
    # Parse the last line for the output path
    for line in result.stdout.strip().split('\n'):
        if line.startswith('[run_closed]'):
            parts = line.split()
            # Extract the JSON filename
            for part in parts:
                if part.endswith('.json'):
                    return part
    return None

def main():
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--seeds', default='1,2,3')
    parser.add_argument('--scenarios', default='S1,S2,S3,S4,S5')
    parser.add_argument('--engines', default='off,heuristic,qp')
    parser.add_argument('--plan-engine', default='lane')
    parser.add_argument('--max-distance', type=float, default=600)
    parser.add_argument('--max-minutes', type=float, default=6)
    parser.add_argument('--log-dir', default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    seeds = [int(s) for s in args.seeds.split(',')]
    scenarios = args.scenarios.split(',')
    engines = args.engines.split(',')
    
    log_dir = Path(args.log_dir) if args.log_dir else ROOT / "runs"
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    total = len(seeds) * len(scenarios) * len(engines)
    print(f"Running {total} experiments: seeds={seeds}, scenarios={scenarios}, engines={engines}")
    print(f"Log dir: {log_dir}")
    
    results = []
    for i, seed in enumerate(seeds):
        for scenario in scenarios:
            for engine in engines:
                print(f"\n[{len(results)+1}/{total}] {scenario} seed={seed} engine={engine}")
                try:
                    out_file = run_one(scenario, seed, engine, args.plan_engine,
                                      args.max_distance, args.max_minutes, log_dir)
                    if out_file:
                        results.append({
                            'scenario': scenario, 'seed': seed, 'engine': engine,
                            'output': out_file, 'status': 'completed'
                        })
                        print(f"  -> {out_file}")
                    else:
                        results.append({
                            'scenario': scenario, 'seed': seed, 'engine': engine,
                            'status': 'failed'
                        })
                        print(f"  -> FAILED")
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    results.append({
                        'scenario': scenario, 'seed': seed, 'engine': engine,
                        'status': 'timeout'
                    })
                    print(f"  -> TIMEOUT")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append({
                        'scenario': scenario, 'seed': seed, 'engine': engine,
                        'status': f'error: {e}'
                    })
                    print(f"  -> ERROR: {e}")
    
    # Save summary
    summary_file = log_dir / f"matrix_summary_{int(time.time())}.json"
    with open(summary_file, 'w') as f:
        json.dump({
            'seeds': seeds, 'scenarios': scenarios, 'engines': engines,
            'results': results, 'timestamp': time.time()
        }, f, indent=2)
    print(f"\nSummary saved to {summary_file}")
    
    # Print summary table
    print("\nSUMMARY:")
    for r in results:
        print(f"  {r['scenario']} seed={r['seed']} engine={r['engine']}: {r['status']}")

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
==> asp-safety/experiments/run_temporal.py <==
"""Warm-vs-cold temporal harness (N-2).

Per scene: walk 4 history frames + the canonical eval frame through
predict_frame() (banks warm, img_metas + pose-derived ego_status
supplied), and compare L2 against single-frame cold predict() on the
eval frame. GT and map come from the canonical sample, so the only
difference is temporal history.

Usage: python experiments/run_temporal.py [--scenes 0,1] [--n-past 4]
"""

import sys
import time
import argparse
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from neural_planner import SparseDrivePlanner
from data_loader import NuScenesLoader, estimate_ego_status
from evaluation import MetricsCalculator
from run_calibration import interp_gt


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--data-root", default="C:/data/sets/nuscenes")
    ap.add_argument("--scenes", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--n-past", type=int, default=4)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    base = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    loader = NuScenesLoader(args.data_root, version="v1.0-mini")
    if loader.use_placeholder:
        raise RuntimeError(f"no nuScenes data at {args.data_root}")
    scenes = ([int(x) for x in args.scenes.split(",")]
              if args.scenes else list(range(loader.num_scenes)))
    planner = SparseDrivePlanner(
        str(base / "SparseDrive" / "ckpt" / "sparsedrive_stage2.pth"),
        device="cuda")

    cold_l2, warm_l2 = [], []
    print(f"Temporal warm-vs-cold on {len(scenes)} scenes "
          f"(n_past={args.n_past})...")
    for idx in scenes:
        frames = loader.get_scene_frames(idx, n_past=args.n_past)
        gt = loader.get_trajectory(idx)
        last = frames[-1]

        # Cold: single independent inference on the eval frame.
        out_c = planner.predict(
            last['images'].astype(np.float32), last['intrinsics'],
            last['extrinsics'], last['ego2global'], last['timestamp'])
        pred_times = (np.arange(6) + 1) * 0.5
        l2_c = MetricsCalculator.l2_error(
            out_c['trajectory'], interp_gt(gt, pred_times))
        cold_l2.append(l2_c)

        # Warm: walk the sequence without resetting; pose-derived status.
        planner.begin_sequence()
        out_w = None
        prev_g = None
        prev_t = None
        for fr in frames:
            if prev_g is None:
                status = np.zeros(18, dtype=np.float32)
            else:
                dt = fr['timestamp'] - prev_t
                status = estimate_ego_status(prev_g, fr['ego2global'], dt)
            out_w = planner.predict_frame(
                fr['images'].astype(np.float32), fr['intrinsics'],
                fr['extrinsics'], fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp'],
                ego_status=status)
            prev_g, prev_t = fr['ego2global'], fr['timestamp']
        planner.end_sequence()
        l2_w = MetricsCalculator.l2_error(
            out_w['trajectory'], interp_gt(gt, pred_times))
        warm_l2.append(l2_w)
        print(f"  scene {idx}: cold L2={l2_c:.2f}m warm L2={l2_w:.2f}m")

    print("\n=== Temporal result ===")
    print(f"  cold mean L2: {np.mean(cold_l2):.2f} m")
    print(f"  warm mean L2: {np.mean(warm_l2):.2f} m")
    print(f"  delta (cold-warm): {np.mean(cold_l2) - np.mean(warm_l2):+.2f} m")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

==> asp-safety/experiments/sweep_qp_params.py <==
"""QP optimizer hyperparameter sweep (P6 follow-up, GPU-free).

Grid over projected-gradient knobs (lr, steps, lane_pull) on the
frozen trajs.json dump. Each combo runs CorrectorQP with
heuristic_fallback=False (pure optimizer power) over all 10 scenes;
fix rate, L2 delta on fixed scenes, and mean latency pick the knee.
Mirrors the P-5 threshold-sweep precedent: keep defaults unless a
combo shows a measured win with no regression.

Usage (from asp-safety/):
    python experiments/sweep_qp_params.py [--out FILE]
"""

import sys
import time
import json
import argparse
import itertools
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

import numpy as np

from checker import TrajectoryChecker
from corrector import CorrectorQP
from pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline

GRID = {
    'lr': [0.1, 0.2, 0.4],
    'steps': [30, 60],
    'lane_pull': [0.0, 0.05],
}


def _l2(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    n = min(len(a), len(b))
    return float(np.mean(np.linalg.norm(a[:n] - b[:n], axis=1)))


def run_sweep(dump_path: str):
    dump = json.loads(Path(dump_path).read_text())
    ids = sorted(dump.keys(), key=int)
    base_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    checker = TrajectoryChecker(str(base_dir / "rules"))

    scenes = []
    for sid in ids:
        s = dump[sid]
        pred = np.asarray(s['pred_1s'], dtype=np.float32)
        gt = np.asarray(s['gt'], dtype=np.float32)
        boxes = np.asarray(s['boxes'], dtype=np.float32)
        vels = np.asarray(s.get('velocities', np.zeros((0, 3))),
                          dtype=np.float32)
        scores = np.asarray(s.get('scores', np.zeros((0,))),
                            dtype=np.float32)
        agents = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            boxes, vels, scores, len(pred))
        scenes.append((sid, pred, gt, s['map_data'], agents))

    combos = list(itertools.product(
        GRID['lr'], GRID['steps'], GRID['lane_pull']))
    rows = []
    for lr, steps, lane_pull in combos:
        qp = CorrectorQP(
            checker,
            optimizer_params={'lr': lr, 'steps': steps,
                              'lane_pull': lane_pull},
            heuristic_fallback=False)
        fixed = l2d = ms = n_unsafe = 0
        for sid, pred, gt, map_data, agents in scenes:
            kw = dict(map_data=map_data, other_vehicles=agents or None)
            ok_p, _ = checker.check(pred, **kw)
            if ok_p:
                continue
            n_unsafe += 1
            t0 = time.perf_counter()
            try:
                solved, _ = qp.correct(pred, **kw)
                dt = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
                ok_f, _ = checker.check(solved, **kw)
                if ok_f:
                    fixed += 1
                    l2d += _l2(pred, gt[:len(pred)]) - _l2(
                        solved, gt[:len(solved)])
                ms += dt
            except RuntimeError:
                ms += (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
        row = {'lr': lr, 'steps': steps, 'lane_pull': lane_pull,
               'fixed': fixed, 'unsafe': n_unsafe,
               'fix_rate': fixed / n_unsafe if n_unsafe else 1.0,
               'l2_improve_mean': (l2d / fixed) if fixed else 0.0,
               'ms_mean': ms / n_unsafe if n_unsafe else 0.0}
        rows.append(row)
        print(f"  lr={lr} steps={steps} pull={lane_pull}: "
              f"fixed {fixed}/{n_unsafe} "
              f"L2+{row['l2_improve_mean']:.2f}m "
              f"{row['ms_mean']:.0f} ms")
    return {'grid': GRID, 'rows': rows}


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="QP param sweep")
    ap.add_argument("--dump", default=str(
        Path(__file__).parent / "trajs.json"))
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="JSON rows path")
    args = ap.parse_args()
    summary = run_sweep(args.dump)
    if args.out:
        Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
        print(f"wrote {args.out}")

==> asp-safety/experiments/sweep_thresholds.py <==
"""Threshold sweep proper (P-5): finding-rate-vs-value knees.

GPU-free: loads per-scene {gt, pred_1s, map_data} dumped by
run_calibration.py --dump-trajs, rewrites one .lp default at a time,
and counts GT false-positive scenes vs pred hit scenes per value.
Rule files are backed up and restored (try/finally).

Usage:
  python experiments/run_calibration.py --dump-trajs experiments/trajs.json ...
  python experiments/sweep_thresholds.py --dump experiments/trajs.json
"""

import sys
import re
import json
import shutil
import argparse
import itertools
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent / "src"))

from checker import TrajectoryChecker
from pipeline import SafetyLayerPipeline

BASE = Path(__file__).parent.parent
RULES = BASE / "rules"

# Agent-context params (no .lp rewrite; passed to run_all).
AGENT_SWEEP = [
    ("min_score", [0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7]),
    ("max_agents", [5, 10, 20, 40]),
]

# (label, file, pattern-with-one-group, formatter, [values])
SWEEP = [
    ("bubble_5m", "collision_avoidance.lp",
     r"default_collision_dist2\((\d+)\)\.", "default_collision_dist2({}).",
     [90000, 160000, 250000, 360000]),
    ("long_gap", "collision_avoidance.lp",
     r"min_longitudinal_gap\((\d+)\)\.", "min_longitudinal_gap({}).",
     [200, 300, 400]),
    ("lat_clear", "collision_avoidance.lp",
     r"min_lateral_clearance\((\d+)\)\.", "min_lateral_clearance({}).",
     [75, 100, 150]),
    ("default_cap", "speed_regulation.lp",
     r"S2 > (\d+)", "S2 > {}",
     [1562500, 1929321, 2500000]),
    ("wrong_way_min", "lane_discipline.lp",
     r"min_wrong_way_speed2\((\d+)\)\.", "min_wrong_way_speed2({}).",
     [625, 2500, 10000]),
    ("worker_clear2", "emergency.lp",
     r"worker_clearance2\((\d+)\)\.", "worker_clearance2({}).",
     [22500, 90000, 360000]),
]


def set_param(fname, pattern, fmt, value):
    p = RULES / fname
    src = p.read_text()
    new, n = re.subn(pattern, fmt.format(value), src, count=1)
    assert n == 1, f"pattern not found once in {fname}: {pattern}"
    p.write_text(new)


def backup():
    bak = {}
    for _, fname, _, _, _ in SWEEP:
        p = RULES / fname
        if fname not in bak:
            bak[fname] = p.read_text()
    return bak


def restore(bak):
    for fname, src in bak.items():
        (RULES / fname).write_text(src)


def run_all(checker, scenes, min_score=0.3, max_agents=20):
    """Returns (gt_fp_scenes, pred_hit_scenes, gt_counts, pred_counts)."""
    gt_fp = pred_hit = 0
    gt_counts, pred_counts = Counter(), Counter()
    for idx, entry in scenes.items():
        gt = np.array(entry["gt"], dtype=np.float32)
        pred = np.array(entry["pred_1s"], dtype=np.float32)
        m = entry["map_data"]
        boxes = np.array(entry.get("boxes", np.zeros((0, 7))),
                         dtype=np.float32)
        vels = np.array(entry.get("velocities", np.zeros((0, 3))),
                        dtype=np.float32)
        scores = np.array(entry.get("scores", np.zeros((0,))),
                          dtype=np.float32)
        gt_agents = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            boxes, vels, scores, len(gt), max_agents=max_agents,
            min_score=min_score)
        pred_agents = SafetyLayerPipeline._detections_to_trajectories(
            boxes, vels, scores, len(pred), max_agents=max_agents,
            min_score=min_score)
        ok, v = checker.check(gt, map_data=m,
                              other_vehicles=gt_agents or None)
        if not ok:
            gt_fp += 1
            for d in v:
                if d.get("type", "violation") != "warning":
                    gt_counts[d["rule"]] += 1
        ok, v = checker.check(pred, map_data=m,
                              other_vehicles=pred_agents or None)
        if not ok:
            pred_hit += 1
            for d in v:
                if d.get("type", "violation") != "warning":
                    pred_counts[d["rule"]] += 1
    return gt_fp, pred_hit, gt_counts, pred_counts


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dump", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    scenes = json.loads(Path(args.dump).read_text())
    print(f"sweeping {len(scenes)} dumped scenes "
          f"({', '.join(sorted(scenes))})")
    bak = backup()
    rows = []
    try:
        checker = TrajectoryChecker(str(RULES))
        # Baseline (current defaults) first.
        gt_fp, pred_hit, gc, pc = run_all(checker, scenes)
        rows.append(("baseline", "-", gt_fp, pred_hit, dict(gc),
                     dict(pc)))
        print(f"baseline: GT-FP scenes={gt_fp} pred-hit scenes={pred_hit}")
        for label, fname, pattern, fmt, values in SWEEP:
            for val in values:
                set_param(fname, pattern, fmt, val)
                c = TrajectoryChecker(str(RULES))
                gt_fp, pred_hit, gc, pc = run_all(c, scenes)
                rows.append((label, val, gt_fp, pred_hit, dict(gc),
                             dict(pc)))
                print(f"{label}={val}: GT-FP={gt_fp} pred-hit={pred_hit} "
                      f"GT={dict(gc)} pred={dict(pc)}")
        for label, values in AGENT_SWEEP:
            for val in values:
                kw = {"min_score": val} if label == "min_score" \
                    else {"max_agents": val}
                gt_fp, pred_hit, gc, pc = run_all(checker, scenes, **kw)
                rows.append((label, val, gt_fp, pred_hit, dict(gc),
                             dict(pc)))
                print(f"{label}={val}: GT-FP={gt_fp} pred-hit={pred_hit} "
                      f"GT={dict(gc)} pred={dict(pc)}")
    finally:
        restore(bak)

    print("\n=== Sweep knees (pick values where GT-FP drops without "
          "losing pred hits) ===")
    for label, val, gt_fp, pred_hit, gc, pc in rows:
        print(f"  {label}={val}: GT-FP scenes {gt_fp}, "
              f"pred-hit scenes {pred_hit}")
    if args.out:
        Path(args.out).write_text(json.dumps(
            [{"param": l, "value": v, "gt_fp": g, "pred_hit": p,
              "gt_counts": gc, "pred_counts": pc}
             for l, v, g, p, gc, pc in rows], indent=2))
        print(f"wrote {args.out}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
